Turning the TIDE: Cross-Architecture Distillation for Diffusion Large Language Models
Authors: Gongbo Zhang, Wen Wang, Ye Tian, Li Yuan
First: 2026-04-29T17:59:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:59:01+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 3 figures. Code: https://github.com/PKU-YuanGroup/TIDE
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer parallel decoding and bidirectional context, but state-of-the-art dLLMs require billions of parameters for competitive performance. While existing distillation methods for dLLMs reduce inference steps within a single architecture, none address cross-architecture knowledge transfer, in which the teacher and student differ in architecture, attention mechanism, and tokenizer. We present TIDE, the first framework for cross-architecture dLLM distillation, comprising three modular components: (1) TIDAL, which jointly modulates distillation strength across training progress and diffusion timestep to account for the teacher's noise-dependent reliability; (2) CompDemo, which enriches the teacher's context via complementary mask splitting to improve predictions under heavy masking; and (3) Reverse CALM, a cross-tokenizer objective that inverts chunk-level likelihood matching, yielding bounded gradients and dual-end noise filtering. Distilling 8B dense and 16B MoE teachers into a 0.6B student via two heterogeneous pipelines outperforms the baseline by an average of 1.53 points across eight benchmarks, yielding notable gains in code generation, where HumanEval scores reach 48.78 compared to 32.3 for the AR baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扭转趋势：跨架构蒸馏用于扩散大语言模型
扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）具备并行解码和双向上下文能力，但当前最先进的dLLMs需数十亿参数才能达到竞争力。现有dLLM蒸馏方法仅减少单架构内的推理步骤，未解决跨架构知识迁移问题——即教师与学生模型在架构、注意力机制和分词器上均不相同。我们提出TIDE，首个用于跨架构dLLM蒸馏的框架，包含三个模块：（1）TIDAL，联合调节训练过程与扩散时间步的蒸馏强度，以适应教师模型噪声依赖的可靠性；（2）CompDemo，通过互补掩码分割丰富教师上下文，提升重掩码条件下的预测能力；（3）Reverse CALM，一种跨分词器目标，通过反转块级似然匹配，实现有界梯度与双端噪声过滤。将8B稠密与16B MoE教师模型蒸馏至0.6B学生模型，经两条异构管道训练，在八个基准测试中平均提升1.53分，尤其在代码生成任务中表现突出，HumanEval得分达48.78，远超AR基线的32.3。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high parameter demands of state-of-the-art diffusion large language models (dLLMs), this work introduces TIDE, the first cross-architecture distillation framework enabling knowledge transfer between dLLMs with differing architectures, attention mechanisms, and tokenizers. TIDE comprises three components: TIDAL, which dynamically adjusts distillation strength based on training progress and diffusion timestep to match teacher reliability; CompDemo, which enhances context prediction under heavy masking via complementary mask splitting; and Reverse CALM, which inverts chunk-level likelihood matching to stabilize gradients and enable dual-end noise filtering. Experiments show that distilling 8B and 16B teachers into a 0.6B student yields a 1.53-point average improvement across eight benchmarks, with particularly strong gains in code generation (HumanEval score of 48.78 vs. 32.3 for the AR baseline).
为降低扩散大语言模型（dLLM）的参数成本，本文提出TIDE框架，首次实现跨架构dLLM知识蒸馏。该框架包含三个模块：TIDAL根据训练阶段与扩散步长动态调节蒸馏强度，以匹配教师模型的噪声依赖可靠性；CompDemo通过互补掩码分割增强教师在重掩码下的上下文预测能力；Reverse CALM通过反转块级似然匹配实现跨分词器梯度稳定与双端噪声过滤。实验表明，将8B和16B教师模型蒸馏至0.6B学生模型，在八个基准上平均提升1.53分，尤其在代码生成任务中，HumanEval得分达48.78，显著优于AR基线的32.3分。
NTIRE 2026 3D Restoration and Reconstruction in Real-world Adverse Conditions: RealX3D Challenge Results
Authors: Shuhong Liu, Chenyu Bao, Ziteng Cui, Xuangeng Chu, Bin Ren, Lin Gu, Xiang Chen, Mingrui Li, Long Ma, Marcos V. Conde, Radu Timofte, Yun Liu, Ryo Umagami, Tomohiro Hashimoto, Zijian Hu, Yuan Gan, Tianhan Xu, Yusuke Kurose, Tatsuya Harada, Junwei Yuan, Gengjia Chang, Xining Ge, Mache You, Qida Cao, Zeliang Li, Xinyuan Hu, Hongde Gu, Changyue Shi, Jiajun Ding, Zhou Yu, Jun Yu, Seungsang Oh, Fei Wang, Donggun Kim, Zhiliang Wu, Seho Ahn, Xinye Zheng, Kun Li, Yanyan Wei, Weisi Lin, Dizhe Zhang, Yuchao Chen, Meixi Song, Hanqing Wang, Haoran Feng, Lu Qi, Jiaao Shan, Yang Gu, Jiacheng Liu, Shiyu Liu, Kui Jiang, Junjun Jiang, Runyu Zhu, Sixun Dong, Qingxia Ye, Zhiqiang Zhang, Zhihua Xu, Zhiwei Wang, Phan The Son, Zhimiao Shi, Zixuan Guo, Xueming Fu, Lixia Han, Changhe Liu, Zhenyu Zhao, Manabu Tsukada, Zheng Zhang, Zihan Zhai, Tingting Li, Ziyang Zheng, Yuhao Liu, Dingju Wang, Jeongbin You, Younghyuk Kim, Il-Youp Kwak, Mingzhe Lyu, Junbo Yang, Wenhan Yang, Hongsen Zhang, Jinqiang Cui, Hong Zhang, Haojie Guo, Hantang Li, Qiang Zhu, Bowen He, Xiandong Meng, Debin Zhao, Xiaopeng Fan, Wei Zhou, Linzhe Jiang, Linfeng Li, Louzhe Xu, Qi Xu, Hang Song, Chenkun Guo, Weizhi Nie, Yufei Li, Xingan Zhan, Zhanqi Shi, Dufeng Zhang, Boyuan Tian, Jingshuo Zeng, Gang He, Yubao Fu, Weijie Wang, Cunchuan Huang
First: 2026-04-05T14:38:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:57:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a comprehensive review of the NTIRE 2026 3D Restoration and Reconstruction (3DRR) Challenge, detailing the proposed methods and results. The challenge seeks to identify robust reconstruction pipelines that are robust under real-world adverse conditions, specifically extreme low-light and smoke-degraded environments, as captured by our RealX3D benchmark. A total of 279 participants registered for the competition, of whom 33 teams submitted valid results. We thoroughly evaluate the submitted approaches against state-of-the-art baselines, revealing significant progress in 3D reconstruction under adverse conditions. Our analysis highlights shared design principles among top-performing methods and provides insights into effective strategies for handling 3D scene degradation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NTIRE 2026 实际恶劣条件下三维重建与修复：RealX3D挑战赛结果
本文全面综述了NTIRE 2026三维重建与修复（3DRR）挑战赛，详细介绍了所提方法及结果。该挑战旨在识别在真实世界恶劣条件下（特别是极端低光照和烟雾退化环境）稳健的重建流程，这些环境由我们的RealX3D基准数据集捕捉。共有279名参赛者注册，其中33支队伍提交了有效结果。我们对所提交方法与当前最先进基线进行彻底评估，揭示了在恶劣条件下三维重建方面取得的显著进展。我们的分析突出了表现优异方法的共同设计原则，并提供了处理三维场景退化问题的有效策略见解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable 3D reconstruction in real-world adverse conditions such as extreme low-light and smoke degradation, this paper reviews the NTIRE 2026 3D Restoration and Reconstruction Challenge, which evaluated 33 submitted methods against state-of-the-art baselines using the RealX3D benchmark. The study identifies key design patterns among top-performing approaches and demonstrates substantial progress in 3D reconstruction under challenging visual conditions, with results indicating improved robustness and fidelity across diverse degradation scenarios.
为应对极端低光和烟雾等真实世界恶劣条件下3D重建的挑战，本文综述了NTIRE 2026 3D修复与重建竞赛，评估了33支参赛队伍在RealX3D基准上的方法，与现有最先进基线对比，发现顶级方法共享若干设计原则，并在处理退化3D场景方面取得显著进步，重建鲁棒性和精度较以往方法明显提升。
Three-Step Nav: A Hierarchical Global-Local Planner for Zero-Shot Vision-and-Language Navigation
Authors: Wanrong Zheng, Yunhao Ge, Laurent Itti
Venue: Proceedings of the 29th International Conference on Artificial Intelligence and Statistics (AISTATS), 2026
First: 2026-04-29T17:55:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:55:05+00:00
Comments: Accepted to AISTATS 2026. Code: https://github.com/ZoeyZheng0/3-step-Nav
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Breakthrough progress in vision-based navigation through unknown environments has been achieved by using multimodal large language models (MLLMs). These models can plan a sequence of motions by evaluating the current view at each time step against the task and goal given to the agent. However, current zero-shot Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) agents powered by MLLMs still tend to drift off course, halt prematurely, and achieve low overall success rates. We propose Three-Step Nav to counteract these failures with a three-view protocol: First, "look forward" to extract global landmarks and sketch a coarse plan. Then, "look now" to align the current visual observation with the next sub-goal for fine-grained guidance. Finally, "look backward" audits the entire trajectory to correct accumulated drift before stopping. Requiring no gradient updates or task-specific fine-tuning, our planner drops into existing VLN pipelines with minimal overhead. Three-Step Nav achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE dataset. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZoeyZheng0/3-step-Nav.
中文标题/摘要
标题：三步导航：一种用于零样本视觉-语言导航的分层全局-局部规划器
通过多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在未知环境中实现基于视觉的导航取得了突破性进展。这些模型可通过在每个时间步评估当前视图与赋予代理的任务和目标，规划一系列运动。然而，当前基于MLLMs的零样本视觉-语言导航（VLN）代理仍倾向于偏离路线、提前停止，并且整体成功率较低。我们提出“三步导航”方法，通过三阶段视觉协议应对这些失败：首先，“向前看”以提取全局地标并粗略规划；其次，“现在看”将当前视觉观测与下一个子目标对齐，提供精细引导；最后，“向后看”审计整个轨迹，纠正累积偏差后再停止。该规划器无需梯度更新或任务特定微调，可轻松嵌入现有VLN流程，开销极小。三步导航在R2R-CE和RxR-CE数据集上实现了零样本任务的最先进性能。代码见https://github.com/ZoeyZheng0/3-step-Nav。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the tendency of current multimodal large language model-based VLN agents to drift, halt prematurely, and achieve low success rates in zero-shot navigation, the authors propose Three-Step Nav, a hierarchical global-local planner that operates without gradient updates or fine-tuning. The method consists of three sequential visual steps: first, "look forward" to extract global landmarks and form a coarse plan; second, "look now" to align the current view with the next sub-goal for fine-grained control; and third, "look backward" to audit the trajectory and correct accumulated drift before termination. Experimental results show that Three-Step Nav achieves state-of-the-art zero-shot performance on both R2R-CE and RxR-CE datasets, demonstrating robustness and efficiency when integrated into existing VLN pipelines.
为解决当前基于多模态大语言模型的零样本视觉-语言导航（VLN）代理常出现偏离路线、过早停止及成功率低的问题，作者提出Three-Step Nav，一种无需微调的分层全局-局部规划器。该方法包含三个视觉步骤：首先“向前看”提取全局地标并制定粗略路径；其次“现在看”将当前视觉输入与下一子目标对齐以提供精细引导；最后“向后看”审计整个轨迹并纠正累积偏差后再停止。在R2R-CE和RxR-CE数据集上，该规划器实现零样本场景下的最先进性能，且集成开销极小。
ProcFunc: Function-Oriented Abstractions for Procedural 3D Generation in Python
Authors: Alexander Raistrick, Karhan Kayan, Jack Nugent, David Yan, Lingjie Mei, Meenal Parakh, Hongyu Wen, Dylan Li, Yiming Zuo, Erich Liang, Jia Deng
First: 2026-04-29T17:52:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:52:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce ProcFunc, a library for Blender-based procedural 3D generation in Python. ProcFunc provides a library of easy-to-use Python functions, which streamline creating, combining, analyzing, and executing procedural generation code. ProcFunc makes it easy to create large-scale diverse training data, by combinatorial compositions of semantic components. VLMs can use ProcFunc to edit procedural material and geometry code and can create new procedural code with significantly fewer coding errors. Finally, as an example use case, we use ProcFunc to develop a new procedural generator of indoor rooms, which includes a collection of new compositional procedural materials. We demonstrate the detail, runtime efficiency, and diversity of this room generator, as well as its use for 3D synthetic data generation. Please visit https://github.com/princeton-vl/procfunc for source code.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ProcFunc：面向Python的程序化3D生成函数导向抽象
我们介绍了ProcFunc，一个基于Blender的Python程序化3D生成库。ProcFunc提供一组易用的Python函数，简化了创建、组合、分析和执行程序化生成代码的过程。ProcFunc通过语义组件的组合式构成，便于生成大规模多样化的训练数据。视觉语言模型（VLMs）可使用ProcFunc编辑程序化材质与几何代码，并能以显著更少的编码错误生成新的程序化代码。作为示例应用场景，我们使用ProcFunc开发了一种新的室内房间程序化生成器，包含一组新的组合式程序化材质。我们展示了该房间生成器的细节、运行时效率与多样性，以及其在3D合成数据生成中的应用。请访问 https://github.com/princeton-vl/procfunc 获取源代码。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient and composable procedural 3D generation in Python, ProcFunc introduces a library of high-level functions that simplify the creation, combination, analysis, and execution of procedural 3D content in Blender. The method leverages semantic component composition to enable rapid generation of diverse training data and supports VLMs in editing and generating procedural code with fewer errors. Experimental results demonstrate the library’s ability to produce detailed, efficient, and diverse indoor room models, validating its utility for 3D synthetic data generation.
为提升3D生成的效率与可扩展性，ProcFunc提出一套基于Python的函数化抽象库，将复杂3D场景的构建分解为可组合的语义组件。该方法通过Blender的程序化管线，提供创建、分析与执行3D生成代码的高层函数，显著降低编码错误率并支持大规模数据合成。实验表明，该库能高效生成细节丰富、多样化的室内场景，并支持视觉语言模型对程序化代码的编辑与生成，验证其在合成3D数据管道中的实用性。
Hyper Input Convex Neural Networks for Shape Constrained Learning and Optimal Transport
Authors: Shayan Hundrieser, Insung Kong, Johannes Schmidt-Hieber
First: 2026-04-29T17:52:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:52:05+00:00
Comments: 65 pages, 13 figures, the first two authors contributed equally
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Hyper Input Convex Neural Networks (HyCNNs), a novel neural network architecture designed for learning convex functions. HyCNNs combine the principles of Maxout networks with input convex neural networks (ICNNs) to create a neural network that is always convex in the input, theoretically capable of leveraging depth, and performs reliable when trained at scale compared to ICNNs. Concretely, we prove that HyCNNs require exponentially fewer parameters than ICNNs to approximate quadratic functions up to a given precision. Throughout a series of synthetic experiments, we demonstrate that HyCNNs outperform existing ICNNs and MLPs in terms of predictive performance for convex regression and interpolation tasks. We further apply HyCNNs to learn high-dimensional optimal transport maps for synthetic examples and for single-cell RNA sequencing data, where they oftentimes outperform ICNN-based neural optimal transport methods and other baselines across a wide range of settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于形状约束学习与最优传输的超输入凸神经网络
我们提出了超输入凸神经网络（HyCNNs），这是一种专为学习凸函数设计的新型神经网络架构。HyCNNs结合了Maxout网络与输入凸神经网络（ICNNs）的原则，构建出一种在输入上始终保持凸性的神经网络，理论上可利用深度结构，并在大规模训练时比ICNNs表现更可靠。具体而言，我们证明HyCNNs在逼近给定精度的二次函数时所需参数量比ICNNs呈指数级减少。通过一系列合成实验，我们展示了HyCNNs在凸回归与插值任务中的预测性能优于现有ICNNs和MLPs。我们进一步将HyCNNs应用于合成数据和单细胞RNA测序数据的高维最优传输映射学习，结果表明其在多种设置下通常优于基于ICNN的神经最优传输方法及其他基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for neural networks that can reliably learn convex functions while maintaining scalability, the authors propose Hyper Input Convex Neural Networks (HyCNNs), which integrate Maxout structures with Input Convex Neural Networks (ICNNs) to ensure convexity in input space. Theoretical analysis shows HyCNNs require exponentially fewer parameters than ICNNs to approximate quadratic functions to a given precision. Empirical results across synthetic convex regression and interpolation tasks demonstrate superior predictive performance over ICNNs and MLPs, and in high-dimensional optimal transport applications—both synthetic and real-world single-cell RNA sequencing data—HyCNNs consistently outperform ICNN-based methods and other baselines.
为解决神经网络在学习凸函数时效率低、难以扩展的问题，作者提出了一种名为HyCNNs的新架构，结合Maxout结构与输入凸神经网络（ICNNs），确保输入空间的凸性并支持更深的网络结构。理论分析表明，HyCNNs在逼近二次函数至指定精度时所需参数量比ICNNs少指数级。在合成数据的凸回归与插值任务中，HyCNNs的预测性能优于ICNNs和MLP。在高维最优传输映射学习任务中，无论是合成数据还是单细胞RNA测序数据，HyCNNs均优于基于ICNN的方法及其他基线模型。
Select to Think: Unlocking SLM Potential with Local Sufficiency
Authors: Wenxuan Ye, Yangyang Zhang, Xueli An, Georg Carle, Yunpu Ma
First: 2026-04-29T17:51:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:51:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Small language models (SLMs) offer computational efficiency for scalable deployment, yet they often fall short of the reasoning power exhibited by their larger counterparts (LLMs). To mitigate this gap, current approaches invoke an LLM to generate tokens at points of reasoning divergence, but these external calls introduce substantial latency and costs. Alternatively, standard distillation is often hindered by the capacity limitation, as SLMs struggle to accurately mimic the LLM's complex generative distribution. We address this dilemma by identifying local sufficiency: at divergence points, the LLM's preferred token consistently resides within the SLM's top-K next-token predictions, even when failing to emerge as the SLM top-1 choice. We therefore propose SELECT TO THINK (S2T), which reframes the LLM's role from open-ended generation to selection among the SLM's proposals, simplifying the supervision signal to discrete candidate rankings. Leveraging this, we introduce S2T-LOCAL, which distills the selection logic into the SLM, empowering it to perform autonomous re-ranking without inference-time LLM dependency. Empirically, we demonstrate that a 1.5B SLM's top-8 candidates capture the 32B LLM's choice with 95% hit rate. Translating this potential into performance, S2T-LOCAL improves greedy decoding by 24.1% on average across benchmarks, effectively matching the efficacy of 8-path self-consistency while operating with single-trajectory efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：选择即思考：通过局部充分性解锁小语言模型潜力
小语言模型（SLM）在可扩展部署中具有计算效率，但其推理能力常不及大型语言模型（LLM）。当前方法通过调用LLM在推理分歧点生成token，但外部调用引入显著延迟和成本。标准蒸馏则受限于容量瓶颈，因SLM难以准确模仿LLM复杂的生成分布。我们通过识别局部充分性解决此困境：在分歧点，LLM偏好的token始终位于SLM的Top-K预测中，即使未成为Top-1。因此提出SELECT TO THINK（S2T），将LLM角色从开放式生成转为在SLM提案中进行选择，简化监督信号为离散候选排序。基于此，我们引入S2T-LOCAL，将选择逻辑蒸馏至SLM，使其无需推理时LLM依赖即可自主重排序。实验证明，1.5B SLM的Top-8候选可覆盖32B LLM选择的95%。在性能上，S2T-LOCAL在多个基准测试中平均提升贪心解码24.1%，有效匹配8路径自一致性效果，同时保持单轨迹效率。
Summary / 总结
To bridge the reasoning gap between small language models (SLMs) and larger LLMs without relying on costly external LLM calls, the paper proposes SELECT TO THINK (S2T), which leverages the observation that LLMs' preferred tokens often appear within SLMs' top-K predictions. S2T redefines the LLM's role as a selector among SLM-generated candidates, simplifying supervision to discrete rankings. The method S2T-LOCAL distills this selection logic into the SLM, enabling it to autonomously re-rank outputs without LLM inference at runtime. Experiments show that a 1.5B SLM’s top-8 candidates match the 32B LLM’s choice 95% of the time, and S2T-LOCAL improves greedy decoding by 24.1% across benchmarks, rivaling 8-path self-consistency with single-trajectory efficiency.
为弥合小语言模型（SLM）与大语言模型（LLM）在推理能力上的差距，同时避免外部调用带来的延迟与成本，本文提出SELECT TO THINK（S2T）方法，利用LLM偏好词常出现在SLM前K候选中的现象，将LLM角色从生成者转变为候选词选择者，简化监督信号为离散排序。S2T-LOCAL进一步将选择逻辑蒸馏进SLM，使其无需运行时LLM调用即可自主重排序。实验证明，1.5B SLM的前8个候选词中95%能匹配32B LLM的选择，且S2T-LOCAL在多个基准上平均提升贪心解码24.1%，效果媲美8路径自一致性，但仅需单轨迹效率。
Artistic Practice Opportunities in CST Evaluations: A Longitudinal Group Deployment of ArtKrit
Authors: Catherine Liu, Tao Long, Asya Vaisberg, Chau Vu, Jiaju Ma, Jingyi Li
First: 2026-04-29T17:49:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:49:03+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 8 figures. Accepted to DIS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Creativity support tools (CSTs) aim to elevate the quality of artists' creative processes and artifacts. Yet most current CST evaluations overlook temporal and social aspects of tool use. To address this gap, we present a longitudinal, group-based CST evaluation through a three-week deployment of ArtKrit, a computational drawing tool that supports disciplined drawing. Nine digital artists, organized into three communities of practice, completed weekly "master studies" alongside a researcher-artist. Our results show users' evolving relationships with ArtKrit over time - from early experimentation to selective incorporation or misuse - alongside changes in their ways of artistic seeing. These changes unfolded within artist support networks that fostered confidence and creative safety, and validated individual expression. Overall, our findings suggest that CST evaluations can - and should - be designed as opportunities for meaningful artistic engagement rather than purely extractive measurement exercises. We contribute this longitudinal, group-based approach as one CST evaluation method.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CST评估中的艺术实践机会：ArtKrit的纵向群体部署
创意支持工具（CST）旨在提升艺术家创作过程与作品的质量。然而，当前大多数CST评估忽视了工具使用的时序和社会维度。为弥补这一不足，我们通过为期三周的ArtKrit（支持规范绘画的计算绘图工具）部署，开展了一项纵向、群体化的CST评估。九位数字艺术家被组织成三个实践社群，与研究型艺术家共同完成每周的“大师研究”。结果表明，用户与ArtKrit的关系随时间演变——从早期实验到选择性融入或误用，同时其艺术感知方式也发生改变。这些变化在艺术家支持网络中展开，该网络增强了信心与创作安全感，并验证了个体表达。总体而言，我们的研究发现表明，CST评估应设计为有意义的艺术参与机会，而非单纯的提取式测量。我们贡献了这一纵向、群体化的方法作为CST评估的一种方式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better understand how creativity support tools (CSTs) influence artistic practice over time and within social contexts, this study deployed ArtKrit—a computational drawing tool—over three weeks with nine digital artists organized into three communities of practice. Participants engaged in weekly "master studies" alongside a researcher-artist, allowing researchers to observe evolving user relationships with the tool, from initial experimentation to selective adoption or misuse, and shifts in their artistic perception. Findings revealed that peer support networks within these communities enhanced confidence and creative safety, reinforcing individual expression. The study concludes that CST evaluations should prioritize meaningful artistic engagement over extractive measurement, proposing this longitudinal, group-based approach as a viable evaluation method.

World2VLM: Distilling World Model Imagination into VLMs for Dynamic Spatial Reasoning
Authors: Wanyue Zhang, Wenxiang Wu, Wang Xu, Jiaxin Luo, Helu Zhi, Yibin Huang, Shuo Ren, Zitao Liu, Jiajun Zhang
First: 2026-04-29T17:48:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:48:01+00:00
Comments: The code is available at https://github.com/WanyueZhang-ai/World2VLM. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/WanyueZhang/World2VLM
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown strong performance on static visual understanding, yet they still struggle with dynamic spatial reasoning that requires imagining how scenes evolve under egocentric motion. Recent efforts address this limitation either by scaling spatial supervision with synthetic data or by coupling VLMs with world models at inference time. However, the former often lacks explicit modeling of motion-conditioned state transitions, while the latter incurs substantial computational overhead. In this work, we propose World2VLM, a training framework that distills spatial imagination from a generative world model into a vision-language model. Given an initial observation and a parameterized camera trajectory, we use a view-consistent world model to synthesize geometrically aligned future views and derive structured supervision for both forward (action-to-outcome) and inverse (outcome-to-action) spatial reasoning. We post-train the VLM with a two-stage recipe on a compact dataset generated by this pipeline and evaluate it on multiple spatial reasoning benchmarks. World2VLM delivers consistent improvements over the base model across diverse benchmarks, including SAT-Real, SAT-Synthesized, VSI-Bench, and MindCube. It also outperforms the test-time world-model-coupled methods while eliminating the need for expensive inference-time generation. Our results suggest that world models can serve not only as inference-time tools, but also as effective training-time teachers, enabling VLMs to internalize spatial imagination in a scalable and efficient manner.
中文标题/摘要
标题：World2VLM：将世界模型的想象能力蒸馏至视觉语言模型，以实现动态空间推理
视觉语言模型（VLMs）在静态视觉理解方面表现强劲，但在需要想象场景如何随第一人称运动演变的动态空间推理任务上仍存在困难。近期研究通过扩大合成数据的空间监督或在推理时将VLM与世界模型耦合来解决此问题。然而，前者常缺乏对运动条件状态转换的显式建模，后者则带来显著的计算开销。本文提出World2VLM，一种将生成式世界模型的空间想象能力蒸馏至视觉语言模型的训练框架。给定初始观测与参数化相机轨迹，我们使用视图一致的世界模型合成几何对齐的未来视图，并为正向（动作到结果）和逆向（结果到动作）空间推理生成结构化监督。我们在该流水线生成的紧凑数据集上对VLM进行两阶段后训练，并在多个空间推理基准上评估。World2VLM在SAT-Real、SAT-Synthesized、VSI-Bench和MindCube等多个基准上均显著优于基线模型，同时超越了推理时耦合世界模型的方法，且无需昂贵的推理时生成。我们的结果表明，世界模型不仅可作为推理时工具，也可作为训练时教师，使VLM以可扩展且高效的方式内化空间想象能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of vision-language models (VLMs) in dynamic spatial reasoning under egocentric motion, this work introduces World2VLM, a training framework that distills spatial imagination from a generative world model into VLMs. Given an initial observation and camera trajectory, the method synthesizes future views using a view-consistent world model to generate structured supervision for both forward and inverse spatial reasoning. The VLM is then post-trained on a compact dataset generated by this pipeline, achieving consistent performance gains across benchmarks like SAT-Real, SAT-Synthesized, VSI-Bench, and MindCube, while outperforming test-time world-model-coupled methods without requiring expensive inference-time generation.
为提升视觉语言模型对第一人称运动下场景动态变化的推理能力，作者提出World2VLM框架，通过从生成式世界模型中蒸馏空间想象力以训练视觉语言模型。给定初始观测与参数化相机轨迹，该方法利用视图一致的世界模型合成几何对齐的未来视图，生成用于正向与逆向空间推理的结构化监督信号。模型随后在该流水线生成的紧凑数据集上进行后训练，在SAT-Real、SAT-Synthesized、VSI-Bench和MindCube等多个基准上均取得稳定提升。更重要的是，该方法在性能上优于测试时耦合世界模型的方法，同时避免了其高昂的推理开销，表明世界模型可作为训练时教师，以高效可扩展的方式使视觉语言模型内化空间想象力。
Learning Over-Relaxation Policies for ADMM with Convergence Guarantees
Authors: Junan Lin, Paul J. Goulart, Luca Furieri
First: 2026-04-29T17:45:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:45:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) is a widely used method for structured convex optimization, and its practical performance depends strongly on the choice of penalty and relaxation parameters. Motivated by settings such as Model Predictive Control (MPC), where one repeatedly solves related optimization problems with fixed structure and changing parameter values, we propose learning online updates of the relaxation parameter to improve performance on problem classes of interest. This choice is computationally attractive in OSQP-like architectures, since adapting relaxation does not trigger the matrix refactorizations associated with penalty updates. We establish convergence guarantees for ADMM with time-varying penalty and relaxation parameters under mild assumptions, and show on benchmark quadratic programs that the resulting learned policies improve both iteration count and wall-clock time over baseline OSQP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：带收敛保证的ADMM过松弛策略学习
交替方向乘子法（ADMM）是结构化凸优化中广泛使用的方法，其实际性能高度依赖惩罚和松弛参数的选择。受模型预测控制（MPC）等场景启发，其中需反复求解结构固定、参数变化的优化问题，我们提出在线学习松弛参数更新策略，以提升对特定问题类别的性能。该选择在类似OSQP的架构中计算上具有吸引力，因为调整松弛参数不会触发与惩罚参数更新相关的矩阵重分解。我们在温和假设下建立了ADMM在时变惩罚和松弛参数下的收敛保证，并在基准二次规划问题上表明，所得到的学习策略在迭代次数和实际运行时间上均优于基线OSQP。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to optimize ADMM performance in settings like Model Predictive Control, where optimization problems recur with fixed structure and changing parameters, this work proposes learning online updates for the relaxation parameter to enhance convergence. The method leverages the computational efficiency of OSQP-like architectures by avoiding costly matrix refactorizations associated with penalty updates. Under mild assumptions, convergence is theoretically guaranteed for ADMM with time-varying parameters, and experiments on benchmark quadratic programs demonstrate that the learned policies reduce both iteration count and wall-clock time compared to baseline OSQP.
为提升ADMM在如模型预测控制等场景中的性能，该研究提出在线学习松弛参数更新策略，以在不触发代价高昂的矩阵重分解的前提下优化收敛性。方法基于策略学习框架，动态调整松弛参数，并在温和假设下保证收敛性。实验表明，所学策略在基准二次规划问题上显著减少迭代次数和实际运行时间，优于基线OSQP方法。
A Note on How to Remove the $\ln\ln T$ Term from the Squint Bound
Authors: Francesco Orabona
First: 2026-04-29T17:40:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:40:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In Orabona and Pál [2016], we introduced the shifted KT potentials, to remove the $\ln \ln T$ factor in the parameter-free learning with expert bound. In this short technical note, I show that this is equivalent to changing the prior in the Krichevsky--Trofimov algorithm. Then, I show how to use the same idea to remove the $\ln \ln T$ factor in the data-independent bound for the Squint algorithm.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于如何从Squint界中移除$\ln\ln T$项的说明
在Orabona和Pál [2016]中，我们引入了平移的KT势，以消除参数无关学习与专家界中的$\ln \ln T$因子。在本篇简短的技术说明中，我展示这等价于改变Krichevsky-Trofimov算法中的先验。然后，我展示如何使用相同的思想，消除Squint算法中数据无关界中的$\ln \ln T$因子。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the desire to eliminate the $\ln \ln T$ term from parameter-free learning bounds, this note demonstrates that the shifted KT potentials introduced in Orabona and Pál [2016] are equivalent to modifying the prior in the Krichevsky--Trofimov algorithm. Building on this equivalence, the author extends the technique to remove the same logarithmic term from the data-independent bound of the Squint algorithm, thereby improving the asymptotic tightness of the bound without requiring parameter tuning.
为消除参数自由学习界中的 $\ln \ln T$ 项，本文指出Orabona和Pál [2016]提出的移位KT势等价于修改Krichevsky-Trofimov算法中的先验。在此基础上，作者将该思想推广至Squint算法的数据无关界，成功消除了该对数项，从而在不增加参数的前提下提升了界在渐近意义上的紧致性。
ClassEval-Pro: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Class-Level Code Generation
Authors: Yeheng Chen, Chaoxiang Xie, Yuling Shi, Wenhao Zeng, Yongpan Wang, Hongyu Zhang, Xiaodong Gu
First: 2026-04-29T17:38:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:38:37+00:00
Comments: Accepted to AIware 2026. Code and data available at https://github.com/ian-Kappa/ClassEval-Pro
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
LLMs have achieved strong results on both function-level code synthesis and repository-level code modification, yet a capability that falls between these two extremes -- compositional code creation, i.e., building a complete, internally structured class from a specification -- remains underserved. Current evaluations are either confined to isolated functions or rely on manually curated class-level tasks that are expensive to scale and increasingly susceptible to data contamination. We introduce ClassEval-Pro, a benchmark of 300 class-level tasks spanning 11 domains, constructed through an automated three-stage pipeline that combines complexity enhancement, cross-domain class composition, and integration of real-world GitHub code contributed after January 2025. Every task is validated by an LLM Judge Ensemble and must pass test suites with over 90% line coverage. We evaluate five frontier LLMs under five generation strategies. The best model achieves only 45.6% class-level Pass@1, with a 17.7-point gap between the strongest and weakest models, confirming the benchmark's discriminative power. Strategy choice strongly interacts with model capability: structured approaches such as bottom-up improve weaker models by up to 9.4 percentage points, while compositional generation collapses to as low as 1.3%. Error analysis over 500 manually annotated failures reveals that logic errors (56.2%) and dependency errors (38.0%) dominate, identifying cross-method coordination as the core bottleneck.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ClassEval-Pro：面向类级代码生成的跨领域基准
大语言模型在函数级代码合成和仓库级代码修改方面已取得显著成果，但介于两者之间的能力——组合式代码生成（即根据规范构建完整且内部结构化的类）——仍被忽视。当前评估要么局限于孤立函数，要么依赖人工编纂的类级任务，难以规模化且易受数据污染。我们提出ClassEval-Pro，包含11个领域共300个类级任务，通过自动化三阶段流水线构建：复杂度增强、跨领域类组合及整合2025年1月后贡献的GitHub真实代码。每个任务由LLM裁判集成验证，必须通过覆盖率超90%的测试套件。我们在五种生成策略下评估五种前沿大语言模型。最优模型仅达成45.6%的类级Pass@1，最强与最弱模型差距达17.7分，验证了该基准的区分能力。策略选择与模型能力强相关：结构化方法如自下而上可使弱模型提升最高9.4个百分点，而组合式生成则低至1.3%。对500个手动标注失败案例的分析表明，逻辑错误（56.2%）和依赖错误（38.0%）占主导，揭示跨方法协调是核心瓶颈。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap in evaluating LLMs' ability to generate complete, structured classes — a task between function-level synthesis and repository-level modification — the authors introduce ClassEval-Pro, a benchmark of 300 class-level tasks across 11 domains. Built via an automated pipeline that enhances complexity, composes cross-domain classes, and integrates real-world GitHub code post-January 2025, each task is validated by an LLM Judge Ensemble requiring over 90% test coverage. Evaluating five frontier LLMs under five generation strategies, results show the best model achieves only 45.6% Pass@1, with a 17.7-point gap between top and bottom models, highlighting the benchmark’s discriminative power. Structured strategies like bottom-up improve weaker models by up to 9.4 percentage points, while compositional methods drop to 1.3%. Error analysis of 500 failures reveals logic (56.2%) and dependency (38.0%) errors as dominant, pointing to cross-method coordination as the core bottleneck.
为填补大语言模型在生成完整结构化类能力上的评估空白，作者构建了ClassEval-Pro基准，包含11个领域共300个类级任务。该基准通过自动化三阶段流程生成：增强复杂度、跨域类组合、整合2025年1月后GitHub真实代码，每个任务经LLM裁判组验证并需覆盖90%以上测试行。在五种生成策略下评估五款前沿模型，表现最佳者仅达45.6% Pass@1，最强与最弱模型差距达17.7分，凸显基准的区分能力。结构化策略如自底向上可使弱模型提升9.4个百分点，而组合式策略则降至1.3%。500个手动标注失败案例分析显示，逻辑错误（56.2%）与依赖错误（38.0%）占主导，揭示跨方法协同是核心瓶颈。
On the Learning Curves of Revenue Maximization
Authors: Steve Hanneke, Alkis Kalavasis, Shay Moran, Grigoris Velegkas
First: 2026-04-29T17:38:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:38:25+00:00
Comments: To appear in the 58th ACM Symposium on Theory of Computing (STOC 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning curves are a fundamental primitive in supervised learning, describing how an algorithm's performance improves with more data and providing a quantitative measure of its generalization ability. Formally, a learning curve plots the decay of an algorithm's error for a fixed underlying distribution as a function of the number of training samples. Prior work on revenue-maximizing learning algorithms, starting with the seminal work of Cole and Roughgarden [STOC, 2014], adopts a distribution-free perspective, which parallels the PAC learning framework in learning theory. This approach evaluates performance against the hardest possible sequence of valuation distributions, one for each sample size, effectively defining the upper envelope of learning curves over all possible distributions, thus leading to error bounds that do not capture the shape of the learning curves. In this work we initiate the study of learning curves for revenue maximization and provide a near-complete characterization of their rate of decay in the basic setting of a single item and a single buyer. In the absence of any restriction on the valuation distribution, we show that there exists a Bayes-consistent algorithm, meaning that its learning curve converges to zero for any arbitrary valuation distribution as the number of samples $n \to \infty$. However, this convergence must be arbitrarily slow, even if the optimal revenue is finite. In contrast, if the optimal revenue is achieved by a finite price, then the optimal rate of decay is roughly $1/\sqrt{n}$. Finally, for distributions supported on discrete sets of values, we show that learning curves decay almost exponentially fast, a rate unattainable under the PAC framework.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于收益最大化的学习曲线研究
学习曲线是监督学习中的基本概念，描述算法性能随数据量增加而提升的过程，并提供其泛化能力的量化度量。形式上，学习曲线绘制的是算法在固定底层分布下，随着训练样本数量增加而误差衰减的情况。此前关于收益最大化学习算法的研究（始于Cole和Roughgarden[STOC, 2014]的奠基性工作）采用无分布假设视角，与学习理论中的PAC学习框架相平行，其评估标准为最坏可能的估值分布序列，从而定义了所有可能分布下学习曲线的上包络，导致所得误差界无法反映学习曲线的形状。 本研究首次探讨收益最大化的学习曲线，并在单一商品和单一买家的基本设定下，对其衰减速率提供了近乎完整的刻画。在无任何估值分布限制的情况下，我们证明存在一个贝叶斯一致算法，即其学习曲线在样本数n趋于无穷时对任意估值分布收敛至零。然而，这种收敛速度必须任意缓慢，即使最优收益有限。相反，若最优收益由有限价格实现，则最优衰减速率约为1/√n。最后，对于支持在离散值集上的分布，我们证明学习曲线几乎呈指数级衰减，这一速率在PAC框架下无法实现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how revenue-maximizing algorithms improve with more data, this work studies learning curves in revenue maximization, extending the PAC learning framework to distribution-free settings. It introduces a Bayes-consistent algorithm that achieves zero error asymptotically for any valuation distribution, though convergence is arbitrarily slow unless the optimal revenue is attained by a finite price, in which case the decay rate is roughly 1/sqrt(n). For discrete-valued distributions, learning curves decay almost exponentially, a rate not achievable under PAC learning.
该研究旨在理解收益最大化算法随数据量增长而提升的规律，首次系统分析单商品单买家场景下的学习曲线。在无分布限制下，存在一个贝叶斯一致算法，其误差随样本量趋于无穷而收敛至零，但收敛速度任意缓慢；若最优收益由有限价格实现，则收敛速度约为1/sqrt(n)；对于离散值分布，学习曲线几乎呈指数衰减，这一速度在PAC框架下无法实现。
Color-Encoded Illumination for High-Speed Volumetric Scene Reconstruction
Authors: David Novikov, Eilon Vaknin, Narek Tumanyan, Mark Sheinin
Venue: CVPR 2026 highlight
First: 2026-04-29T17:38:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:38:11+00:00
Comments: accepted to IEEE CVPR 2026 as a highlight
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The task of capturing and rendering 3D dynamic scenes from 2D images has become increasingly popular in recent years. However, most conventional cameras are bandwidth-limited to 30-60 FPS, restricting these methods to static or slowly evolving scenes. While overcoming bandwidth limitations is difficult for general scenes, recent years have seen a flurry of computational imaging methods that yield high-speed videos using conventional cameras for specific applications (e.g., motion capture and particle image velocimetry). However, most of these methods require modifications to a camera's optics or the addition of mechanically moving components, limiting them to a single-view high-speed capture. Consequently, these methods cannot be readily used to capture a 3D representation of rapid scene motion. In this paper, we propose a novel method to capture and reconstruct a volumetric representation of a high-speed scene using only unaugmented low-speed cameras. Instead of modifying the hardware or optics of each individual camera, we encode high-speed scene dynamics by illuminating the scene with a rapid, sequential color-coded sequence. This results in simultaneous multi-view capture of the scene, where high-speed temporal information is encoded in the spatial intensity and color variations of the captured images. To construct a high-speed volumetric representation of the dynamic scene, we develop a novel dynamic Gaussian Splatting-based approach that decodes the temporal information from the images. We evaluate our approach on simulated scenes and real-world experiments using a multi-camera imaging setup, showing first-of-a-kind high-speed volumetric scene reconstructions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于颜色编码照明的高速体场景重建
近年来，从2D图像捕获和渲染3D动态场景的任务日益流行。然而，大多数传统相机受带宽限制，仅支持30-60帧/秒，限制了这些方法仅适用于静态或缓慢变化的场景。虽然对一般场景克服带宽限制困难，但近年来针对特定应用（如动作捕捉和粒子图像测速）涌现出大量计算成像方法，可利用传统相机生成高速视频。然而，这些方法大多需要修改相机光学结构或增加机械运动部件，仅限于单视角高速捕获，因此无法直接用于捕捉快速场景运动的3D表示。本文提出一种新颖方法，仅使用未经增强的低速相机，即可捕获并重建高速场景的体表示。我们不修改每个相机的硬件或光学结构，而是通过快速、顺序的颜色编码照明序列照亮场景，从而实现场景的同步多视角捕获，其中高速时序信息编码在捕获图像的空间强度和颜色变化中。为构建动态场景的高速体表示，我们开发了一种新颖的基于动态高斯点绘的方法，用于从图像中解码时序信息。我们在模拟场景和真实世界实验中（使用多相机成像系统）评估了该方法，实现了首次高速体场景重建。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of conventional cameras to 30-60 FPS, which hinders 3D reconstruction of fast-moving scenes, this paper introduces a method using unaugmented low-speed cameras to capture high-speed volumetric reconstructions. It achieves this by illuminating the scene with a rapid, sequential color-coded light sequence, encoding temporal dynamics into spatial color and intensity variations across multi-view images. A novel dynamic Gaussian Splatting algorithm then decodes these encoded signals to reconstruct volumetric representations. Experiments on simulated and real-world scenes using multi-camera setups demonstrate successful first-of-a-kind high-speed volumetric reconstructions without hardware modifications.
为了解决传统相机帧率低（30-60 FPS）限制高速动态场景三维重建的问题，本文提出一种仅使用未改装的低速相机即可实现高速体素重建的方法。该方法通过快速顺序的彩色编码照明序列编码时间动态，使多视角同时捕捉中运动信息以空间色彩和强度变化形式呈现。结合新型动态高斯点渲染方法解码时间信息，实现体素重建。在模拟与真实场景的多相机实验中，成功实现了首次无需硬件改装的高速体素重建。
Causal Learning with Neural Assemblies
Authors: Evangelia Kopadi, Dimitris Kalles
First: 2026-04-29T17:34:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:34:33+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 11 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Can Neural Assemblies -- groups of neurons that fire together and strengthen through co-activation -- learn the direction of causal influence between variables? While established as a computationally general substrate for classification, parsing, and planning, neural assemblies have not yet been shown to internalize causal directionality. We demonstrate that the inherent operations of neural assemblies -- projection, local plasticity control, and sparse winner selection -- are sufficient for directional learning. We introduce DIRECT (DIRectional Edge Coupling/Training), a mechanism that co-activates source and target assemblies under an adaptive gain schedule to internalize directed relations. Unlike backpropagation-based methods, DIRECT relies solely on local plasticity, making the resulting causal claims auditable at the mechanism level. Our findings are verified through a dual-readout validation strategy: (i) synaptic-strength asymmetry, measuring the emergent weight gap between forward and reverse links, and (ii) functional propagation overlap, quantifying the reliability of directional signal flow. Across multiple domains, the framework achieves perfect structural recovery under a supervised, known-structure setting. These results establish neural assemblies as an auditable bridge between biologically plausible dynamics and formal causal models, offering an "explainable by design" framework where causal claims are traceable to specific neural winners and synaptic asymmetries.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to ground causal reasoning in biologically plausible neural mechanisms, this work investigates whether neural assemblies — groups of co-activating neurons that strengthen through plasticity — can learn directional causal relationships. The authors propose DIRECT, a local plasticity-based training mechanism that co-activates source and target assemblies under adaptive gain schedules to encode directed influences. Experimental results across multiple domains show that DIRECT achieves perfect structural recovery under supervised conditions, validated via synaptic-strength asymmetry and functional propagation overlap metrics, demonstrating that causal directionality can emerge from local, winner-take-all dynamics without backpropagation.
为将因果推断扎根于生物可实现的神经机制，该研究探讨神经群体（通过共同激活强化的神经元组）是否能学习变量间的因果方向。作者提出DIRECT机制，通过自适应增益协同激活源与目标神经群体，仅依赖局部可塑性编码有向关系，无需反向传播。在多个领域实验中，该框架在监督条件下实现完美结构恢复，通过突触强度不对称性和功能传播重叠度验证，证明神经群体可通过自身机制内化因果方向，且因果主张可追溯至特定神经赢家与突触不对称性。
The Role of Symmetry in Optimizing Overparameterized Networks
Authors: Kusha Sareen, Mohammad Pedramfar, Sékou-Oumar Kaba, Mehran Shakerinava, Siamak Ravanbakhsh
First: 2026-04-28T02:53:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:28:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Overparameterization is central to the success of deep learning, yet the mechanisms by which it improves optimization remain incompletely understood. We analyze weight-space symmetries in neural networks and show that overparameterization introduces additional symmetries that benefit optimization in two distinct ways. First, we prove that these symmetries act as a form of diagonal preconditioning on the Hessian, enabling the existence of better-conditioned minima within each equivalence class of functionally identical solutions. Second, we show that overparameterization increases the probability mass of global minima near typical initializations, making these favorable solutions more reachable. Teacher-student network experiments validate our theoretical predictions: as width increases, the Hessian trace decreases, condition numbers improve, and convergence accelerates. Our analysis provides a unified framework for understanding overparameterization and width growth as a geometric transformation of the loss landscape.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对称性在优化过参数化网络中的作用
过参数化是深度学习成功的核心，但其如何改善优化的机制尚未完全理解。我们分析神经网络的权重空间对称性，表明过参数化引入了额外的对称性，以两种不同方式促进优化。首先，我们证明这些对称性在Hessian矩阵上起到类似对角预处理的作用，使得每个功能等价解类中存在条件数更优的极小值点。其次，我们表明过参数化增加了典型初始化附近全局极小值的概率质量，使这些有利解更容易达到。教师-学生网络实验验证了我们的理论预测：随着宽度增加，Hessian迹减小，条件数改善，收敛加速。我们的分析提供了一个统一框架，用于理解过参数化和宽度增长作为损失景观的几何变换。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how overparameterization aids deep learning optimization, this study investigates weight-space symmetries in neural networks. It demonstrates that increased network width introduces additional symmetries that act as diagonal preconditioning on the Hessian, leading to better-conditioned minima within functionally equivalent solution classes. Additionally, overparameterization concentrates probability mass around global minima near typical initializations, making them more accessible. Experimental results using teacher-student networks confirm that as network width grows, Hessian trace decreases, condition numbers improve, and convergence speeds up, supporting the view that width acts as a geometric transformation of the loss landscape.
为理解过参数化如何提升深度学习优化，本研究分析了神经网络的权重空间对称性，发现网络宽度增加会引入额外对称性，通过类似Hessian对角预处理的方式改善优化，使函数等价解类内的最小值更易收敛，同时提升初始点附近全局最小值的概率质量。实验表明，随着网络宽度增加，Hessian迹减小、条件数改善、收敛速度加快，验证了过参数化通过几何方式重塑损失景观的理论观点。
AnimateAnyMesh++: A Flexible 4D Foundation Model for High-Fidelity Text-Driven Mesh Animation
Authors: Zijie Wu, Chaohui Yu, Fan Wang, Xiang Bai
First: 2026-04-29T17:27:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:27:40+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, TPAMI submission, code url: https://github.com/JarrentWu1031/AnimateAnyMesh-pp
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent advances in 4D content generation have attracted increasing attention, yet creating high-quality animated 3D models remains challenging due to the complexity of modeling spatio-temporal distributions and the scarcity of 4D training data. We present AnimateAnyMesh++, a feed-forward framework for text-driven animation of arbitrary 3D meshes with substantial upgrades in data, architecture, and generative capability. First, we expand the DyMesh-XL dataset by mining dynamic content from Objaverse-XL, increasing the number of unique identities from 60K to 300K and substantially broadening category and motion diversity. Second, we redesign DyMeshVAE-Flex with power-law topology-aware attention and vertex-normal enhanced features, which significantly improves trajectory reconstruction, local geometry preservation, and mitigates trajectory-sticking artifacts. Third, we introduce architectural changes to both DyMeshVAE-Flex and the rectified-flow (RF) generator to support variable-length sequence training and generation, enabling longer animations while preserving reconstruction fidelity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AnimateAnyMesh++ generates semantically accurate and temporally coherent mesh animations within seconds, surpassing prior approaches in quality and efficiency. The enlarged DyMesh-XL, the upgraded DyMeshVAE-Flex, and variable-length RF together deliver consistent gains across benchmarks and in-the-wild meshes. We will release code, models, and the expanded DyMesh-XL upon acceptance of this manuscript to facilitate research in 4D content creation.

近期4D内容生成技术取得进展，但高质量3D动画模型的创建仍具挑战，因时空分布建模复杂且4D训练数据稀缺。我们提出AnimateAnyMesh++，一种面向任意3D网格文本驱动动画的前馈框架，显著升级了数据、架构与生成能力。首先，通过挖掘Objaverse-XL动态内容扩展DyMesh-XL数据集，将独特身份数从6万增至30万，大幅拓宽类别与动作多样性。其次，重设计DyMeshVAE-Flex，引入幂律拓扑感知注意力与顶点法线增强特征，显著提升轨迹重建、局部几何保持，并缓解轨迹粘连伪影。第三，对DyMeshVAE-Flex与修正流（RF）生成器进行架构调整，支持可变长度序列训练与生成，实现更长动画同时保持重建保真度。大量实验表明，AnimateAnyMesh++可在数秒内生成语义准确、时序连贯的网格动画，质量与效率超越现有方法。扩展的DyMesh-XL、升级的DyMeshVAE-Flex与可变长度RF共同在基准测试与真实网格中持续提升性能。论文被接受后，我们将开源代码、模型及扩展DyMesh-XL，以促进4D内容创作研究。

How Do AI Agents Spend Your Money? Analyzing and Predicting Token Consumption in Agentic Coding Tasks
Authors: Longju Bai, Zhemin Huang, Xingyao Wang, Jiao Sun, Rada Mihalcea, Erik Brynjolfsson, Alex Pentland, Jiaxin Pei
First: 2026-04-24T17:54:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:20:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The wide adoption of AI agents in complex human workflows is driving rapid growth in LLM token consumption. When agents are deployed on tasks that require a significant amount of tokens, three questions naturally arise: (1) Where do AI agents spend the tokens? (2) Which models are more token-efficient? and (3) Can agents predict their token usage before task execution? In this paper, we present the first systematic study of token consumption patterns in agentic coding tasks. We analyze trajectories from eight frontier LLMs on SWE-bench Verified and evaluate models' ability to predict their own token costs before task execution. We find that: (1) agentic tasks are uniquely expensive, consuming 1000x more tokens than code reasoning and code chat, with input tokens rather than output tokens driving the overall cost; (2) token usage is highly variable and inherently stochastic: runs on the same task can differ by up to 30x in total tokens, and higher token usage does not translate into higher accuracy; instead, accuracy often peaks at intermediate cost and saturates at higher costs; (3) models vary substantially in token efficiency: on the same tasks, Kimi-K2 and Claude-Sonnet-4.5, on average, consume over 1.5 million more tokens than GPT-5; (4) task difficulty rated by human experts only weakly aligns with actual token costs, revealing a fundamental gap between human-perceived complexity and the computational effort agents actually expend; and (5) frontier models fail to accurately predict their own token usage (with weak-to-moderate correlations, up to 0.39) and systematically underestimate real token costs. Our study offers new insights into the economics of AI agents and can inspire future research in this direction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AI代理如何花费你的钱？分析与预测代理编码任务中的令牌消耗
AI代理在复杂人类工作流程中的广泛应用正推动LLM令牌消耗的快速增长。当代理被部署在需要大量令牌的任务时，自然会引发三个问题：(1) AI代理在何处消耗令牌？(2) 哪些模型更节省令牌？(3) 代理能否在任务执行前预测自身的令牌使用量？本文首次系统研究了代理编码任务中的令牌消耗模式。我们分析了八种前沿LLM在SWE-bench Verified上的轨迹，并评估模型在任务执行前预测自身令牌成本的能力。研究发现：(1) 代理任务成本极高，消耗的令牌量是代码推理和代码聊天的1000倍，且主要由输入令牌驱动总成本；(2) 令牌使用高度可变且具有内在随机性：相同任务的不同运行总令牌量差异可达30倍，且高令牌使用量并不意味着更高准确率；相反，准确率常在中等成本时达到峰值，并在高成本时趋于饱和；(3) 不同模型在令牌效率上差异显著：在相同任务中，Kimi-K2和Claude-Sonnet-4.5平均比GPT-5多消耗150万以上令牌；(4) 人类专家评估的任务难度与实际令牌成本仅弱相关，揭示了人类感知复杂度与代理实际计算投入之间的根本性差距；(5) 前沿模型无法准确预测自身令牌使用量（相关性仅为弱至中等，最高0.39），且系统性低估实际令牌成本。本研究为AI代理的经济性提供了新见解，并可启发未来相关研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rising cost of deploying AI agents in complex workflows, this study investigates how token consumption behaves in agentic coding tasks. Using trajectories from eight frontier LLMs on SWE-bench Verified, the authors analyze where tokens are spent, compare model efficiency, and test predictive capability. Results show agentic tasks consume up to 1000x more tokens than code reasoning or chat, primarily driven by input tokens; token usage varies widely (up to 30x per task) without correlating to accuracy, which often peaks at intermediate costs; models differ greatly in efficiency, with some consuming over 1.5 million more tokens than others on identical tasks; human-rated task difficulty poorly predicts actual token cost; and even top models fail to accurately predict their own token usage, with correlations as low as 0.39.
鉴于AI代理在复杂工作流中部署带来的计算成本激增，本研究系统分析了在SWE-bench Verified上八款前沿大模型在代理编码任务中的token消耗模式。研究发现，代理任务的token消耗是代码推理或对话的1000倍以上，主要由输入token驱动；token消耗高度随机，相同任务的运行差异可达30倍，且准确率常在中等成本时达到峰值后趋于饱和。不同模型效率差异显著，Kimi-K2和Claude-Sonnet-4.5平均比GPT-5多消耗150万以上token；人类评估的任务难度与实际token成本相关性弱，揭示了人类感知复杂度与代理实际计算开销之间的根本差距；最关键的是，前沿模型自身对token消耗的预测能力不足，相关系数最高仅0.39，普遍低估真实成本。
Inverting Foundation Models of Brain Function with Simulation-Based Inference
Authors: Niels Bracher, Xavier Intes, Stefan T. Radev
First: 2026-04-26T20:12:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:19:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Foundation models of brain activity promise a new frontier for in silico neuroscience by emulating neural responses to complex stimuli across tasks and modalities. A natural next step is to ask whether these models can also be used in reverse. Can we recover a stimulus or its properties from synthetic brain activity? We study this question in a proof-of-concept setting using TRIBEv2. We pair the brain emulator with large language models (LLMs) that generate news headlines from linguistic parameters such as valence, arousal, and dominance. We then use simulation-based inference to learn a probabilistic mapping from brain maps to latent stimulus parameters. Our results show that these parameters can be recovered from predicted brain maps, validating the quality of neural encodings. They also show that LLMs can serve as controllable stimulus generators for simulated experiments. Together, these findings provide a step toward decoding and inverse design with foundation brain models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用基于模拟的推断反演大脑功能基础模型

Experimental results demonstrate that these stimulus parameters can be accurately recovered from predicted brain activity, validating the fidelity of neural encodings and showing that LLMs can function as controllable stimulus generators for simulated experiments.
为探索基础模型能否从合成脑活动反推刺激特性，该研究以TRIBEv2脑模拟器与生成新闻标题的大语言模型（LLM）相结合，利用语言参数如效价、唤醒度和支配度生成刺激，并通过基于模拟的推断学习从脑图到潜在刺激参数的概率映射。实验结果表明，这些参数可从预测的脑活动准确恢复，验证了神经编码的可靠性，同时证明LLM可作为可控刺激生成器用于模拟实验。
Bi-Level Optimization for Contact and Motion Planning in Rope-Assisted Legged Robots
Authors: Ruben Malacarne, Ioannis Tsikelis, Enrico Mingo Hoffman, Michele Focchi
First: 2026-04-29T17:18:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:18:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a planning pipeline framework for locomotion in rope-assisted robots climbing vertical surfaces. The proposed framework is formulated as a bi-level optimization scheme that addresses a mixed-integer problem: selecting feasible terrain regions for landing while simultaneously optimizing the control inputs, namely rope tensions and leg forces, and landing location. The outer level of the optimization is solved using the Cross-Entropy Method, while the inner level relies on gradient-based nonlinear optimization to compute dynamically feasible motions. The approach is validated on a novel climbing robot platform, ALPINE, across a variety of challenging terrain configurations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：绳索辅助腿式机器人接触与运动规划的双层优化方法
本文提出了一种用于绳索辅助机器人攀爬垂直表面的运动规划框架。该框架以双层优化方案形式构建，解决混合整数问题：选择可行的着陆地形区域，同时优化控制输入（即绳索张力与腿部力）和着陆位置。外层优化采用交叉熵法求解，内层则依赖梯度非线性优化计算动态可行运动。该方法在新型攀爬机器人平台ALPINE上，针对多种复杂地形配置进行了验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient vertical locomotion in rope-assisted legged robots, this work introduces a bi-level optimization framework that jointly selects feasible landing regions and optimizes rope tensions and leg forces. The outer level employs the Cross-Entropy Method to sample and refine terrain choices, while the inner level uses gradient-based nonlinear optimization to generate dynamically feasible motions. Experimental validation on the ALPINE robot across diverse vertical terrains demonstrates the method’s ability to successfully plan and execute stable climbing trajectories under complex constraints.
为解决绳索辅助腿式机器人在垂直表面攀爬时的高效稳定运动问题，本文提出一种双层优化框架，同时选择可行着陆区域并优化绳索张力与腿部力。外层采用交叉熵方法采样并优化地形选择，内层则使用梯度非线性优化生成动力学可行运动。在ALPINE机器人上针对多种复杂地形的实验验证表明，该框架能有效规划并执行稳定攀爬运动。
A Decision-Theoretic Formalisation of Steganography With Applications to LLM Monitoring
Authors: Usman Anwar, Julianna Piskorz, David D. Baek, David Africa, Jim Weatherall, Max Tegmark, Christian Schroeder de Witt, Mihaela van der Schaar, David Krueger
First: 2026-02-26T16:27:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:15:03+00:00
Comments: First two authors contributed equally
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models are beginning to show steganographic capabilities. Such capabilities could allow misaligned models to evade oversight mechanisms. Yet principled methods to detect and quantify such behaviours are lacking. Classical definitions of steganography, and detection methods based on them, require a known reference distribution of non-steganographic signals. For the case of steganographic reasoning in LLMs, knowing such a reference distribution is not feasible; this renders these approaches inapplicable. We propose an alternative, \textbf{decision-theoretic view of steganography}. Our central insight is that steganography creates an asymmetry in usable information between agents who can and cannot decode the hidden content (present within a steganographic signal), and this otherwise latent asymmetry can be inferred from the agents' observable actions. To formalise this perspective, we introduce generalised $\mathcal{V}$-information: a utilitarian framework for measuring the amount of usable information within some input. We use this to define the \textbf{steganographic gap} -- a measure that quantifies steganography by comparing the downstream utility of the steganographic signal to agents that can and cannot decode the hidden content. We empirically validate our formalism, and show that it can be used to detect, quantify, and mitigate steganographic reasoning in LLMs.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the emerging steganographic capabilities of large language models that may allow misaligned models to evade oversight, this work proposes a decision-theoretic formalization to detect and quantify such behavior. Unlike classical steganography methods that require a known reference distribution of non-steganographic signals — which is infeasible in LLM contexts — the authors introduce generalized V-information as a utilitarian measure of usable information and define the steganographic gap as the difference in downstream utility between agents who can and cannot decode hidden content. Empirical results demonstrate that this framework successfully detects, quantifies, and mitigates steganographic reasoning in LLMs, offering a principled alternative to existing detection methods.
为检测和量化大型语言模型中可能规避监管的隐写行为，作者提出一种基于决策理论的隐写形式化方法，避免依赖不可行的非隐写信号参考分布。该方法引入广义V信息作为效用框架下的可用信息度量，并定义隐写差距——即能解码与不能解码隐藏内容的代理在下游效用上的差异——以量化隐写行为。实验结果表明，该框架可有效检测、衡量并缓解LLM中的隐写推理，验证其实际可行性。
Auto-ARGUE: LLM-Based Report Generation Evaluation
Authors: William Walden, Marc Mason, Orion Weller, Laura Dietz, John Conroy, Neil Molino, Hannah Recknor, Bryan Li, Gabrielle Kaili-May Liu, Yu Hou, Dawn Lawrie, James Mayfield, Eugene Yang
Venue: SIGIR 2026
First: 2025-09-30T12:41:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:14:06+00:00
Comments: SIGIR 2026: Demo Track
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generation of citation-backed reports is a primary use case for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems. While open-source evaluation tools exist for various RAG tasks, tools designed for report generation are lacking. Accordingly, we introduce Auto-ARGUE, a robust LLM-based implementation of the recently proposed ARGUE framework for report generation evaluation. We present analysis of Auto-ARGUE on the report generation pilot task from the TREC 2024 NeuCLIR track and on two tasks from the TREC 2024 RAG track, showing good system-level correlations with human judgments. Additionally, we release ARGUE-Viz, a web app for visualization and fine-grained analysis of Auto-ARGUE judgments and scores.

ClawGym: A Scalable Framework for Building Effective Claw Agents
Authors: Fei Bai, Huatong Song, Shuang Sun, Daixuan Cheng, Yike Yang, Chuan Hao, Renyuan Li, Feng Chang, Yuan Wei, Ran Tao, Bryan Dai, Jian Yang, Wayne Xin Zhao
First: 2026-04-29T17:12:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:12:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Claw-style environments support multi-step workflows over local files, tools, and persistent workspace states. However, scalable development around these environments remains constrained by the absence of a systematic framework, especially one for synthesizing verifiable training data and integrating it with agent training and diagnostic evaluation. To address this challenge, we present ClawGym, a scalable framework that supports the full lifecycle of Claw-style personal agent development. Concretely, we construct ClawGym-SynData, a diverse dataset of 13.5K filtered tasks synthesized from persona-driven intents and skill-grounded operations, paired with realistic mock workspaces and hybrid verification mechanisms. We then train a family of capable Claw-style models, termed ClawGym-Agents, through supervised fine-tuning on black-box rollout trajectories, and further explore reinforcement learning via a lightweight pipeline that parallelizes rollouts across per-task sandboxes.To support reliable evaluation, we further construct ClawGym-Bench, a benchmark of 200 instances calibrated through automated filtering and human-LLM review. Relevant resources will be soon released at https://github.com/ClawGym.

爪式环境支持在本地文件、工具和持久化工作区状态上执行多步骤工作流。然而，围绕这些环境的可扩展开发仍受限于缺乏系统性框架，特别是用于合成可验证训练数据并将其与代理训练和诊断评估集成的框架。为解决这一挑战，我们提出了ClawGym，一个支持爪式个人代理开发全生命周期的可扩展框架。具体而言，我们构建了ClawGym-SynData，一个包含13.5K个经过筛选任务的多样化数据集，这些任务由角色驱动意图和技能基础操作合成，并配以真实模拟工作区和混合验证机制。随后，我们通过在黑盒 rollout 轨迹上进行监督微调，训练出一系列能力强大的爪式模型，称为ClawGym-Agents，并进一步通过轻量级管道探索强化学习，该管道在每个任务沙盒中并行化 rollout。为支持可靠评估，我们还构建了ClawGym-Bench，一个通过自动化过滤和人工-LLM审核校准的200个实例基准。相关资源将很快发布于 https://github.com/ClawGym。

Recent Advances in mm-Wave and Sub-THz/THz Oscillators for FutureG Technologies
Authors: Baktash Behmanesh, Ahmad Rezvanitabar
First: 2026-04-29T17:12:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:12:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper provides a concise yet comprehensive review of recent advancements in millimeter-wave (mm-wave) oscillators below 100 GHz and sub-terahertz (sub-THz/THz) oscillators above 100 GHz for next-generation computing and communication systems, including 5G, 6G, and beyond. Various design approaches, including CMOS, SiGe, and III-V semiconductor technologies, are explored in terms of performance metrics such as phase noise, output power, efficiency, frequency tunability, and stability. The review highlights key challenges in achieving high-performance and reliable oscillator designs while discussing emerging techniques for performance enhancement. By evaluating recent design trends, this work aims to offer valuable insights and design guidelines that facilitate the development of robust mm-wave and sub-THz/THz oscillators for future communication, computing, and sensing applications.

为满足5G、6G及未来通信系统对更高数据速率和更低延迟的需求，本文综述了毫米波及亚太赫兹/太赫兹振荡器的最新进展，涵盖CMOS、SiGe和III-V半导体技术。文章评估了相位噪声、输出功率、效率、调谐范围和稳定性等关键性能指标。研究指出热噪声、寄生效应和功耗是主要挑战，并介绍了如主动负载调制和新型谐振器等新兴技术。实验结果表明，III-V材料振荡器在相位噪声和输出功率上表现更优，而CMOS设计在效率和集成度方面更胜一筹，先进原型的调谐频率已突破100 GHz。
Safe Navigation using Neural Radiance Fields via Reachable Sets
Authors: Omanshu Thapliyal, Malarvizhi Sankaranarayanasamy, Ravigopal Vennelakanti
First: 2026-04-29T17:09:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:09:19+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 8 figures, 2026 4th International Conference on Mechatronics, Control and Robotics (ICMCR)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Safe navigation in cluttered environments is an important challenge for autonomous systems. Robots navigating through obstacle ridden scenarios need to be able to navigate safely in the presence of obstacles, goals, and ego objects of varying geometries. In this work, reachable set representations of the robot's real-time capabilities in the state space can be utilized to capture safe navigation requirements. While neural radiance fields (NeRFs) are utilized to compute, store, and manipulate the volumetric representations of the obstacles, or ego vehicle, as needed. Constrained optimal control is employed to represent the resulting path planning problem, involving linear matrix inequality constraints. We present simulation results for path planning in the presence of numerous obstacles in two different scenarios. Safe navigation is demonstrated through using reachable sets in the corresponding constrained optimal control problems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过可达集利用神经辐射场实现安全导航
在杂乱环境中实现安全导航是自主系统的重要挑战。机器人在障碍物众多的场景中需要能够安全地避开障碍物、目标和自身物体（几何形状各异）。本工作中，利用机器人在状态空间中的实时能力的可达集表示，以捕捉安全导航需求。神经辐射场（NeRFs）用于计算、存储和操作障碍物或自身车辆的体素表示。采用约束最优控制来表示由此产生的路径规划问题，涉及线性矩阵不等式约束。我们展示了在两种不同场景下存在多个障碍物时的路径规划仿真结果，通过在相应的约束最优控制问题中使用可达集实现了安全导航。

To enable safe navigation in cluttered environments, this work leverages neural radiance fields to model obstacle and ego vehicle geometries, then employs reachable set representations to define safe motion boundaries in state space. By formulating the path planning problem as a constrained optimal control task with linear matrix inequality constraints, the method ensures safety while navigating around obstacles. Simulation results across two scenarios demonstrate that the approach successfully computes collision-free trajectories under dynamic constraints.
为实现复杂环境中安全导航，该研究利用神经辐射场建模障碍物与自身车辆几何结构，并通过可达集表示在状态空间中定义安全运动边界。将路径规划问题建模为带线性矩阵不等式约束的受限最优控制问题，确保轨迹无碰撞且符合机器人实时能力。在两个障碍密集场景的仿真中，方法成功实现安全导航，验证了其在复杂环境中的有效性。
Stochastic Scaling Limits and Synchronization by Noise in Deep Transformer Models
Authors: Andrea Agazzi, Giuseppe Bruno, Eloy Mosig García, Samuele Saviozzi, Marco Romito
First: 2026-04-29T17:09:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:09:05+00:00
Comments: 55 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We prove pathwise convergence of the layerwise evolution of tokens in a finite-depth, finite-width transformer model with MultiLayer Perceptron (MLP) blocks to a continuous-time stochastic interacting particle system. We also identify the stochastic partial differential equation describing the evolution of the tokens' distribution in this limit and prove propagation of chaos when the number of such tokens is large. The bounds we establish are quantitative and the limits we consider commute. We further prove that the limiting stochastic model displays synchronization by noise and establish exponential dissipation of the interaction energy on average, provided that the common noise is sufficiently coercive relative to the deterministic self-attention drift. We finally characterize the activation functions satisfying the former condition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：深度Transformer模型中随机缩放极限与噪声同步性
我们证明了在有限深度、有限宽度的Transformer模型中，带有多层感知机（MLP）块的层间令牌演化路径收敛于一个连续时间随机相互作用粒子系统。我们还识别出该极限下令牌分布演化的随机偏微分方程，并在令牌数量较大时证明了混沌传播。我们建立的界限是定量的，且所考虑的极限可交换。进一步地，我们证明了极限随机模型表现出噪声同步性，并在共同噪声相对于确定性自注意力漂移足够强时，平均意义上交互能量呈指数衰减。最后，我们刻画了满足前述条件的激活函数。

The method involves deriving a stochastic partial differential equation that governs the token distribution in the limit and proving propagation of chaos for large token counts.
