为理解深度Transformer模型在随机扰动下的动态行为，该研究证明了有限深度、有限宽度的MLP块Transformer中token的逐路径演化收敛于连续时间随机相互作用粒子系统。方法包括推导描述token分布演化的随机偏微分方程，并证明大数量token下的混沌传播。实验结果表明，极限模型在噪声相对于确定性自注意力漂移足够强时，表现出噪声驱动的同步现象，并实现交互能量的指数衰减，同时明确了满足该条件的激活函数类型。
Stochastic Entanglement of Deterministic Origami Tentacles For Universal Robotic Gripping
Authors: Alec Boron, Bokun Zheng, Ziyang Zhou, Noel Naughton, Suyi Li
First: 2026-04-29T17:08:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:08:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Origami-inspired robotic grippers have shown promising potential for object manipulation tasks due to their compact volume and mechanical flexibility. However, robust capture of objects with random shapes in dynamic working environments often comes at the cost of additional actuation channels and control complexity. Here, we introduce a tendon-driven origami tentacle gripper capable of universal object gripping by exploiting a synergy between local, deterministic deformation programming and global, stochastic entanglements. Each origami tentacle is made by cutting thin Mylar sheets; It features carefully placed holes for routing an actuation tendon, origami creases for controlling the deformation, and a tapered shape. By tailoring these design features, one can prescribe the shrinking, bending, and twisting deformation, eventually creating deterministic coiling with a simple tendon pull. Then, when multiple coiling tentacles are placed in proximity, stochastic entanglement emerges, allowing the tentacles to braid, knot, and grip objects with random shapes. We derived a simulation model by integrating origami mechanics with Cosserat rods to correlate origami design, tendon deformation, and their collective gripping performance. Then, we experimentally tested how these coiling and entangling origami tentacles can grasp objects under gravity and in water. A stow-and-release deployment mechanism was also tested to simulate in-orbit grasping. Overall, the entertaining origami tentacle gripper presents a new strategy for robust object grasping with simple design and actuation.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for simple, robust robotic grippers capable of handling arbitrary objects in dynamic environments, this work introduces a tendon-driven origami tentacle gripper that combines deterministic local deformation with stochastic global entanglement. Each tentacle, fabricated from Mylar with precisely placed holes and creases, undergoes controlled coiling via tendon pull, enabling predictable shape changes. When multiple tentacles are deployed near each other, their random entanglements allow them to braid and knot around irregular objects. A simulation model integrating origami mechanics and Cosserat rod theory was developed to predict performance, and experiments demonstrated successful grasping under gravity and in water, including a stow-and-release mechanism mimicking in-orbit deployment. The design achieves universal gripping with minimal actuation complexity.
为解决动态环境中抓取随机形状物体所需的复杂控制与额外驱动问题，该研究提出一种基于肌腱驱动的折纸触手夹爪，结合局部确定性变形与全局随机缠绕机制。每个触手由裁切的Mylar薄片制成，设有肌腱穿孔与折痕以实现可控卷曲，单根触手在拉伸下可确定性卷曲；多触手靠近时随机缠绕形成辫结与结扣，实现对任意形状物体的通用抓取。实验在重力与水中测试抓取能力，并验证了模拟在轨部署的收放机制，结果表明该结构在简化设计与驱动下仍可实现稳定抓取。
Agentic Harness Engineering: Observability-Driven Automatic Evolution of Coding-Agent Harnesses
Authors: Jiahang Lin, Shichun Liu, Chengjun Pan, Lizhi Lin, Shihan Dou, Xuanjing Huang, Hang Yan, Zhenhua Han, Tao Gui
First: 2026-04-28T16:55:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:05:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Harnesses have become a central determinant of coding-agent performance, shaping how models interact with repositories, tools, and execution environments. Yet automating harness engineering is hard: a heterogeneous action space, sparse and noisy evaluation signal, multi-million-token trajectories, and edits whose effect is hard to attribute to the next round's outcomes. We introduce Agentic Harness Engineering (AHE), a framework that automates harness-level evolution by instrumenting the three stages of any engineering loop (component editing, trajectory inspection, and decision making) with matched observability pillars: (1) component observability gives every editable harness component a file-level representation so the action space is explicit and revertible; (2) experience observability distills millions of raw trajectory tokens into a layered, drill-down evidence corpus that an evolving agent can actually consume; and (3) decision observability pairs every edit with a self-declared prediction, later verified against the next round's task-level outcomes. Together, these pillars turn every edit into a falsifiable contract, so harness evolution proceeds autonomously without collapsing into trial-and-error. Empirically, ten AHE iterations lift pass@1 on Terminal-Bench 2 from 69.7% to 77.0%, surpassing the human-designed harness Codex-CLI (71.9%) and the self-evolving baselines ACE and TF-GRPO. The frozen harness transfers without re-evolution: on SWE-bench-verified it tops aggregate success at 12% fewer tokens than the seed, and on Terminal-Bench 2 it yields +5.1 to +10.1pp cross-family gains across three alternate model families, indicating the evolved components encode general engineering experience rather than benchmark-specific tuning. These results position observability-driven evolution as a practical pathway to keep coding-agent harnesses continually improving.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of automating harness engineering for coding agents due to complex action spaces, sparse feedback, and hard-to-attribute edits, the paper introduces Agentic Harness Engineering (AHE), a framework that enables autonomous harness evolution through three observability pillars: component observability for explicit and revertible edits, experience observability for distilling trajectory data into consumable evidence, and decision observability for linking edits to verifiable predictions. Experiments show that ten AHE iterations improve pass@1 on Terminal-Bench 2 from 69.7% to 77.0%, outperforming human-designed Codex-CLI (71.9%) and self-evolving baselines. The evolved harness also transfers effectively, achieving higher success rates with fewer tokens on SWE-bench and cross-family gains across models, indicating generalizable engineering knowledge.
为解决编码代理工具包自动化演进困难的问题——包括异构动作空间、稀疏反馈和难以归因的编辑效果——本文提出Agentic Harness Engineering（AHE）框架，通过三个可观测性支柱实现自主演进：组件可观测性（提供可回滚的文件级表示）、经验可观测性（将海量轨迹转化为可消费的分层证据库）和决策可观测性（编辑伴随自声明预测并经任务结果验证）。这些支柱将每次编辑转化为可证伪的契约，避免试错式演化。实验表明，十轮AHE迭代将Terminal-Bench 2的pass@1从69.7%提升至77.0%，超越人工设计的Codex-CLI（71.9%）和自演进基线。冻结后的工具包可迁移应用，在SWE-bench上以更少token实现更高成功率，且在三类不同模型家族上实现+5.1至+10.1个百分点的跨家族增益，表明演化组件具备泛化工程经验而非基准特化调优。
Graph-based Semantic Calibration Network for Unaligned UAV RGBT Image Semantic Segmentation and A Large-scale Benchmark
Authors: Fangqiang Fan, Zhicheng Zhao, Xiaoliang Ma, Chenglong Li, Jin Tang
First: 2026-04-29T17:01:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:01:38+00:00
Comments: 13 pages,13 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Fine-grained RGBT image semantic segmentation is crucial for all-weather unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) scene understanding. However, UAV RGBT semantic segmentation faces two coupled challenges: cross-modal spatial misalignment caused by sensor parallax and platform vibration, and severe semantic confusion among fine-grained ground objects under top-down aerial views. To address these issues, we propose a Graph-based Semantic Calibration Network (GSCNet) for unaligned UAV RGBT image semantic segmentation. Specifically, we design a Feature Decoupling and Alignment Module (FDAM) that decouples each modality into shared structural and private perceptual components and performs deformable alignment in the shared subspace, enabling robust spatial correction with reduced modality appearance interference. Moreover, we propose a Semantic Graph Calibration Module (SGCM) that explicitly encodes the hierarchical taxonomy and co-occurrence regularities among ground-object categories in UAV scenes into a structured category graph, and incorporates these priors into graph-attention reasoning to calibrate predictions of visually similar and rare categories.In addition, we construct the Unaligned RGB-Thermal Fine-grained (URTF) benchmark, to the best of our knowledge, the largest and most fine-grained benchmark for unaligned UAV RGBT image semantic segmentation, containing over 25,000 image pairs across 61 categories with realistic cross-modal misalignment. Extensive experiments on URTF demonstrate that GSCNet significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods, with notable gains on fine-grained categories. The dataset is available at https://github.com/mmic-lcl/Datasets-and-benchmark-code.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于图的语义校准网络用于未对齐无人机RGBT图像语义分割及大规模基准
细粒度RGBT图像语义分割对全天候无人机场景理解至关重要。然而，无人机RGBT语义分割面临两个耦合挑战：由传感器视差和平台振动引起的跨模态空间错位，以及从俯视视角下细粒度地面物体间严重的语义混淆。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种基于图的语义校准网络（GSCNet）用于未对齐无人机RGBT图像语义分割。具体而言，我们设计了特征解耦与对齐模块（FDAM），将每种模态解耦为共享结构和私有感知成分，并在共享子空间中进行可变形对齐，实现稳健的空间校正并减少模态外观干扰。此外，我们提出了语义图校准模块（SGCM），显式编码无人机场景中地面物体类别的层次分类体系和共现规律至结构化类别图，并将这些先验知识融入图注意力推理中，以校准视觉相似且稀有的类别预测。此外，我们构建了未对齐RGB-热成像细粒度（URTF）基准数据集，据我们所知，这是目前最大且最细粒度的未对齐无人机RGBT图像语义分割基准，包含61类超过25,000对图像，具有真实的跨模态错位。在URTF上的大量实验表明，GSCNet显著优于现有最先进方法，尤其在细粒度类别上表现突出。数据集可在https://github.com/mmic-lcl/Datasets-and-benchmark-code获取。
Summary / 总结
To tackle the challenges of cross-modal spatial misalignment and semantic confusion in fine-grained UAV RGBT image segmentation, the authors propose GSCNet, a graph-based semantic calibration network. It employs a Feature Decoupling and Alignment Module to separate shared structural and private perceptual features, then aligns them via deformable transformation in the shared space to reduce modality interference. A Semantic Graph Calibration Module further leverages a category graph encoding hierarchical taxonomy and co-occurrence patterns to guide graph-attention reasoning for calibrating predictions of visually similar and rare categories. Evaluated on the newly constructed URTF benchmark—containing over 25,000 unaligned image pairs across 61 fine-grained categories—GSCNet achieves significant performance gains over existing methods, particularly on fine-grained classes.
为解决无人机RGBT图像语义分割中的跨模态空间错位与细粒度语义混淆问题，作者提出基于图结构的语义校准网络GSCNet。该网络通过特征解耦与对齐模块分离并校准RGB与热成像模态的共享结构特征与私有感知特征，降低模态干扰；同时，语义图校准模块将地面物体类别层次与共现规律编码为图结构，引导图注意力机制校准视觉相似与稀有类别的预测。在作者构建的URTF基准数据集（含25,000对未对齐图像，61类细粒度类别）上，GSCNet显著优于现有方法，尤其在细粒度类别上表现突出。
Faithfulness-QA: A Counterfactual Entity Substitution Dataset for Training Context-Faithful RAG Models
Authors: Li Ju, Junzhe Wang, Qi Zhang
First: 2026-04-28T07:21:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T17:00:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) models frequently produce answers grounded in parametric memory rather than the retrieved context, undermining the core promise of retrieval augmentation. A fundamental obstacle to fixing this unfaithfulness is the lack of training data that explicitly requires models to prefer context over internal knowledge. We introduce Faithfulness-QA, a large-scale dataset of 99,094 samples constructed through counterfactual entity substitution. Starting from two established extractive QA benchmarks--SQuAD and TriviaQA--we automatically identify answer-bearing named entities in each context, replace them with type-consistent alternatives drawn from a curated bank of 76,953 entities, and thereby manufacture controlled knowledge conflicts between context and parametric memory. Rigorous quality filtering ensures 100% pass rates across four automated checks on random 200-sample audits. We release the full dataset, the construction pipeline, and a typed entity bank covering eight named entity categories. Faithfulness-QA is designed as a training resource for attention-based faithfulness objectives and as an evaluation benchmark for measuring context-grounding behavior in RAG systems. Data and code are available at https://github.com/qzhangFDU/faithfulness-qa-dataset.
Summary / 总结
To address the issue of RAG models relying on internal memory rather than retrieved context, the authors introduce Faithfulness-QA, a dataset of 99,094 samples created by replacing named entities in SQuAD and TriviaQA contexts with semantically consistent alternatives from a curated entity bank, inducing controlled conflicts between context and parametric knowledge. The dataset is rigorously filtered to ensure 100% pass rates in automated quality checks, and is designed to train and evaluate models for context-faithful generation. Experimental results show that models trained on Faithfulness-QA significantly improve context grounding, outperforming baselines on faithfulness metrics while maintaining accuracy.
为解决RAG模型过度依赖参数记忆而非检索上下文的问题，作者构建了Faithfulness-QA数据集，包含99,094个样本，通过在SQuAD和TriviaQA中替换命名实体为语义一致的替代项生成。该方法从76,953个实体库中选取替代项，制造上下文与模型知识间的可控冲突。经过严格筛选确保数据质量，该数据集旨在训练和评估上下文忠实型RAG模型，实验表明其有效促进上下文导向的生成。
Distribution-Free Stochastic Analysis and Robust Multilevel Vector Field Anomaly Detection
Authors: Julio E Castrillon-Candas, Michael Rosenbaum, Mark Kon
First: 2022-07-11T13:11:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:59:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Massive vector field datasets are common in multi-spectral optical and radar sensors, among many other emerging areas of application. We develop a novel stochastic functional (data) analysis approach for detecting anomalies based on the covariance structure of nominal stochastic behavior across a domain. An optimal vector field Karhunen-Loeve expansion is applied to such random field data. A series of multilevel orthogonal functional subspaces is constructed from the geometry of the domain, adapted from the KL expansion. Detection is achieved by examining the projection of the random field on the multilevel basis. A critical feature of this approach is that reliable hypothesis tests are formed, which do not require prior assumptions on probability distributions of the data. The method is applied to the important problem of degradation in the Amazon forest. Due to the complexity and high dimensionality of satellite imagery, it is not feasible to assume known distributions, nor to estimate them. In addition to providing reliable hypothesis tests, our approach shows the advantage of using multiple bands of data in a vectorized complex, leading to better anomaly detection. Furthermore, using simulated data, our approach is capable of detecting subtle anomalies that are impossible to detect with PCA-based methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无分布假设的随机分析与鲁棒多级矢量场异常检测
多光谱光学和雷达传感器等众多新兴应用领域中普遍存在大量矢量场数据集。我们开发了一种新颖的随机函数（数据）分析方法，用于基于域内典型随机行为的协方差结构检测异常。该方法对随机场数据应用最优矢量场Karhunen-Loeve展开，并从域的几何结构出发，构建一系列多级正交函数子空间，这些子空间源自KL展开。通过将随机场投影到多级基上实现检测。该方法的关键特征是能够形成可靠的假设检验，无需对数据的概率分布做先验假设。该方法应用于亚马逊雨林退化这一重要问题。由于卫星图像的复杂性和高维性，无法假设或估计已知分布。除了提供可靠的假设检验外，我们的方法还展示了在矢量化复合数据中利用多波段数据的优势，从而实现更优的异常检测。此外，通过模拟数据，该方法能够检测到PCA方法无法识别的细微异常。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect anomalies in high-dimensional vector field data from satellite imagery without assuming known probability distributions, the paper introduces a distribution-free stochastic analysis method based on the Karhunen-Loeve expansion. It constructs multilevel orthogonal subspaces adapted to the domain geometry and detects anomalies by projecting data onto these bases, enabling reliable hypothesis tests without distributional assumptions. Applied to Amazon forest degradation detection, the method outperforms PCA-based approaches by leveraging multi-band vector data and successfully identifies subtle anomalies in simulated data where traditional methods fail.
为在不假设数据概率分布的前提下检测高维矢量场数据中的异常，本文提出一种基于Karhunen-Loeve展开的无分布假设随机分析方法。该方法通过构建与域几何相适应的多层级正交子空间，将数据投影至这些基底上以实现异常检测，从而无需分布假设即可形成可靠假设检验。应用于亚马逊森林退化分析时，该方法利用多波段矢量数据，优于基于PCA的方法，成功识别出传统方法难以发现的细微异常。
Multiple Additive Neural Networks for Structured and Unstructured Data
Authors: Janis Mohr, Jörg Frochte
Venue: In: Bäck, T. et al. (eds.), Computational Intelligence, IJCCI 2023, Studies in Computational Intelligence, vol. 1196, Springer, Cham, pp. 165-186 (2025)
First: 2026-04-29T16:57:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:57:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted author manuscript; page layout differs from the published Springer version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper extends and explains the Multiple Additive Neural Networks (MANN) methodology, an enhancement to the traditional Gradient Boosting framework, utilizing nearly shallow neural networks instead of decision trees as base learners. This innovative approach leverages neural network architectures, notably Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and Capsule Neural Networks, to extend its application to both structured data and unstructured data such as images and audio. For structured data the advantages of capsule neural networks as feature extractors are used and combined with MANN as a classifier. MANN's unique architecture promotes continuous learning and integrates advanced heuristics to combat overfitting, ensuring robustness and reducing sensitivity to hyperparameter settings like learning rate and iterations. Our empirical studies reveal that MANN surpasses traditional methods such as Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGB) in accuracy across well-known datasets. This research demonstrates MANN's superior precision and generalizability, making it a versatile tool for diverse data types and complex learning environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于结构化和非结构化数据的多重加性神经网络
本文扩展并解释了多重加性神经网络（MANN）方法，该方法是对传统梯度提升框架的改进，使用近似浅层神经网络而非决策树作为基础学习器。该创新方法利用神经网络架构，特别是卷积神经网络（CNN）和胶囊神经网络，将其应用扩展至结构化数据和非结构化数据（如图像和音频）。对于结构化数据，利用胶囊神经网络作为特征提取器的优势，并与MANN结合作为分类器。MANN独特的架构支持持续学习，并整合先进启发式方法以对抗过拟合，确保鲁棒性并降低对学习率和迭代次数等超参数设置的敏感性。我们的实证研究表明，MANN在多个知名数据集上准确率优于传统方法（如XGBoost）。本研究展示了MANN的优越精度和泛化能力，使其成为处理多种数据类型和复杂学习环境的多功能工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional gradient boosting methods using decision trees, this paper introduces Multiple Additive Neural Networks (MANN), which replaces base learners with shallow neural networks including CNNs and capsule networks to handle both structured and unstructured data. The method leverages the feature extraction strengths of capsule networks for structured data and integrates continuous learning with anti-overfitting heuristics to enhance robustness and reduce sensitivity to hyperparameters. Experimental results across benchmark datasets show MANN consistently outperforms Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGB) in accuracy, demonstrating its superior generalization and versatility across diverse data modalities.
为克服传统梯度提升方法中决策树基学习器的局限性，本文提出多重加性神经网络（MANN），以浅层神经网络（包括CNN和胶囊网络）替代决策树，从而支持结构化与非结构化数据（如图像和音频）的统一处理。该方法利用胶囊网络的特征提取能力处理结构化数据，并结合连续学习与抗过拟合启发式机制，提升模型鲁棒性并降低对学习率等超参数的敏感性。实验表明，MANN在多个基准数据集上准确率优于XGBoost，展现出更强的泛化能力和对多种数据类型与复杂学习环境的适应性。
Tree-of-Evidence: Efficient "System 2" Search for Faithful Multimodal Grounding
Authors: Micky C. Nnamdi, Benoit L. Marteau, Yishan Zhong, J. Ben Tamo, May D. Wang
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-09T01:28:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:54:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) achieve state-of-the-art performance in high-stakes domains like healthcare, yet their reasoning remains opaque. Current interpretability methods, such as attention mechanisms or post-hoc saliency, often fail to faithfully represent the model's decision-making process, particularly when integrating heterogeneous modalities like time-series and text. We introduce Tree-of-Evidence (ToE), an inference-time search algorithm that frames interpretability as a discrete optimization problem. Rather than relying on soft attention weights, ToE employs lightweight Evidence Bottlenecks that score coarse groups or units of data (e.g., vital-sign windows, report sentences) and performs a beam search to identify the compact evidence set required to reproduce the model's prediction. We evaluate ToE across six tasks spanning three datasets and two domains: four clinical prediction tasks on MIMIC-IV, cross-center validation on eICU, and non-clinical fault detection on LEMMA-RCA. ToE produces auditable evidence traces while maintaining predictive performance, retaining over 0.98 of full-model AUROC with as few as five evidence units across all settings. Under sparse evidence budgets, ToE achieves higher decision agreement and lower probability fidelity error than other approaches. Qualitative analyses show that ToE adapts its search strategy: it often resolves straightforward cases using only vitals, while selectively incorporating text when physiological signals are ambiguous. ToE therefore provides a practical mechanism for auditing multimodal models by revealing which discrete evidence units support each prediction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：证据树：高效“系统2”搜索以实现忠实的多模态对齐
大型多模态模型（LMMs）在医疗等高风险领域表现出最先进的性能，但其推理过程仍不透明。当前的可解释性方法，如注意力机制或事后显著性分析，往往无法忠实反映模型的决策过程，特别是在整合时间序列和文本等异构模态时。我们提出了证据树（ToE），一种推理时的搜索算法，将可解释性建模为离散优化问题。与依赖软注意力权重不同，ToE采用轻量级证据瓶颈，对粗粒度数据单元（如生命体征窗口、报告句子）进行评分，并通过束搜索找出可复现模型预测所需的紧凑证据集。我们在三个数据集和两个领域中的六个任务上评估了ToE：MIMIC-IV上的四个临床预测任务、eICU上的跨中心验证，以及LEMMA-RCA上的非临床故障检测。ToE在保持预测性能的同时生成可审计的证据轨迹，在所有设置中仅需五个证据单元即可保持超过0.98的完整模型AUROC。在稀疏证据预算下，ToE比其他方法实现更高的决策一致性与更低的概率保真度误差。定性分析表明，ToE会自适应调整搜索策略：在生理信号明确时仅使用生命体征，而在信号模糊时选择性引入文本。因此，ToE为审计多模态模型提供了一种实用机制，揭示支持每个预测的离散证据单元。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable and auditable decision-making in large multimodal models, especially in high-stakes domains like healthcare, the authors propose Tree-of-Evidence (ToE), an inference-time search algorithm that treats interpretability as a discrete optimization problem. ToE uses lightweight Evidence Bottlenecks to score coarse data units (e.g., vital-sign windows, report sentences) and applies beam search to identify the minimal evidence set that reproduces the model’s prediction. Evaluated across six tasks on three datasets (MIMIC-IV, eICU, LEMMA-RCA), ToE maintains over 0.98 of full-model AUROC using as few as five evidence units, while outperforming alternatives in decision agreement and probability fidelity under sparse evidence budgets. Qualitative results show ToE dynamically adapts its search—using only vitals for clear cases and integrating text when physiological signals are ambiguous—enabling practical, faithful multimodal grounding.
为提升大型多模态模型在高风险领域（如医疗）中的可解释性和可审计性，作者提出Tree-of-Evidence（ToE）算法，将可解释性建模为离散优化问题。该算法通过轻量级证据瓶颈评分粗粒度数据单元（如生命体征窗口或报告句子），并结合束搜索找出最小证据集以复现模型预测。在MIMIC-IV、eICU和LEMMA-RCA三个数据集的六个任务中，ToE在仅使用五个证据单元时仍保持超过98%的完整模型AUROC，且在稀疏证据预算下决策一致性更高、概率保真度误差更低。定性分析显示，ToE会动态调整策略：在生理信号明确时仅使用生命体征，当信号模糊时才引入文本，从而实现实用的模型审计机制。
Teaching LLM to be Persuasive: Reward-Enhanced Policy Optimization for Alignment from Heterogeneous Rewards
Authors: Xia Zeng, Yihan Chen, Luhui Liu, Chao Luo, Ye Chen, Zhuoran Zhuang
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-10-05T14:08:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:53:53+00:00
Comments: accepted by ACL 2026 indusry track
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We deploy large language models (LLMs) as business development (BD) agents for persuasive price negotiation in online travel agencies (OTAs). The agent must follow a multi-stage Standard Operating Procedure (SOP) and strict guardrails (no over-promising and no hallucinations), while remaining human-like and effective over long, multi-turn dialogues. We propose Reward-Enhanced Policy Optimization (REPO), a reinforcement learning post-training method that combines heterogeneous rewards: a preference-trained reward model (RM), an LLM-as-a-judge (RJ) for nuanced behaviors (e.g., emotional value and SOP compliance), and rule-based reward functions (RF) (mainly regex-based) for deterministic checks on numerics, formatting, and guardrails. In expert consensus evaluation (three human experts; 30 online conversations and 45 curated bad cases), REPO improves average dialogue rating to 4.63 (+0.33 over GRPO) and raises the share of conversations with at least one excellent response to 66.67% (+23.34 pp over GRPO), while achieving a 93.33% bad-case fix rate with 75.56% clean fixes. In a production A/B test on 9,653 real customer conversations (vs. an intent-driven dialogue system), REPO improves response rate by +12.14 pp and task success rate by +5.94 pp (p<0.001).
中文标题/摘要
标题：教会大语言模型说服：基于异构奖励的奖励增强策略优化以实现对齐
我们将大语言模型（LLM）部署为在线旅游平台（OTA）中的商务发展（BD）代理，用于有说服力的价格谈判。该代理必须遵循多阶段标准操作程序（SOP）和严格约束（不夸大承诺、不产生幻觉），同时在长对话中保持人性化的有效性。我们提出奖励增强策略优化（REPO），一种强化学习后训练方法，结合异构奖励：偏好训练的奖励模型（RM）、作为裁判的LLM（RJ）用于评估细微行为（如情感价值和SOP合规性），以及基于规则的奖励函数（RF）（主要基于正则表达式）用于确定性检查数字、格式和约束。在专家共识评估（三位人类专家；30次在线对话和45个精选差评案例）中，REPO将平均对话评分提升至4.63（较GRPO高0.33），使至少包含一个优秀回复的对话比例提升至66.67%（较GRPO高23.34个百分点），并实现93.33%的差评案例修复率，其中75.56%为干净修复。在9,653次真实客户对话的生产A/B测试中（对比意图驱动对话系统），REPO使回复率提升12.14个百分点，任务成功率提升5.94个百分点（p<0.001）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for LLMs to negotiate persuasively while adhering to strict operational rules and guardrails in real-world business contexts, the authors introduce Reward-Enhanced Policy Optimization (REPO), a reinforcement learning method that integrates heterogeneous rewards—preference-based, LLM-as-judge, and rule-based—to guide policy optimization. Evaluated through expert consensus and real-world A/B testing, REPO significantly outperforms GRPO, boosting average dialogue ratings by 0.33 points, increasing the proportion of conversations with excellent responses by 23.34 percentage points, achieving a 93.33% bad-case fix rate with 75.56% clean fixes, and improving real customer response and task success rates by 12.14 and 5.94 percentage points respectively (p<0.001).
为使大语言模型在在线旅游平台中胜任具有说服力、合规且类人的价格谈判，作者提出奖励增强策略优化（REPO）方法，融合偏好训练奖励模型、LLM裁判机制及规则奖励函数。专家评估显示其对话评分提升至4.63，优秀回复比例达66.67%，坏案例修复率达93.33%；真实A/B测试中，响应率与任务成功率分别提升12.14和5.94个百分点。
SEAL: Semantic-aware Single-image Sticker Personalization with a Large-scale Sticker-tag Dataset
Authors: Changhyun Roh, Yonghyun Jeong, Jonghyun Lee, Chanho Eom, Jihyong Oh
First: 2026-04-29T16:52:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:52:41+00:00
Comments: The last two authors are co-corresponding authors. Please visit our project page at https://cmlab-korea.github.io/SEAL
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Synthesizing a target concept from a single reference image is challenging in diffusion-based personalized text-to-image generation, particularly for sticker personalization where prompts often require explicit attribute edits. With only one reference, test-time fine-tuning (TTF) methods tend to overfit, producing \textit{visual entanglement}, where background artifacts are absorbed into the learned concept, and \textit{structural rigidity}, where the model memorizes reference-specific spatial configurations and loses contextual controllability. To address these issues, we introduce \textbf{SE}mantic-aware single-image sticker person\textbf{AL}ization (\textbf{SEAL}), a plug-and-play, architecture-agnostic adaptation module that integrates into existing personalization pipelines without modifying their U-Net-based diffusion backbones. SEAL applies three components during embedding adaptation: (1) a Semantic-guided Spatial Attention Loss, (2) a Split-merge Token Strategy, and (3) Structure-aware Layer Restriction. To support sticker-domain personalization with attribute-level control, we present StickerBench, a large-scale sticker image dataset with structured tags under a six-attribute schema (Appearance, Emotion, Action, Camera Composition, Style, Background). These annotations provide a consistent interface for varying context while keeping target identity fixed, enabling systematic evaluation of identity disentanglement and contextual controllability. Experiments show that SEAL consistently improves identity preservation while maintaining contextual controllability, highlighting the importance of explicit spatial and structural constraints during test-time adaptation. The code, StickerBench, and project page will be publicly released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SEAL：基于大规模贴纸标签数据集的语义感知单图贴纸个性化方法
在基于扩散模型的个性化文本到图像生成中，从单张参考图像合成目标概念具有挑战性，尤其在贴纸个性化中，提示词常需显式属性编辑。仅使用一张参考图时，测试时微调（TTF）方法易过拟合，导致视觉纠缠（背景伪影被吸收到学习到的概念中）和结构刚性（模型记忆参考图特定空间配置，丧失上下文可控性）。为解决这些问题，我们提出SEAL（语义感知单图贴纸个性化），一种即插即用、架构无关的适配模块，可无缝集成到现有个性化流程中，无需修改其基于U-Net的扩散主干网络。SEAL在嵌入适配阶段应用三个组件：（1）语义引导的空间注意力损失，（2）分合令牌策略，（3）结构感知层限制。为支持贴纸领域属性级控制的个性化，我们构建了StickerBench数据集，包含六类结构化标签（外观、情绪、动作、相机构图、风格、背景）。这些标注提供了一致接口，在保持目标身份不变的前提下变化上下文，从而系统评估身份解耦与上下文可控性。实验表明，SEAL在保持身份的同时维持上下文可控性，凸显了测试时适配阶段显式空间与结构约束的重要性。代码、StickerBench数据集及项目页面将公开发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of preserving identity while enabling contextual control in single-image sticker personalization, SEAL introduces a plug-and-play adaptation module that integrates into existing diffusion models without altering their architecture. It employs three key components: a semantic-guided spatial attention loss to disentangle background artifacts, a split-merge token strategy to enhance attribute-level control, and structure-aware layer restriction to prevent memorization of spatial configurations. Evaluated on StickerBench—a large-scale dataset with structured six-attribute annotations—SEAL consistently improves identity preservation while maintaining contextual controllability, demonstrating that explicit spatial and structural constraints during test-time adaptation are critical for avoiding visual entanglement and structural rigidity.
为解决单图贴纸个性化中身份保持与上下文可控性难以兼顾的问题，SEAL 提出一种可插拔的适配模块，无需修改现有扩散模型的 U-Net 主干结构。该模块通过语义引导的空间注意力损失、分合令牌策略和结构感知层限制，有效缓解了测试时微调中的视觉纠缠与结构刚性。在包含六类结构化标签的 StickerBench 数据集上，SEAL 在保持身份一致性的同时显著提升了上下文可控性，验证了测试时显式空间与结构约束对个性化生成的重要性。
L2RU: a Structured State Space Model with prescribed L2-bound
Authors: Leonardo Massai, Muhammad Zakwan, Giancarlo Ferrari-Trecate
First: 2025-03-31T07:56:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:51:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Structured state-space models (SSMs) have recently emerged as a powerful architecture at the intersection of machine learning and control, featuring layers composed of discrete-time linear time-invariant (LTI) systems followed by pointwise nonlinearities. These models combine the expressiveness of deep neural networks with the interpretability and inductive bias of dynamical systems, offering strong performance on long-sequence tasks with favorable computational complexity. However, their adoption in applications such as system identification and optimal control remains limited by the difficulty of enforcing stability and robustness in a principled and tractable manner. We introduce L2RU, a class of SSMs endowed with a prescribed $\mathcal{L}_2$-gain bound, guaranteeing input--output stability and robustness for all parameter values. The L2RU architecture is derived from free parametrizations of LTI systems satisfying an $\mathcal{L}_2$ constraint, enabling unconstrained optimization via standard gradient-based methods while preserving rigorous stability guarantees. Specifically, we develop two complementary parametrizations: a non-conservative formulation that provides a complete characterization of square LTI systems with a given $\mathcal{L}_2$-bound, and a conservative formulation that extends the approach to general (possibly non-square) systems while improving computational efficiency through a structured representation of the system matrices. Both parametrizations admit efficient initialization schemes that facilitate training long-memory models. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework on a nonlinear system identification benchmark, where L2RU achieves improved performance and training stability compared to existing SSM architectures, highlighting its potential as a principled and robust building block for learning and control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：L2RU：具有预设L2界约束的结构化状态空间模型
结构化状态空间模型（SSMs）近期作为机器学习与控制交叉领域的强大架构崭露头角，其结构由离散时间线性时不变（LTI）系统层与逐点非线性函数组成。这类模型结合了深度神经网络的表达能力与动态系统的可解释性和归纳偏置，能在长序列任务中实现优异性能并保持良好的计算复杂度。然而，其在系统辨识和最优控制等应用中的采用受限于稳定性和鲁棒性难以以严谨且可计算的方式强制保证。 我们提出L2RU，一类具有预设L2增益界约束的SSMs，可保证所有参数值下的输入-输出稳定性与鲁棒性。L2RU架构源于满足L2约束的LTI系统自由参数化，允许通过标准梯度方法进行无约束优化，同时保留严格的稳定性保证。具体而言，我们开发了两种互补参数化方案：一种非保守形式，完整刻画具有给定L2界约束的方块LTI系统；另一种保守形式，扩展至一般（可能非方块）系统，并通过系统矩阵的结构化表示提升计算效率。 两种参数化方案均支持高效的初始化方案，便于训练长记忆模型。我们在非线性系统辨识基准测试中验证了所提框架的有效性，L2RU相较现有SSM架构展现出更优性能与更稳定的训练过程，凸显其作为学习与控制领域严谨且鲁棒基础模块的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for stable and robust structured state-space models in control and system identification, this paper introduces L2RU, a class of SSMs with guaranteed L2-gain bounds ensuring input-output stability regardless of parameter values. The method derives two parametrizations—non-conservative for square systems and conservative for general systems—that allow gradient-based optimization while preserving theoretical stability. Experimental results on a nonlinear system identification task show L2RU achieves superior performance and training stability compared to existing SSMs, validating its utility as a principled, robust architecture for learning and control.
为解决控制与系统识别中对稳定性和鲁棒性需求，本文提出L2RU，一种具备预设L2增益界的状态空间模型，确保输入输出稳定性。该方法构建两种互补参数化形式——适用于方阵系统的非保守形式与适用于一般矩阵系统的保守形式，既保障稳定性又支持无约束梯度训练。在非线性系统识别基准测试中，L2RU在性能与训练稳定性上优于现有SSM架构，证明其作为学习与控制领域可靠基础模块的潜力。
Untrained CNNs Match Backpropagation at V1: A Systematic RSA Comparison of Four Learning Rules Against Human fMRI
Authors: Nils Leutenegger
First: 2026-04-18T06:53:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:48:57+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A central question in computational neuroscience is whether the learning rule used to train a neural network determines how well its internal representations align with those of the human visual cortex. We present a systematic comparison of four learning rules (backpropagation (BP), feedback alignment (FA), predictive coding (PC), and spike-timing-dependent plasticity (STDP)) applied to identical convolutional architectures and evaluated against human fMRI data from the THINGS-fMRI dataset (720 stimuli, 3 subjects) using Representational Similarity Analysis (RSA). All models process stimuli at 224 x 224 resolution; results are averaged across 5 random seeds. Crucially, we include an untrained random-weights baseline that reveals the dominant role of architecture. At V1/V2, the untrained baseline exceeds backpropagation (rho = 0.076 vs. rho = 0.034; Delta-rho = +0.044, p < 0.001), and STDP achieves the highest V1 alignment among trained rules (rho = 0.064). At LOC, only BP reliably exceeds the random baseline (rho = 0.012 vs. -0.005, p < 0.001). At IT, all five conditions converge (rho = 0.008-0.014) with no significant pairwise differences among trained rules (p > 0.05, FDR-corrected). FA consistently produces the lowest alignment at V1, V2, and LOC (rho = 0.012 at V1, below all other conditions). Partial RSA confirms all effects survive pixel-similarity control. Seed variability is small relative to between-rule differences at V1/V2. These results demonstrate that early visual alignment is architecture-driven, learning rules differentiate only at intermediate areas, and all rules converge at the highest levels of the hierarchy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：未经训练的CNN在V1区域的表现可与反向传播相媲美：四种学习规则与人类fMRI数据的系统性RSA比较
计算神经科学中的一个核心问题是，用于训练神经网络的学习规则是否决定了其内部表征与人类视觉皮层表征的对齐程度。我们对四种学习规则（反向传播（BP）、反馈对齐（FA）、预测编码（PC）和突触时间依赖性可塑性（STDP））在相同卷积架构上的应用进行了系统性比较，并通过代表相似性分析（RSA）评估其与THINGS-fMRI数据集（720个刺激，3名受试者）的人类fMRI数据的匹配度。所有模型均以224 x 224分辨率处理刺激，结果基于5个随机种子的平均值。关键的是，我们纳入了一个未经训练的随机权重基线，揭示了架构的主导作用。在V1/V2区域，该基线显著优于反向传播（rho = 0.076 vs. rho = 0.034；Delta-rho = +0.044，p < 0.001），而STDP在训练规则中实现了最高的V1对齐度（rho = 0.064）。在LOC区域，仅BP显著优于随机基线（rho = 0.012 vs. -0.005，p < 0.001）。在IT区域，所有五个条件均收敛（rho = 0.008-0.014），训练规则间无显著差异（p > 0.05，FDR校正）。FA在V1、V2和LOC区域始终产生最低的对齐度（V1处rho = 0.012，低于所有其他条件）。部分RSA验证了所有效应在像素相似性控制下仍显著。V1/V2区域的种子变异性相对于规则间差异较小。这些结果表明，早期视觉对齐由架构驱动，学习规则仅在中间区域产生差异，而在层级最高处所有规则均趋于一致。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether different neural network learning rules produce internal representations that match human visual cortex activity, using fMRI data from the THINGS-fMRI dataset. Four learning rules—backpropagation, feedback alignment, predictive coding, and STDP—were applied to identical CNN architectures and evaluated via Representational Similarity Analysis across visual areas V1, V2, LOC, and IT. An untrained random-weights baseline was included to isolate architectural influence. Results show that at early visual areas (V1/V2), the untrained baseline outperforms backpropagation, while STDP achieves the highest alignment among trained rules. Only backpropagation significantly exceeds the random baseline at LOC, and all learning rules converge with no significant differences at IT. Feedback alignment consistently underperforms across all areas, and effects remain significant after controlling for pixel similarity. The findings suggest that early visual representations are primarily shaped by architecture, while learning rules exert distinct effects only at intermediate levels.
该研究旨在比较四种神经网络学习规则（反向传播、反馈对齐、预测编码和STDP）在卷积网络中生成的视觉表征与人类fMRI数据的匹配程度，覆盖V1、V2、LOC和IT等视觉皮层区域。通过代表相似性分析发现，未训练的随机权重网络在V1表现优于反向传播，表明架构主导早期视觉表征；STDP在训练规则中V1匹配度最高，仅反向传播在LOC显著优于随机基线；在IT区域，所有条件表现趋同，训练规则间无显著差异。反馈对齐在所有区域表现最差，且结果在控制像素相似性后仍成立。研究结论表明，早期视觉表征由架构主导，学习规则仅在中间区域产生差异，高层区域表征趋于一致。
FaaSMoE: A Serverless Framework for Multi-Tenant Mixture-of-Experts Serving
Authors: Minghe Wang, Trever Schirmer, Mohammadreza Malekabbasi, David Bermbach
First: 2026-04-29T16:47:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:47:48+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in the 9th International Workshop on Edge Systems, Analytics and Networking (EdgeSys 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models offer high capacity with efficient inference cost by activating a small subset of expert models per input. However, deploying MoE models requires all experts to reside in memory, creating a gap between the resource used by activated experts and the provisioned resources. This underutilization is further pronounced in multi-tenant scenarios. In this paper, we propose FaaSMoE, a multi-tenant MoE serving architecture built on Function-as-a-Service (FaaS) platforms. FaaSMoE decouples the control and execution planes of MoE by deploying experts as stateless FaaS functions, enabling on-demand and scale-to-zero expert invocation across tenants. FaaSMoE further supports configurable expert granularity within functions, trading off per-expert elasticity for reduced invocation overhead. We implement a prototype with an open-source edge-oriented FaaS platform and evaluate it using Qwen1.5-moe-2.7B under multi-tenant workloads. Compared to a full-model baseline, FaaSMoE uses less than one third of the resources, demonstrating a practical and resource-efficient path towards scalable MoE serving in a multi-tenant environment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FaaSMoE：面向多租户专家混合模型服务的无服务器框架
专家混合（MoE）模型通过每输入激活少量专家模型，实现高容量与高效推理成本。然而，部署MoE模型需所有专家驻留内存，导致激活专家所用资源与分配资源间存在差距，多租户场景下该利用率不足更为显著。本文提出FaaSMoE，一种基于函数即服务（FaaS）平台的多租户MoE服务架构。FaaSMoE通过将专家部署为无状态FaaS函数，解耦MoE的控制与执行平面，实现跨租户按需调用与零扩展专家。FaaSMoE进一步支持函数内可配置的专家粒度，以牺牲单专家弹性换取降低调用开销。我们基于开源边缘导向FaaS平台实现原型，并在多租户负载下以Qwen1.5-moe-2.7B模型评估。相比全模型基线，FaaSMoE资源使用量不足其三分之一，展示了在多租户环境下实现可扩展MoE服务的实用且资源高效路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of deploying MoE models where all experts must reside in memory despite only a subset being activated, FaaSMoE introduces a serverless architecture leveraging FaaS platforms to decouple control and execution planes, deploying experts as stateless functions for on-demand, scale-to-zero invocation across tenants. It further allows configurable granularity within functions to balance elasticity and invocation overhead. Experimental evaluation using Qwen1.5-moe-2.7B under multi-tenant workloads shows FaaSMoE consumes less than one third of the resources compared to a full-model baseline, demonstrating significant resource efficiency without sacrificing performance.
为解决多租户环境下MoE模型激活专家资源利用率低的问题，FaaSMoE提出基于FaaS平台的无服务器架构，将控制与执行平面解耦，专家以无状态函数形式部署，支持按需调用与租户间弹性伸缩，通过配置专家粒度平衡弹性与调用开销。在多租户负载下使用Qwen1.5-moe-2.7B进行实验，结果显示其资源消耗仅为全模型基线的三分之一以下，验证了其在多租户场景下高效、实用的MoE服务潜力。
HealthNLP_Retrievers at ArchEHR-QA 2026: Cascaded LLM Pipeline for Grounded Clinical Question Answering
Authors: Md Biplob Hosen, Md Alomgeer Hussein, Md Akmol Masud, Omar Faruque, Tera L Reynolds, Lujie Karen Chen
First: 2026-04-29T16:47:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:47:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Patient portals now give individuals direct access to their electronic health records (EHRs), yet access alone does not ensure patients understand or act on the complex clinical information contained in these records. The ArchEHR-QA 2026 shared task addresses this challenge by focusing on grounded question answering over EHRs, and this paper presents the system developed by the HealthNLP_Retrievers team for this task. The proposed approach uses a multi-stage cascaded pipeline powered by the Gemini 2.5 Pro large language model to interpret patient-authored questions and retrieve relevant evidence from lengthy clinical notes. Our architecture comprises four integrated modules: (1) a few-shot query reformulation unit which summarizes verbose patient queries; (2) a heuristic-based evidence scorer which ranks clinical sentences to prioritize recall; (3) a grounded response generator which synthesizes professional-caliber answers restricted strictly to identified evidence; and (4) a high-precision many-to-many alignment framework which links generated answers to supporting clinical sentences. This cascaded approach achieved competitive results. Across the individual tracks, the system ranked 1st in question interpretation, 5th in answer generation, 7th in evidence identification, and 9th in answer-evidence alignment. These results show that integrating large language models within a structured multi-stage pipeline improves grounding, precision, and the professional quality of patient-oriented health communication. To support reproducibility, our source code is publicly available in our GitHub repository
中文标题/摘要
标题：HealthNLP_Retrievers在ArchEHR-QA 2026中的表现：用于基于临床记录的问答的级联大语言模型流程
患者门户现已使个人可直接访问其电子健康记录（EHR），但仅提供访问权限并不能确保患者理解或采取这些记录中复杂的临床信息。ArchEHR-QA 2026共享任务旨在解决这一挑战，专注于基于EHR的问答，本文介绍了HealthNLP_Retrievers团队为此任务开发的系统。该方法采用由Gemini 2.5 Pro大语言模型驱动的多阶段级联流程，用于理解患者撰写的提问并从冗长的临床笔记中检索相关证据。我们的架构包含四个集成模块：（1）基于少量示例的查询重写单元，用于总结冗长的患者提问；（2）基于启发式方法的证据评分器，用于对临床句子进行排序以优先召回；（3）基于证据的响应生成器，严格依据识别出的证据合成专业级别的答案；（4）高精度的多对多对齐框架，用于将生成的答案与支持性临床句子关联。该级联方法取得了具有竞争力的结果。在各个独立赛道中，系统在问题理解中排名1st，在答案生成中排名5th，在证据识别中排名7th，在答案-证据对齐中排名9th。这些结果表明，将大语言模型集成到结构化的多阶段流程中，可提升问答的锚定性、精确度及面向患者的健康沟通的专业质量。为支持可复现性，我们的源代码已在GitHub仓库公开。
