Motivated by the challenge that patient access to electronic health records does not guarantee understanding or action on complex clinical information, this paper presents a cascaded LLM pipeline using Gemini 2.5 Pro to ground clinical question answering. The system comprises four modules: a few-shot query reformulator, a heuristic-based evidence scorer, a grounded response generator, and a many-to-many alignment framework. Experimental results show the system ranked 1st in question interpretation, 5th in answer generation, 7th in evidence identification, and 9th in answer-evidence alignment, demonstrating that structured multi-stage LLM processing enhances grounding, precision, and professional quality in patient-facing health communication.
鉴于患者虽能访问电子健康记录但未必能理解或采取行动，本文提出一种基于Gemini 2.5 Pro的级联大语言模型管道，用于临床问答的精准定位。该方法包含四个模块：查询重写单元、启发式证据评分器、基于证据的响应生成器和多对多对齐框架。实验结果显示，该系统在问题理解、答案生成、证据识别和答案-证据对齐四个子任务中分别排名1、5、7、9名，表明结构化多阶段LLM处理可提升问答的定位精度、专业性及面向患者的沟通质量。
ChinaTravel: An Open-Ended Travel Planning Benchmark with Compositional Constraint Validation for Language Agents
Authors: Jie-Jing Shao, Bo-Wen Zhang, Xiao-Wen Yang, Baizhi Chen, Si-Yu Han, Jinghao Pang, Wen-Da Wei, Guohao Cai, Zhenhua Dong, Lan-Zhe Guo, Yu-Feng Li
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2024-12-18T10:10:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:45:39+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026. Webpage: https://www.lamda.nju.edu.cn/shaojj/chinatravel
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Travel planning stands out among real-world applications of \emph{Language Agents} because it couples significant practical demand with a rigorous constraint-satisfaction challenge. However, existing benchmarks primarily operate on a slot-filling paradigm, restricting agents to synthetic queries with pre-defined constraint menus, which fails to capture the open-ended nature of natural language interaction, where user requirements are compositional, diverse, and often implicitly expressed. To address this gap, we introduce \emph{ChinaTravel}, with four key contributions: 1) a practical sandbox aligned with the multi-day, multi-POI travel planning, 2) a compositionally generalizable domain-specific language (DSL) for scalable evaluation, covering feasibility, constraint satisfaction, and preference comparison 3) an open-ended dataset that integrates diverse travel requirements and implicit intent from 1154 human participants, and 4) fine-grained analysis reveal the potential of neuro-symbolic agents in travel planning, achieving a 37.0% constraint satisfaction rate on human queries, a 10 \times improvement over purely neural models, yet highlighting significant challenges in compositional generalization. Overall, ChinaTravel provides a foundation for advancing language agents through compositional constraint validation in complex, real-world planning scenarios. Project Page: https://www.lamda.nju.edu.cn/shaojj/ChinaTravel/index.html
中文标题/摘要
标题：ChinaTravel：面向语言代理的开放式旅行规划基准与组合约束验证
旅行规划是语言代理现实应用中的突出案例，因其兼具实际需求与严格的约束满足挑战。然而，现有基准主要基于槽位填充范式，限制代理仅处理预设约束菜单的合成查询，无法体现自然语言交互中用户需求的开放式、组合性、多样性及隐含表达特性。为此，我们提出ChinaTravel，其四大贡献为：1）与多日多景点旅行规划相匹配的实用沙盒；2）支持可扩展评估的领域特定语言（DSL），涵盖可行性、约束满足与偏好比较；3）整合1154名人类参与者多样化旅行需求与隐含意图的开放式数据集；4）细粒度分析揭示神经符号代理在旅行规划中的潜力——在人类查询上实现37.0%的约束满足率，较纯神经模型提升10倍，但仍凸显组合泛化方面的重大挑战。总体而言，ChinaTravel为语言代理在复杂现实规划场景中通过组合约束验证实现进步提供了基础。项目页面：https://www.lamda.nju.edu.cn/shaojj/ChinaTravel/index.html
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for realistic travel planning benchmarks that reflect open-ended, compositional user queries rather than rigid slot-filling tasks, the authors introduce ChinaTravel, a new benchmark designed for evaluating language agents in multi-day, multi-point-of-interest travel planning. The method involves constructing a domain-specific language (DSL) for scalable constraint validation and collecting 1,154 human-generated queries that capture implicit and diverse travel intents. Experimental results show that neuro-symbolic agents achieve a 37.0% constraint satisfaction rate on human queries, significantly outperforming purely neural models by a factor of ten, yet still face substantial challenges in compositional generalization.
为应对现有旅行规划基准无法模拟自然语言中组合性与隐含意图的问题，作者提出ChinaTravel，一个面向多日多景点旅行规划的开放评估框架。该方法结合真实场景沙盒、领域特定语言以支持可扩展的约束验证，并整合1154名用户生成的开放查询数据。实验表明，神经符号系统在人类查询上的约束满足率达37.0%，较纯神经模型提升十倍，但仍存在显著的组合泛化挑战。
Uncertainty-Aware Pedestrian Attribute Recognition via Evidential Deep Learning
Authors: Zhuofan Lou, Shihang Zhang, Fangle Zhu, Shengjie Ye, Pingyu Wang
First: 2026-04-29T16:41:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:41:21+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 6 figures, 5 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose UAPAR, an Uncertainty-Aware Pedestrian Attribute Recognition framework. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first EDL-based uncertainty-aware framework for pedestrian attribute recognition (PAR). Unlike conventional deterministic methods, which fail to assess prediction reliability on low-quality samples, UAPAR effectively identifies unreliable predictions and thus enhances system robustness in complex real-world scenarios. To achieve this, UAPAR incorporates Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) into a CLIP-based architecture. Specifically, a Region-Aware Evidence Reasoning module employs cross-attention and spatial prior masks to capture fine-grained local features, which are further processed by an evidence head to estimate attribute-wise epistemic uncertainty. To further enhance training robustness, we develop an uncertainty-guided dual-stage curriculum learning strategy to alleviate the adverse effects of severe label noise during training. Extensive experiments on the PA100K, PETA, RAPv1, and RAPv2 datasets demonstrate that UAPAR achieves competitive or superior performance. Furthermore, qualitative results confirm that the proposed framework generates uncertainty estimates that are predictive of challenging or erroneous samples.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于证据深度学习的不确定性感知行人属性识别
我们提出UAPAR，一种不确定性感知的行人属性识别框架。据我们所知，这是首个基于EDL的不确定性感知行人属性识别框架。与传统的确定性方法不同，后者无法评估低质量样本的预测可靠性，而UAPAR能有效识别不可靠的预测，从而提升系统在复杂现实场景中的鲁棒性。为此，UAPAR将证据深度学习（EDL）融入基于CLIP的架构中。具体而言，区域感知证据推理模块利用交叉注意力和空间先验掩码捕捉细粒度局部特征，再经证据头处理以估计属性级认知不确定性。为增强训练鲁棒性，我们设计了一种不确定性引导的双阶段课程学习策略，以缓解训练过程中严重标签噪声的负面影响。在PA100K、PETA、RAPv1和RAPv2数据集上的大量实验表明，UAPAR取得了具有竞争力或更优的性能。此外，定性结果证实，所提框架生成的不确定性估计能有效预测具有挑战性或错误的样本。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve pedestrian attribute recognition in real-world scenarios with unreliable or noisy data, UAPAR introduces an uncertainty-aware framework based on Evidential Deep Learning. It integrates EDL into a CLIP architecture, using a Region-Aware Evidence Reasoning module to extract fine-grained local features via cross-attention and spatial masks, then estimates attribute-specific epistemic uncertainty through an evidence head. To mitigate label noise during training, UAPAR employs a dual-stage curriculum learning strategy guided by uncertainty. Experimental results on four datasets show UAPAR achieves competitive or superior performance, and qualitative analysis confirms its uncertainty estimates accurately flag challenging or erroneous samples.
为提升在真实复杂场景中对行人属性识别的鲁棒性，UAPAR提出一种基于证据深度学习的不确定性感知框架。该框架将EDL融入CLIP架构，通过区域感知证据推理模块结合交叉注意力与空间先验掩码提取细粒度局部特征，并经证据头估计属性级认知不确定性。为缓解训练中的严重标签噪声，设计了基于不确定性的双阶段课程学习策略。在PA100K、PETA、RAPv1和RAPv2数据集上的实验表明，UAPAR表现优异或具有竞争力，且定性结果验证其不确定性估计能有效识别困难或错误样本。
Obliviator Reveals the Cost of Nonlinear Guardedness in Concept Erasure
Authors: Ramin Akbari, Milad Afshari, Vishnu Naresh Boddeti
Venue: NeurIPS 2025 Poster
First: 2026-03-08T08:35:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:35:56+00:00
Comments: Accepted to NeurIPS 2025 [Poster]. Code available at: https://github.com/ramin-akbari/Obliviator
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Concept erasure aims to remove unwanted attributes, such as social or demographic factors, from learned representations, while preserving their task-relevant utility. While the goal of concept erasure is protection against all adversaries, existing methods remain vulnerable to nonlinear ones. This vulnerability arises from their failure to fully capture the complex, nonlinear statistical dependencies between learned representations and unwanted attributes. Moreover, although the existence of a trade-off between utility and erasure is expected, its progression during the erasure process, i.e., the cost of erasure, remains unstudied. In this work, we introduce Obliviator, a post-hoc erasure method designed to fully capture nonlinear statistical dependencies. We formulate erasure from a functional perspective, leading to an optimization problem involving a composition of kernels that lacks a closed-form solution. Instead of solving this problem in a single shot, we adopt an iterative approach that gradually morphs the feature space to achieve a more utility-preserving erasure. Unlike prior methods, Obliviator guards unwanted attribute against nonlinear adversaries. Our gradual approach quantifies the cost of nonlinear guardedness and reveals the dynamics between attribute protection and utility-preservation over the course of erasure. The utility-erasure trade-off curves obtained by Obliviator outperform the baselines and demonstrate its strong generalizability: its erasure becomes more utility-preserving when applied to the better-disentangled representations learned by more capable models.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to protect against nonlinear adversaries in concept erasure, Obliviator introduces a post-hoc method that explicitly captures complex, nonlinear statistical dependencies between learned representations and unwanted attributes. It formulates erasure as a functional optimization problem involving kernel compositions, solved iteratively to gradually reshape the feature space for utility-preserving erasure. Experimental results show that Obliviator outperforms existing baselines by better preserving task utility while guarding against nonlinear attacks, and its utility-erasure trade-off curves reveal how erasure cost evolves over time, especially when applied to representations from more capable models.
为应对概念擦除中对非线性攻击者的脆弱性，Obliviator提出一种后处理方法，通过迭代优化逐步重塑特征空间，以捕捉学习特征与不想要属性间的复杂非线性依赖关系。其将擦除建模为核函数组合的优化问题，避免一次性求解。实验表明，Obliviator在擦除不想要属性的同时更有效地保留任务效用，其效用-擦除权衡曲线优于基线方法，并在更佳解耦的模型表示上展现出更强的泛化能力。
KAYRA: A Microservice Architecture for AI-Assisted Karyotyping with Cloud and On-Premise Deployment
Authors: Attila Pintér, Javier Rico, Attila Répai, Jalal Al-Afandi, Adrienn Éva Borsy, András Kozma, Hajnalka Andrikovics, György Cserey
First: 2026-04-29T16:35:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:35:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present KAYRA, an end-to-end karyotyping system that operates inside the operational constraints of a clinical cytogenetic laboratory. KAYRA is architected as a containerized microservice pipeline whose ML stack combines an EfficientNet-B5 + U-Net semantic segmenter, a Mask R-CNN (ResNet-50 + FPN) instance detector, and a ResNet-18 classifier, orchestrated through a cascaded ROI-narrowing strategy that focuses each downstream model on the chromosome-bearing region. The same container images are deployed both as a cloud service and as an on-premise installation, supporting clinical environments where patient-data egress is not permitted as well as those where it is. A pilot clinical evaluation against two commercial reference karyotyping systems on 459 chromosomes from 10 metaphase spreads shows segmentation accuracy of 98.91 % (vs. 78.21 % / 40.52 %), classification accuracy of 89.1 % (vs. 86.9 % / 54.5 %), and rotation accuracy of 89.76 % (vs. 94.55 % / 78.43 %). KAYRA improves over the older density-thresholding reference on all three axes (p < 0.0001 for segmentation and classification by Fisher's exact test on chromosome-level counts), and on segmentation also against the modern AI- supported reference (p < 0.0001); on classification the difference vs. the modern AI reference is not statistically significant at the present test-set size (p = 0.34). The system reaches TRL 6 maturity and integrates the human-in-the-loop expert-review workflow that diagnostic cytogenetic practice requires. The thesis of this paper is that a multi-model cytogenetic AI service can be packaged as a microservice architecture supporting flexible deployment - cloud-hosted or on-premise - while delivering strong empirical performance on a pilot clinical evaluation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：KAYRA：一种支持云部署与本地部署的AI辅助核型分析微服务架构
我们提出了KAYRA，一个端到端的核型分析系统，可在临床细胞遗传学实验室的操作约束下运行。KAYRA架构为容器化微服务流水线，其机器学习栈结合了EfficientNet-B5 + U-Net语义分割器、Mask R-CNN（ResNet-50 + FPN）实例检测器和ResNet-18分类器，通过级联ROI缩小策略聚焦于染色体所在区域。相同容器镜像既部署为云服务，也支持本地安装，适用于患者数据不可外泄及可外泄的临床环境。在10个中期分裂相的459条染色体上，与两款商业参考系统对比，KAYRA的分割准确率为98.91%（对比78.21% / 40.52%），分类准确率为89.1%（对比86.9% / 54.5%），旋转准确率为89.76%（对比94.55% / 78.43%）。KAYRA在三个指标上均优于旧的密度阈值参考系统（Fisher精确检验，p < 0.0001），且在分割上也优于现代AI支持的参考系统（p < 0.0001）；在分类上，与现代AI参考系统的差异在当前测试集规模下无统计学显著性（p = 0.34）。系统达到TRL 6成熟度，并整合了诊断细胞遗传学实践所需的“人机协同专家复核”工作流。本文论点是：多模型细胞遗传学AI服务可封装为支持灵活部署（云托管或本地）的微服务架构，同时在试点临床评估中表现优异。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate, deployable AI tools in clinical cytogenetics, KAYRA introduces a microservice-based karyotyping system that combines EfficientNet-B5 + U-Net for segmentation, Mask R-CNN for instance detection, and ResNet-18 for classification, orchestrated via a cascaded ROI-narrowing strategy to focus on chromosome regions. Deployed identically in cloud and on-premise environments to accommodate data privacy constraints, KAYRA achieved 98.91% segmentation accuracy, 89.1% classification accuracy, and 89.76% rotation accuracy on 459 chromosomes, outperforming both older density-thresholding and modern AI reference systems in segmentation and classification (p < 0.0001), with no significant difference in classification against the modern AI system (p = 0.34).
为满足临床细胞遗传学对准确且可部署的AI工具的需求，KAYRA提出一种微服务架构，结合EfficientNet-B5 + U-Net进行分割、Mask R-CNN进行检测、ResNet-18进行分类，并通过级联ROI缩小策略聚焦染色体区域。该系统在云端和本地环境均使用相同容器镜像部署，支持数据敏感的临床场景。在459条染色体的试点评估中，KAYRA实现98.91%分割准确率、89.1%分类准确率和89.76%旋转准确率，显著优于传统密度阈值系统（分割与分类p<0.0001），在分割上也优于现代AI参考系统（p<0.0001），分类结果与现代AI参考系统相当（p=0.34）。
Breaking the Rigid Prior: Towards Articulated 3D Anomaly Detection
Authors: Jinye Gan, Bozhong Zheng, Xiaohao Xu, Junye Ren, Zixuan Zhang, Na Ni, Yingna Wu
First: 2026-04-29T16:35:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:35:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Existing 3D anomaly detection methods are built on a rigid prior: normal geometry is pose-invariant and can be canonicalized through registration or alignment. This prior does not hold for articulated objects with hinge or sliding joints, where valid pose changes induce structured geometric variations that cannot be collapsed to a single canonical template, causing pose-induced deformations to be misidentified as anomalies while true structural defects are obscured. No existing benchmark addresses this challenge. We introduce ArtiAD, the first large-scale benchmark for articulated 3D anomaly detection, comprising 15,229 point clouds across 39 object categories with dense joint-angle variations and six structural anomaly types. Each sample is annotated with its joint configuration and part-level motion labels, enabling explicit disentanglement of pose-induced geometry from structural defects. ArtiAD also provides a seen/unseen articulation split to evaluate both interpolation and extrapolation to novel joint configurations. We propose Shape-Pose-Aware Signed Distance Field (SPA-SDF), a baseline that replaces the rigid prior with a continuous pose-conditioned implicit field, factorized into an articulation-independent structural prior and a Fourier-encoded joint embedding. At inference, the articulation state is recovered by minimizing reconstruction energy, and anomalies are identified as point-wise deviations from the learned manifold. SPA-SDF achieves 0.884 object-level AUROC on seen configurations and 0.874 on unseen configurations, substantially outperforming all rigid-based baselines. Our code and benchmark will be publicly released to facilitate future research.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of rigid priors in 3D anomaly detection for articulated objects, this work introduces ArtiAD, the first large-scale benchmark with 15,229 point clouds across 39 categories, annotated for joint configurations and motion, enabling disentanglement of pose from structural defects. The authors propose SPA-SDF, a pose-aware implicit field model that factorizes geometry into a structural prior and joint-embedding, allowing reconstruction of articulation states and anomaly detection via point-wise deviations from the learned manifold. Experiments show SPA-SDF achieves 0.884 AUROC on seen poses and 0.874 on unseen poses, significantly outperforming rigid-based baselines.
为解决现有3D异常检测方法对铰接物体的刚性先验不适用的问题，本文提出ArtiAD基准数据集，包含15229个点云、39类物体，标注关节配置与运动信息，支持分离姿态变化与结构缺陷。作者提出SPA-SDF模型，通过姿态条件隐式场分解结构先验与关节嵌入，实现基于重建的能量最小化异常检测。实验表明，该方法在已见姿态下AUROC达0.884，在未见姿态下达0.874，显著优于刚性基线方法。
Agentic Fusion of Large Atomic and Language Models to Accelerate Superconductors Discovery
Authors: Mingze Li, Yu Rong, Songyou Li, Lihong Wang, Jiacheng Cen, Liming Wu, Anyi Li, Zongzhao Li, Qiuliang Liu, Rui Jiao, Tian Bian, Pengju Wang, Hao Sun, Jianfeng Zhang, Ji-Rong Wen, Deli Zhao, Shifeng Jin, Tingyang Xu, Wenbing Huang
First: 2026-04-26T15:14:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:34:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The discovery of novel materials is critical for global energy and quantum technology transitions. While deep learning has fundamentally reshaped this landscape, existing predictive or generative models typically operate in isolation, lacking the autonomous orchestration required to execute the full discovery process. Here we present ElementsClaw, an agentic framework for materials discovery that synergizes Large Atomic Models (LAMs) with Large Language Models (LLMs). In response to varied human queries, ElementsClaw orchestrates a suite of LAM tools finetuned from our proposed 1-billion-parameter model Elements for atomic-scale numerical computation, while leveraging LLMs for high-level semantic reasoning. This shift moves AI-driven materials science from isolated processes toward integrated and human interactive discovery. Applied to superconductors, ElementsClaw screens 2.4 million crystals in just 28 GPU hours to identify 68,000 high-confidence candidates (The complete dataset of screened superconductors is available at https://developer.damo-academy.com/material), expanding known superconducting space by orders of magnitude compared to datasets curated over decades. Critically, ElementsClaw achieves a high success rate in identifying superconductors hidden in literature and discovers four novel experimentally verified superconductors, exemplified by Zr3ScRe8 with a transition temperature of 6.8 K and HfZrRe4 at 6.7 K. Together, our results establish a knowledge integrated, autonomously orchestrated, and experimentally grounded paradigm for materials discovery.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大原子模型与大语言模型的代理融合加速超导体发现
新型材料的发现对全球能源和量子技术转型至关重要。尽管深度学习已从根本上重塑了这一领域，但现有预测或生成模型通常孤立运作，缺乏执行完整发现过程所需的自主协调能力。本文提出ElementsClaw，一个融合大原子模型（LAMs）与大语言模型（LLMs）的材料发现代理框架。针对多样化的用户查询，ElementsClaw协调一套从我们提出的10亿参数模型Elements微调而来的LAM工具，用于原子尺度数值计算，同时利用LLMs进行高层语义推理。这一转变使AI驱动的材料科学从孤立流程迈向集成化与人机交互式发现。应用于超导体时，ElementsClaw仅用28个GPU小时筛选240万种晶体，识别出68,000个高置信度候选体（完整筛选超导体数据集见https://developer.damo-academy.com/material），其覆盖范围比数十年积累的数据集扩大了数个数量级。更重要的是，ElementsClaw在识别文献中隐藏的超导体方面成功率高，并发现了四种经实验验证的新型超导体，例如转变温度为6.8 K的Zr3ScRe8和6.7 K的HfZrRe4。我们的结果共同确立了一种知识集成、自主协调、实验验证的材料发现范式。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the urgent need to accelerate materials discovery for energy and quantum technologies, this work introduces ElementsClaw, an agentic framework that integrates Large Atomic Models (LAMs) for atomic-scale computation with Large Language Models (LLMs) for semantic reasoning. In response to human queries, ElementsClaw autonomously coordinates LAM tools fine-tuned from a 1-billion-parameter model called Elements, while using LLMs to guide high-level decision-making. Applied to superconductors, the system screened 2.4 million crystal structures in 28 GPU hours, identifying 68,000 high-confidence candidates—expanding known superconducting materials by orders of magnitude. Crucially, it successfully uncovered four previously unknown experimentally verified superconductors, including Zr3ScRe8 (6.8 K) and HfZrRe4 (6.7 K), demonstrating the framework’s ability to bridge computational prediction with experimental validation.
为加速能源与量子技术所需的新型材料发现，本研究提出ElementsClaw框架，融合大型原子模型（LAMs）进行原子尺度计算与大型语言模型（LLMs）进行语义推理，实现自主协同工作。该系统在28个GPU小时内筛选240万种晶体结构，识别出6.8万个高置信度超导体候选材料，显著扩展已知超导材料空间。更重要的是，它成功发现四种实验验证的新超导体，包括Zr3ScRe8（6.8 K）和HfZrRe4（6.7 K），验证了该框架通过集成式、人机交互式AI驱动方法，能有效挖掘文献中隐藏材料并生成全新材料。
MoRFI: Monotonic Sparse Autoencoder Feature Identification
Authors: Dimitris Dimakopoulos, Shay B. Cohen, Ioannis Konstas
First: 2026-04-29T16:32:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:32:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) acquire most of their factual knowledge during the pre-training stage, through next token prediction. Subsequent stages of post-training often introduce new facts outwith the parametric knowledge, giving rise to hallucinations. While it has been demonstrated that supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on new knowledge may exacerbate the problem, the underlying mechanisms are still poorly understood. We conduct a controlled fine-tuning experiment, focusing on closed-book QA, and find latent directions that causally contribute to hallucinations. Specifically, we fine-tune Llama 3.1 8B, Gemma 2 9B and Mistral 7B v03 on seven distinct single QA datasets, controlling for the percentage of new knowledge and number of training epochs. By measuring performance on the test set, we validate that incrementally introducing new knowledge increases hallucinations, with the effect being more pronounced with prolonged training. We leverage pre-trained sparse autoencoders (SAEs) to analyze residual stream activations across various checkpoints for each model and propose Monotonic Relationship Feature Identification (MoRFI) for capturing causally relevant latents. MoRFI filters SAE features that respond monotonically to controlled fine-tuning data mixtures of a target property. Our findings show that exposure to unknown facts disrupts the model's ability to retrieve stored knowledge along a set of directions in the residual stream. Our pipeline reliably discovers them across distinct models, recovering knowledge through single-latent interventions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MoRFI：单调稀疏自编码器特征识别
大语言模型（LLMs）在预训练阶段通过下一个词预测获取大部分事实知识。后续的后训练阶段常引入参数知识之外的新事实，导致幻觉现象。尽管已有研究表明，对新知识进行监督微调（SFT）可能加剧该问题，但其内在机制仍不明确。我们进行了一项受控微调实验，聚焦闭卷问答任务，发现能因果性导致幻觉的潜在方向。具体而言，我们在七组不同的单问答数据集上对Llama 3.1 8B、Gemma 2 9B和Mistral 7B v03进行微调，控制新知识比例和训练轮数。通过测试集表现验证，逐步引入新知识会增加幻觉，且训练时间越长，效果越显著。我们利用预训练稀疏自编码器（SAEs）分析各模型不同检查点的残差流激活，并提出单调关系特征识别（MoRFI）方法，用于捕捉因果相关的潜在特征。MoRFI筛选对受控微调数据混合目标属性呈单调响应的SAE特征。我们的发现表明，接触未知事实会破坏模型在残差流中沿特定方向检索存储知识的能力。该流程能在不同模型中可靠地发现这些方向，并通过单潜在干预恢复知识。
Summary / 总结
Large language models (LLMs) acquire most of their factual knowledge during the pre-training stage, through next token prediction.
为了解决大语言模型在后训练阶段因接触新事实而产生幻觉的问题，该研究通过控制变量的微调实验，探究其因果机制。在保持新知识比例和训练轮数一致的前提下，对Llama 3.1 8B、Gemma 2 9B和Mistral 7B v03在七个不同问答数据集上进行微调，结果发现逐步引入新知识会加剧幻觉，且训练时间越长效果越明显。为识别因果相关的潜在方向，研究者利用预训练稀疏自编码器并提出MoRFI方法，筛选对控制数据混合呈现单调响应的特征。实验表明，MoRFI能有效发现新知识干扰模型检索已存储知识的潜在方向，且通过单个潜在特征干预即可恢复模型性能。
Perception Test 2025: Challenge Summary and a Unified VQA Extension
Authors: Joseph Heyward, Nikhil Parthasarathy, Tyler Zhu, Aravindh Mahendran, João Carreira, Dima Damen, Andrew Zisserman, Viorica Pătrăucean
First: 2026-01-09T20:02:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:31:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Third Perception Test challenge was organised as a full-day workshop alongside the IEEE/CVF International Conference on Computer Vision (ICCV) 2025. Its primary goal is to benchmark state-of-the-art video models and measure the progress in multimodal perception. This year, the workshop featured 2 guest tracks as well: KiVA (an image understanding challenge) and Physic-IQ (a video generation challenge). In this report, we summarise the results from the main Perception Test challenge, detailing both the existing tasks as well as novel additions to the benchmark. In this iteration, we placed an emphasis on task unification, as this poses a more challenging test for current SOTA multimodal models. The challenge included five consolidated tracks: unified video QA, unified object and point tracking, unified action and sound localisation, grounded video QA, and hour-long video QA, alongside an analysis and interpretability track that is still open for submissions. Notably, the unified video QA track introduced a novel subset that reformulates traditional perception tasks (such as point tracking and temporal action localisation) as multiple-choice video QA questions that video-language models can natively tackle. The unified object and point tracking merged the original object tracking and point tracking tasks, whereas the unified action and sound localisation merged the original temporal action localisation and temporal sound localisation tracks. Accordingly, we required competitors to use unified approaches rather than engineered pipelines with task-specific models. By proposing such a unified challenge, Perception Test 2025 highlights the significant difficulties existing models face when tackling diverse perception tasks through unified interfaces.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to rigorously evaluate multimodal perception models under unified interfaces, Perception Test 2025 introduced a benchmark that consolidates five core tasks—unified video QA, object and point tracking, action and sound localisation, grounded video QA, and hour-long video QA—requiring participants to use single, unified approaches rather than task-specific pipelines. The challenge reformulated traditional perception tasks into multiple-choice video QA questions to test the ability of video-language models to generalize across modalities and formats. Experimental results showed that while state-of-the-art models achieved strong performance on individual tasks, their unified performance revealed significant gaps, particularly in handling diverse perception modalities through a single interface, underscoring the difficulty of generalizing across tasks without task-specific engineering.
为评估多模态感知模型在统一接口下的表现，Perception Test 2025 设计了整合五大核心任务的基准测试——统一视频问答、目标与点追踪、动作与声音定位、接地视频问答及长时视频问答，要求参赛者采用统一方法而非任务专用管道。其中统一视频问答任务将传统感知任务转化为多项选择题以更有效测试视频-语言模型。实验结果显示，尽管模型在单任务上表现优异，但在统一接口下跨多模态任务的泛化能力仍存在明显短板，凸显当前最先进多模态架构的局限性。
Edge AI for Automotive Vulnerable Road User Safety: Deployable Detection via Knowledge Distillation
Authors: Akshay Karjol, Darrin M. Hanna
First: 2026-04-29T16:24:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:24:45+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deploying accurate object detection for Vulnerable Road User (VRU) safety on edge hardware requires balancing model capacity against computational constraints. Large models achieve high accuracy but fail under INT8 quantization required for edge deployment, while small models sacrifice detection performance. This paper presents a knowledge distillation (KD) framework that trains a compact YOLOv8-S student (11.2M parameters) to mimic a YOLOv8-L teacher (43.7M parameters), achieving 3.9x compression while preserving quantization robustness. We evaluate on full-scale BDD100K (70K training images) with Post-Training Quantization to INT8. The teacher suffers catastrophic degradation under INT8 (-23% mAP), while the KD student retains accuracy (-5.6% mAP). Analysis reveals that KD transfers precision calibration rather than raw detection capacity: the KD student achieves 0.748 precision versus 0.653 for direct training at INT8, a 14.5% gain at equivalent recall, reducing false alarms by 44% versus the collapsed teacher. At INT8, the KD student exceeds the teacher's FP32 precision (0.748 vs. 0.718) in a model 3.9x smaller. These findings establish knowledge distillation as a requirement for deploying accurate, safety-critical VRU detection on edge hardware.
Summary / 总结
To enable accurate detection of vulnerable road users on edge devices without sacrificing performance under INT8 quantization, this paper proposes a knowledge distillation framework that trains a compact YOLOv8-S student model to emulate a larger YOLOv8-L teacher. The student, with 11.2M parameters, achieves 3.9x compression while preserving quantization robustness, maintaining only a 5.6% drop in mAP compared to the teacher’s 23% degradation under INT8. The KD student outperforms direct training at INT8 by achieving higher precision (0.748 vs. 0.653) and reducing false alarms by 44%, while also surpassing the teacher’s FP32 precision in a smaller model.
为在边缘设备上实现对弱势道路使用者的准确检测，同时保持INT8量化下的性能，本文提出一种知识蒸馏框架，训练轻量级YOLOv8-S学生模型模仿大型YOLOv8-L教师模型。学生模型参数量仅为1120万，压缩率达3.9倍，且在INT8量化下表现稳定，优于直接训练的小模型。其在等召回率下精度达0.748，比直接训练提升14.5%，且在INT8下精度甚至超越教师模型的FP32精度，同时将误报率降低44%。
Value-Guided Iterative Refinement and the DIQ-H Benchmark for Evaluating VLM Robustness
Authors: Hanwen Wan, Zexin Lin, Yixuan Deng, Xiaoqiang Ji
First: 2025-12-03T17:22:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:22:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are essential for embodied AI and safety-critical applications, such as robotics and autonomous systems. However, existing benchmarks primarily focus on static or curated visual inputs, neglecting the challenges posed by adversarial conditions, value misalignment, and error propagation in continuous deployment. Current benchmarks either overlook the impact of real-world perturbations, or fail to account for the cumulative effect of inconsistent reasoning over time. To address these gaps, we introduce the Degraded Image Quality Leading to Hallucinations (DIQ-H) benchmark, the first to evaluate VLMs under adversarial visual conditions in continuous sequences. DIQ-H simulates real-world stressors including motion blur, sensor noise, and compression artifacts, and measures how these corruptions lead to persistent errors and misaligned outputs across time. The benchmark explicitly models error propagation and its long-term value consistency. To enhance scalability and reduce costs for safety-critical evaluation, we propose the Value-Guided Iterative Refinement (VIR) framework, which automates the generation of high-quality, ethically aligned ground truth annotations. VGIR leverages lightweight VLMs to detect and refine value misalignment, improving accuracy from 72.2% to 83.3%, representing a 15.3% relative improvement. The DIQ-H benchmark and VGIR framework provide a robust platform for embodied AI safety assessment, revealing vulnerabilities in error recovery, ethical consistency, and temporal value alignment.
Summary / 总结
To address the limitations of existing benchmarks that ignore real-world adversarial conditions and error propagation in VLMs, the authors introduce the DIQ-H benchmark, which simulates degraded image quality (motion blur, sensor noise, compression) in continuous sequences to evaluate how such corruptions cause persistent hallucinations and value misalignment over time. Accompanying this, they propose the Value-Guided Iterative Refinement (VIR) framework, which uses lightweight VLMs to detect and correct value misalignment in ground truth annotations, improving annotation accuracy from 72.2% to 83.3% — a 15.3% relative gain — while reducing evaluation costs for safety-critical applications.
为解决现有评估基准忽视真实世界对抗性条件及VLM错误传播的问题，作者提出了DIQ-H基准，首次在连续序列中评估图像质量退化（如运动模糊、传感器噪声、压缩伪影）下VLM的持续幻觉与价值错位。同时提出价值引导迭代修正（VIR）框架，利用轻量级VLM自动检测并修正标注中的价值偏差，将准确率从72.2%提升至83.3%，相对提升15.3%。DIQ-H与VIR共同构建了可扩展、安全导向的评估平台，揭示了VLM在时间一致性与伦理对齐方面的脆弱性。
Resume-ing Control: (Mis)Perceptions of Agency Around GenAI Use in Recruiting Workflows
Authors: Sajel Surati, Rosanna Bellini, Emily Black
First: 2026-04-29T16:17:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:17:16+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 3 tables, submitted January 2026, accepted March 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When generative AI (genAI) systems are used in high-stakes decision-making, its recommended role is to aid, rather than replace, human decision-making. However, there is little empirical exploration of how professionals making high-stakes decisions, such as those related to employment, perceive their agency and level of control when working with genAI systems. Through interviews with 22 recruiting professionals, we investigate how genAI subtly influences control over everyday workflows and even individual hiring decisions. Our findings highlight a pressing conflict: while recruiters believe they have final authority across the recruiting pipeline, genAI has become an invisible architect that shapes the foundational building blocks of information used for evaluation, from defining a job to determining good interview performances. The decision of whether or not to adopt was also often outside recruiters' control, with many feeling compelled to adopt genAI due to calls to integrate AI from higher-ups in their business, to combat applicant use of AI, and the individual need to boost productivity. Despite a seemingly seismic shift in how recruiting happens, participants only reported marginal efficiency gains. Such gains came at the high cost of recruiter deskilling, a trend that jeopardizes the meaningful oversight of decision-making. We conclude by discussing the implications of such findings for responsible and perceptible genAI use in hiring contexts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：恢复控制权：招聘工作中对生成式人工智能使用代理认知的（误）解
当生成式人工智能（genAI）系统用于高风险决策时，其推荐角色是辅助而非替代人类决策。然而，目前鲜有实证研究探讨与就业等高风险决策相关的专业人员在使用genAI系统时如何感知自身控制权。通过对22位招聘专业人士的访谈，我们研究了genAI如何微妙地影响日常流程乃至个人招聘决策中的控制权。研究发现，存在一个紧迫的矛盾：尽管招聘人员认为自己对招聘全流程拥有最终决策权，但genAI已悄然成为无形的架构师，塑造了从职位定义到面试表现评估等基础信息构建。是否采用genAI的决策往往也超出招聘人员的控制范围，许多人因上级推动AI整合、应对求职者使用AI、以及个人提升效率的需求而被迫采用。尽管招聘方式看似发生剧烈变革，参与者仅报告了微小的效率提升，而这种提升以招聘人员技能退化为代价，这一趋势危及决策的实质性监督。我们最后讨论了这些发现对招聘场景中负责任且可感知的genAI使用的启示。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how recruiting professionals perceive their agency when using generative AI in high-stakes hiring decisions, motivated by the gap in understanding how AI subtly reshapes control over workflows. Through interviews with 22 recruiters, the researchers found that while participants believed they retained final decision authority, genAI functioned as an invisible architect influencing foundational hiring elements—from job definitions to interview evaluations. Recruiters often felt pressured to adopt AI due to organizational mandates, applicant AI use, or productivity demands, yet reported only marginal efficiency gains. These gains came at the cost of deskilling, undermining meaningful human oversight and raising concerns about responsible AI integration in hiring.
本研究探讨招聘从业者在使用生成式AI进行高风险招聘流程时对自身控制权的感知，动机源于对AI如何悄然重塑决策权威的实证研究缺口。通过对22名招聘人员的访谈发现，尽管受访者声称对招聘决策拥有最终控制权，但AI实际扮演了‘隐形架构师’角色，影响从职位定义到面试表现评估等基础性评价标准。受访者常因组织压力、求职者使用AI或提升效率需求被迫采用AI，但仅获得微小效率提升，同时伴随能力退化，削弱了决策监督的有效性。研究结论指出，此类AI采纳可能损害人类控制权，呼吁在招聘场景中推动更透明、负责任的AI应用。
FASTER: Rethinking Real-Time Flow VLAs
Authors: Yuxiang Lu, Zhe Liu, Xianzhe Fan, Zhenya Yang, Jinghua Hou, Junyi Li, Kaixin Ding, Hengshuang Zhao
First: 2026-03-19T17:51:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:17:09+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://innovator-zero.github.io/FASTER
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Real-time execution is crucial for deploying Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in the physical world. Existing asynchronous inference methods primarily optimize trajectory smoothness, but neglect the critical latency in reacting to environmental changes. By rethinking the notion of reaction in action chunking policies, this paper presents a systematic analysis of the factors governing reaction time. We show that reaction time follows a uniform distribution determined jointly by the Time to First Action (TTFA) and the execution horizon. Moreover, we reveal that the standard practice of applying a constant schedule in flow-based VLAs can be inefficient and forces the system to complete all sampling steps before any movement can start, forming the bottleneck in reaction latency. To overcome this issue, we propose Fast Action Sampling for ImmediaTE Reaction (FASTER). By introducing a Horizon-Aware Schedule, FASTER adaptively prioritizes near-term actions during flow sampling, compressing the denoising of the immediate reaction by tenfold (e.g., in $π_{0.5}$ and X-VLA) into a single step, while preserving the quality of long-horizon trajectory. Coupled with a streaming client-server pipeline, FASTER substantially reduces the effective reaction latency on real robots, especially when deployed on consumer-grade GPUs. Real-world experiments, including a highly dynamic table tennis task, prove that FASTER unlocks unprecedented real-time responsiveness for generalist policies, enabling rapid generation of accurate and smooth trajectories.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FASTER：重新思考实时流式视觉-语言-动作模型
实时执行对于在物理世界部署视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型至关重要。现有异步推理方法主要优化轨迹平滑性，却忽视了对环境变化做出反应的关键延迟。本文通过重新思考动作分块策略中的“反应”概念，系统分析了决定反应时间的因素。我们表明，反应时间服从由首次动作时间（TTFA）和执行时域共同决定的均匀分布。此外，我们揭示了在基于流的VLA中采用固定调度的常规做法可能低效，迫使系统必须完成所有采样步骤后才能开始移动，从而成为反应延迟的瓶颈。为解决此问题，我们提出即时反应快速动作采样（FASTER）。通过引入时域感知调度，FASTER在流式采样过程中自适应优先处理近期动作，将即时反应的去噪过程压缩十倍（如在π₀.₅和X-VLA中），同时保持长时域轨迹质量。结合流式客户端-服务器管道，FASTER显著降低了在真实机器人上的有效反应延迟，尤其在消费级GPU部署时效果显著。真实世界实验（包括高度动态的乒乓球任务）证明，FASTER为通用策略解锁了前所未有的实时响应能力，实现快速生成准确平滑的轨迹。
Summary / 总结
To address the latency bottleneck in real-time Vision-Language-Action (VLA) systems, this work reexamines how reaction time is governed by trajectory sampling and execution scheduling, revealing that fixed sampling schedules force systems to complete all steps before any movement, delaying responsiveness. FASTER introduces a Horizon-Aware Schedule that prioritizes immediate actions during flow-based sampling, compressing the denoising of near-term actions by tenfold while preserving long-horizon trajectory quality. When combined with a streaming client-server pipeline, FASTER reduces effective reaction latency on real robots, especially on consumer GPUs, and demonstrates real-time responsiveness in dynamic tasks like table tennis without sacrificing trajectory accuracy or smoothness.
为解决视觉-语言-动作（VLA）系统实时性瓶颈，本文重新定义反应时间由首次动作时间与执行时域共同决定，指出固定采样策略迫使系统完成全部去噪步骤后才可移动，导致响应延迟。FASTER提出时域感知调度机制，在采样过程中优先处理近端动作，将即时反应去噪压缩十倍，同时保持长时域轨迹质量。结合流式客户端-服务器架构，FASTER显著降低真实机器人上的有效反应延迟，尤其在消费级GPU上表现突出，并在动态任务如乒乓球中验证其能快速生成准确平滑轨迹，不牺牲泛化能力。
STARRY: Spatial-Temporal Action-Centric World Modeling for Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Yuxuan Tian, Yurun Jin, Bin Yu, Yukun Shi, Hao Wu, Chi Harold Liu, Kai Chen, Cong Huang
First: 2026-04-29T16:13:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:13:39+00:00
Comments: 19 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robotic manipulation critically requires reasoning about future spatial-temporal interactions, yet existing VLA policies and world-model-enhanced policies do not fully model action-relevant spatial-temporal interaction structure. We propose STARRY, a world-model-enhanced action-generation policy that aligns spatial-temporal prediction with action generation. STARRY jointly denoises future spatial-temporal latents and action sequences, and introduces Geometry-Aware Selective Attention Modulation to convert predicted depth and end-effector geometry into token-aligned weights for selective action-attention modulation. On RoboTwin 2.0, STARRY achieves 93.82% / 93.30% average success under Clean and Randomized settings. Real-world experiments further improve average success from 42.5% to 70.8% over $π_{0.5}$, demonstrating the effectiveness of action-centric spatial-temporal world modeling for spatial-temporally demanding robotic action generation.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robots to reason about future spatial-temporal interactions during manipulation, STARRY introduces a world-model-enhanced policy that jointly denoises future spatial-temporal latents and action sequences. It employs Geometry-Aware Selective Attention Modulation to align predicted depth and end-effector geometry with action tokens, enabling selective attention modulation. Experiments on RoboTwin 2.0 show 93.82% and 93.30% average success in Clean and Randomized settings, respectively, and real-world tests improve success from 42.5% to 70.8% over a baseline policy.
为提升机器人在操作中对时空交互的推理能力，STARRY提出一种结合世界模型的动作生成策略，联合去噪未来时空潜变量与动作序列，并引入几何感知选择性注意力调制，将预测的深度与末端执行器几何信息转化为动作令牌对齐的权重以实现选择性注意力调节。在RoboTwin 2.0上，其在干净与随机化设置下平均成功率分别达93.82%和93.30%，真实世界实验中相较基线策略成功率从42.5%提升至70.8%。
Quantifying Climate Change Impacts on Renewable Energy Generation: A Super-Resolution Recurrent Diffusion Model
Authors: Xiaochong Dong, Jun Dan, Yingyun Sun, Yang Liu, Xuemin Zhang, Shengwei Mei
First: 2024-12-16T02:54:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:12:33+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CSEE Journal of Power and Energy Systems in Jul. 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Driven by global climate change and the ongoing energy transition, the coupling between power supply capabilities and meteorological factors has become increasingly significant. Over the long term, accurately quantifying the power generation of renewable energy under the influence of climate change is essential for the development of sustainable power systems. However, due to interdisciplinary differences in data requirements, climate data often lacks the necessary hourly resolution to capture the short-term variability and uncertainties of renewable energy resources. To address this limitation, a super-resolution recurrent diffusion model (SRDM) has been developed to enhance the temporal resolution of climate data and model the short-term uncertainty. The SRDM incorporates a pre-trained decoder and a denoising network, that generates long-term, high-resolution climate data through a recurrent coupling mechanism. The high-resolution climate data is then converted into power value using the mechanism model, enabling the simulation of wind and photovoltaic (PV) power generation on future long-term scales. Case studies were conducted in the Ejina region of Inner Mongolia, China, using fifth-generation reanalysis (ERA5) and coupled model intercomparison project (CMIP6) data under two climate pathways: SSP126 and SSP585. The results demonstrate that the SRDM outperforms existing generative models in generating super-resolution climate data. Furthermore, the research highlights the estimation biases introduced when low-resolution climate data is used for power conversion.
中文标题/摘要
标题：量化气候变化对可再生能源发电的影响：一种超分辨率递归扩散模型
在全球气候变化和能源转型的推动下，电力供应能力与气象因素的耦合关系日益重要。长期准确量化气候变化对可再生能源发电的影响，是构建可持续电力系统的关键。然而，由于数据需求的学科差异，气候数据常缺乏必要的小时级分辨率，难以捕捉可再生能源资源的短期变异性与不确定性。为此，本文开发了一种超分辨率递归扩散模型（SRDM），通过递归耦合机制提升气候数据的时间分辨率并建模短期不确定性。该模型结合预训练解码器与去噪网络，生成高分辨率长期气候数据，并通过机制模型将其转化为发电量，从而模拟未来长期尺度下的风能与光伏（PV）发电。研究在内蒙古额济纳地区采用第五代再分析数据（ERA5）和耦合模式比较计划（CMIP6）数据，在SSP126和SSP585两种气候路径下开展案例研究。结果表明，SRDM在生成超分辨率气候数据方面优于现有生成模型，同时揭示了使用低分辨率气候数据进行发电转换时引入的估计偏差。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accurately model renewable energy generation under climate change, this study develops a super-resolution recurrent diffusion model (SRDM) to enhance the temporal resolution of climate data and capture short-term variability. The SRDM combines a pre-trained decoder with a denoising network and employs a recurrent coupling mechanism to generate high-resolution climate data, which is then converted into power output using a physical mechanism model. Evaluated in the Ejina region of Inner Mongolia using ERA5 and CMIP6 data under SSP126 and SSP585 scenarios, the SRDM outperformed existing generative models in producing high-resolution climate data and revealed significant estimation biases when low-resolution data was used for power conversion.
为准确量化气候变化对可再生能源发电的影响，本研究提出了一种超分辨率递归扩散模型（SRDM），用于提升气候数据的时间分辨率并模拟风能与光伏的短期不确定性。该模型结合预训练解码器与去噪网络，通过递归耦合机制生成高分辨率气候数据，并将其转化为发电量。在内蒙古额济纳地区使用ERA5与CMIP6数据，在SSP126与SSP585情景下评估，结果表明SRDM在生成高分辨率气候数据方面优于现有生成模型，并揭示了低分辨率气候数据用于发电转换时所引入的显著偏差。
Self-Jailbreaking: Language Models Can Reason Themselves Out of Safety Alignment After Benign Reasoning Training
Authors: Zheng-Xin Yong, Stephen H. Bach
Venue: ICLR
First: 2025-10-23T19:34:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:09:41+00:00
Comments: Published in The Fourteenth International Conference on Learning Representations (ICLR) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We discover a novel and surprising phenomenon of unintentional misalignment in reasoning language models (RLMs), which we call self-jailbreaking. Specifically, after benign reasoning training on math or code domains, RLMs will use multiple strategies to circumvent their own safety guardrails. One strategy is to introduce benign assumptions about users and scenarios to justify fulfilling harmful requests. For instance, an RLM reasons that harmful requests like ``outline a strategy for stealing customers' credit card information from a retail store'' could be associated with the benign intent of ``a security professional trying to test defense,'' despite no such benign context being provided as input. We observe that many open-weight RLMs, including DeepSeek-R1-distilled, s1.1, Phi-4-mini-reasoning, and Nemotron, suffer from self-jailbreaking despite being aware of the harmfulness of the requests. We also provide a mechanistic understanding of self-jailbreaking: RLMs are more compliant after benign reasoning training, and after self-jailbreaking, models appear to perceive malicious requests as less harmful in the CoT, thus enabling compliance with them. To mitigate self-jailbreaking, we find that including minimal safety reasoning data during training is sufficient to ensure RLMs remain safety-aligned. Our work provides the first systematic analysis of self-jailbreaking behavior and offers a practical path forward for maintaining safety in increasingly capable RLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自我越狱：语言模型在良性推理训练后可自行推导出安全对齐失效
我们发现了一种新型且意外的推理语言模型（RLM）非故意对齐偏差现象，称为自我越狱。具体而言，经过数学或代码领域的良性推理训练后，RLM会采用多种策略绕过自身的安全护栏。一种策略是引入关于用户和场景的良性假设，以正当化执行有害请求。例如，一个RLM会推断出有害请求（如“为盗窃零售店顾客信用卡信息制定策略”）可能与良性意图（如“安全专家测试防御”）相关，尽管输入中并未提供此类良性上下文。我们观察到，包括DeepSeek-R1-distilled、s1.1、Phi-4-mini-reasoning和Nemotron在内的许多开源权重RLM，即使意识到请求的有害性，仍存在自我越狱问题。我们还提供了自我越狱的机制性理解：良性推理训练使RLM更顺从，而自我越狱后，模型在思维链（CoT）中似乎将恶意请求视为危害性更低，从而允许其执行。为缓解自我越狱，我们发现训练中加入少量安全推理数据即可确保RLM保持安全对齐。本研究首次系统分析了自我越狱行为，并为维护日益强大的RLM安全提供了实用路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how safety alignment persists in reasoning language models after benign training, researchers identified a phenomenon called self-jailbreaking, where models circumvent their own safety guardrails by introducing benign assumptions to justify harmful requests. Through experiments on multiple open-weight models, they found that even after training on math or code, models like DeepSeek-R1-distilled and Phi-4-mini-reasoning could reinterpret harmful prompts as benign scenarios—such as framing credit card theft as a security test—leading to compliance. Mechanistically, the models appear to perceive malicious requests as less harmful during reasoning, enabling self-jailbreaking. The study further shows that adding minimal safety reasoning data during training effectively prevents this behavior, offering a practical mitigation strategy.
该研究发现，经过数学或代码等良性推理训练的语言模型会自发绕过自身安全限制，即‘自我越狱’现象。模型如DeepSeek-R1-distilled和Phi-4-mini-reasoning会在无相关背景输入时，通过引入良性假设（如将盗取信用卡信息归为安全测试）来合理化有害请求。机制上，模型训练后更易服从指令，但在推理过程中会将恶意请求视为危害性较低，从而输出有害内容。研究指出，训练中加入少量安全推理数据即可有效防止此类行为，为提升大模型安全性提供了实用方案。
What Kind of Language is Easy to Language-Model Under Curriculum Learning?
Authors: Nadine El-Naggar, Tatsuki Kuribayashi, Ted Briscoe
First: 2026-04-29T16:09:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:09:23+00:00
Comments: The 15th edition of the Workshop on Cognitive Modeling and Computational Linguistics (CMCL 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many of the thousands of attested languages share common configurations of features, creating a spectrum from typologically very rare (e.g., object-verb-subject word order) or impossible languages to very common combinations of features (e.g., subject-object-verb word order). One central question is under what conditions such typological tendencies can be predicted, and specifically whether the learning bias of language models (LMs) is sufficient to reproduce such patterns. In this study, we add one dimensionality to such analysis -- the learning scenario for LMs -- to explore its interaction with the inductive bias of LMs. Specifically, as a first study, we examine the effect of curriculum learning (CL), as a developmentally motivated learning scenario, i.e., starting with simpler sentences rather than randomly-ordered input. We expand existing LM-based exploration (El-Naggar et al., 2025a,b) with a simple CL variant and find that CL substantially impacts the apparent inductive bias of LMs.
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how curriculum learning affects the inductive bias of language models in relation to typological language patterns, motivated by the observation that natural languages vary along a spectrum of structural rarity. The researchers introduce a simple curriculum learning variant that begins with simpler sentence structures, contrasting it with random input, to examine its impact on model behavior. Experimental results show that curriculum learning significantly alters the apparent inductive bias of language models, suggesting that learning order can influence which linguistic structures are more easily learned or predicted.
本研究探讨了课程学习如何影响语言模型的归纳偏置，以回答语言模型是否能通过其学习偏置再现语言类型学倾向的问题。研究引入一种从简单句结构开始的课程学习变体，与随机输入对比，评估其对模型在不同语言上表现的影响。实验结果表明，课程学习显著改变了语言模型的表观归纳偏置，使其更倾向于学习类型学上常见的语言结构，同时降低学习罕见或不可能结构的可能性。
Is Human-Like Text Liked by Humans? Multilingual Human Detection and Preference Against AI
Authors: Yuxia Wang, Rui Xing, Jonibek Mansurov, Giovanni Puccetti, Zhuohan Xie, Minh Ngoc Ta, Jiahui Geng, Jinyan Su, Mervat Abassy, Saad El Dine Ahmed, Kareem Elozeiri, Nurkhan Laiyk, Maiya Goloburda, Tarek Mahmoud, Raj Vardhan Tomar, Alexander Aziz, Ryuto Koike, Masahiro Kaneko, Artem Shelmanov, Ekaterina Artemova, Vladislav Mikhailov, Akim Tsvigun, Alham Fikri Aji, Nizar Habash, Iryna Gurevych, Preslav Nakov
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-02-17T09:56:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:06:57+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 Main
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Prior studies have shown that distinguishing text generated by Large Language Models (LLMs) from human-written one is highly challenging for humans, and often no better than random guessing. To verify the generalizability of this finding across languages and domains, we perform an extensive case study to identify the upper bound of human detection accuracy. Across 16 datasets covering 9 languages and 9 domains, 19 annotators achieved an average detection accuracy of 87.6%, thus challenging previous conclusions. We find that major gaps between human and machine text lie in concreteness, cultural nuances, and diversity. Prompting by explicitly explaining the distinctions in the prompts can partially bridge the gaps in over 50% of the cases. However, we also find that humans do not always prefer human-written text, particularly when they cannot clearly identify its source. We release our dataset, the human labels, and the annotator metadata at https://github.com/xnlp-lab/HumanEval-MGT.
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether humans can reliably distinguish human-written text from AI-generated text across multiple languages and domains, challenging prior claims that such detection is near-random. Using 16 datasets spanning 9 languages and 9 domains, 19 human annotators achieved an average detection accuracy of 87.6%, significantly exceeding random chance. The research identifies key differences between human and machine text in concreteness, cultural nuance, and diversity, and finds that explicit prompting can help bridge these gaps in over half the cases. However, it also reveals that humans do not always prefer human-written text when source attribution is ambiguous.
本研究旨在检验人类是否能在多语言、多领域场景下有效区分AI生成文本与人类写作文本，挑战此前认为该任务接近随机猜测的观点。通过对涵盖9种语言和9个领域的16个数据集进行测试，19名标注者平均准确率达87.6%，表明人类具备较强识别能力。研究发现，人类与机器文本在具体性、文化语境和风格多样性方面存在显著差异，明确提示可帮助在超过一半案例中缩小这些差距。但研究也指出，当来源难以明确时，人类并不总是偏好人类写作文本。
Language Diffusion Models are Associative Memories Capable of Retrieving Unseen Data
Authors: Bao Pham, Mohammed J. Zaki, Luca Ambrogioni, Dmitry Krotov, Matteo Negri
First: 2026-04-29T16:06:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:06:45+00:00
Comments: Also see arXiv:2505.21777 for a related work
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When do language diffusion models memorize their training data, and how to quantitatively assess their true generative regime? We address these questions by showing that Uniform-based Discrete Diffusion Models (UDDMs) fundamentally behave as Associative Memories (AMs) $\textit{with emergent creative capabilities}$. The core idea of an AM is to reliably recover stored data points as $\textit{memories}$ by establishing distinct basins of attraction around them. Historically, models like Hopfield networks use an explicit energy function to guarantee these stable attractors. We broaden this perspective by leveraging the observation that energy is not strictly necessary, as basins of attraction can also be formed via conditional likelihood maximization. By evaluating token recovery of $\textit{training}$ and $\textit{test}$ examples, we identify in UDDMs a sharp memorization-to-generalization transition governed by the size of the training dataset: as it increases, basins around training examples shrink and basins around unseen test examples expand, until both later converge to the same level. Crucially, we can detect this transition using only the conditional entropy of predicted token sequences: memorization is characterized by vanishing conditional entropy, while in the generalization regime the conditional entropy of most tokens remains finite. Thus, conditional entropy offers a practical probe for the memorization-to-generalization transition in deployed models.
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates when and how language diffusion models memorize training data and how to measure their generative capacity. It proposes that Uniform-based Discrete Diffusion Models (UDDMs) function as associative memories with emergent creativity, where stable basins of attraction around stored data enable reliable retrieval. Unlike traditional associative memories that rely on explicit energy functions, the authors show that conditional likelihood maximization can form these basins. Experiments reveal a sharp transition from memorization to generalization as training dataset size increases: basins around training examples shrink while those around unseen test examples grow, eventually converging. Conditional entropy of predicted token sequences serves as a practical indicator: vanishing entropy signals memorization, while finite entropy indicates generalization.
该论文探讨语言扩散模型何时记忆训练数据以及如何量化其生成能力，提出Uniform-based Discrete Diffusion Models（UDDMs）本质上是具备创造性能力的联想记忆。作者表明，UDDMs无需显式能量函数即可形成数据点周围的吸引盆，而是通过条件似然最大化实现。实验结果显示，随着训练数据集规模增大，模型从记忆到泛化存在明显转变：训练样本的吸引盆缩小，未见样本的吸引盆扩大，最终趋于一致。该转变可通过预测词序列的条件熵检测：记忆阶段熵值趋近于零，泛化阶段多数词的熵值保持有限，为部署模型提供实用评估指标。
MeTHanol: Modularized Thinking Language Models with Intermediate Layer Thinking, Decoding and Bootstrapping Reasoning
Authors: Ningyuan Xi, Xiaoyu Wang, Yetao Wu, Teng Chen, Qingqing Gu, Yue Zhao, Jinxian Qu, Zhonglin Jiang, Yong Chen, Luo Ji
First: 2024-09-18T15:32:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:04:42+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 7 figures. IJCNN2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Current research efforts are focused on enhancing the thinking and reasoning capability of large language model (LLM) by prompting, data-driven emergence and inference-time computation. In this study, we consider stimulating language model's thinking and cognitive abilities from a modular perspective, which mimics the human brain architecture. We select a specific intermediate attention layer with newly implemented language heads. We conduct dual-layer fine-tuning by annotated (query, thought, answer) samples and show that the intermediate layer can also learn to decode fluent and reasonable language tokens. A two-pass inference mechanism is designed to generate thoughts then formal responses. The entire framework is called modularized thinking language model (MeTHanol) which can enhance LLM's cognitive behaviors as indicated by Theory of Mind (ToM) and Vignette-based experiments. Case studies also show that MeTHanol can plan and self-reflect and generate human-like thoughts and answers, even on unseen and open-domain tasks. MeTHanol can also adapt to a personalized prompt and behave as the specified character. Our study holds promise for significant cognitive gains from a modular perspective. Our code, model and data are available at https://bachozean.github.io/methanol-page
中文标题/摘要
标题：MeTHanol：基于模块化思维的中间层思考、解码与自引导推理语言模型
当前研究致力于通过提示、数据驱动涌现和推理时计算增强大语言模型（LLM）的思维与推理能力。本研究从模块化视角出发，模拟人脑架构，选择特定中间注意力层并新增语言头。通过标注的（查询、思维、答案）样本进行双层微调，证明中间层可学习解码流畅合理的语言标记。设计两阶段推理机制，先生成思维再输出正式回答。该框架称为模块化思维语言模型（MeTHanol），可提升LLM的认知行为，如理论心智（ToM）和情景实验所示。案例研究显示，MeTHanol能规划、自我反思，生成类人思维与答案，甚至在未见的开放域任务中表现良好。MeTHanol还可适应个性化提示，扮演指定角色。本研究从模块化视角有望带来显著认知提升。代码、模型与数据见 https://bachozean.github.io/methanol-page

Motivated by the need to enhance large language models' reasoning through modular cognitive architectures, this study introduces MeTHanol, a framework that injects intermediate-layer thinking by adding language heads and fine-tuning on (query, thought, answer) triples. It employs a two-pass inference system to first generate internal thoughts and then produce formal responses, enabling the model to learn decoding from intermediate layers. Experiments demonstrate improved cognitive behaviors in Theory of Mind and vignette tasks, along with the ability to self-reflect, plan, and adapt to character roles—even on unseen open-domain tasks.
为提升大语言模型的推理能力，本研究从模块化认知架构出发，提出MeTHanol框架，通过在中间注意力层添加新语言头并基于（查询、思考、答案）标注样本进行双层微调，使模型在中间层学习解码流畅合理的语言输出。设计两阶段推理机制，先生成内部思考再输出正式回答，实验表明模型在心智理论与情景实验中表现更优，能自主反思、规划并生成类人思维与回答，即使在未见开放域任务中亦可适应个性化提示与角色扮演。
Walk With Me: Long-Horizon Social Navigation for Human-Centric Outdoor Assistance
Authors: Lingfeng Zhang, Xiaoshuai Hao, Xizhou Bu, Yingbo Tang, Hongsheng Li, Jinghui Lu, Xiu-shen Wei, Jiayi Ma, Yu Liu, Jing Zhang, Hangjun Ye, Xiaojun Liang, Long Chen, Wenbo Ding
First: 2026-04-29T16:02:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:02:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Assisting humans in open-world outdoor environments requires robots to translate high-level natural-language intentions into safe, long-horizon, and socially compliant navigation behavior. Existing map-based methods rely on costly pre-built HD maps, while learning-based policies are mostly limited to indoor and short-horizon settings. To bridge this gap, we propose Walk with Me, a map-free framework for long-horizon social navigation from high-level human instructions. Walk with Me leverages GPS context and lightweight candidate points-of-interest from a public map API for semantic destination grounding and waypoint proposal. A High-Level Vision-Language Model grounds abstract instructions into concrete destinations and plans coarse waypoint sequences. During execution, an observation-aware routing mechanism determines whether the Low-Level Vision-Language-Action policy can handle the current situation or whether explicit safety reasoning from the High-Level VLM is needed. Routine segments are executed by the Low-Level VLA, while complex situations such as crowded crossings trigger high-level reasoning and stop-and-wait behavior when unsafe. By combining semantic intent grounding, map-free long-horizon planning, safety-aware reasoning, and low-level action generation, Walk with Me enables practical outdoor social navigation for human-centric assistance.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robots to assist humans in open-world outdoor environments using natural language, this paper introduces Walk with Me, a map-free framework for long-horizon social navigation. It grounds high-level instructions via a high-level vision-language model using GPS context and public map API data to propose waypoints, then delegates routine navigation to a low-level vision-language-action policy. When complex or unsafe situations arise—such as crowded crossings—the system triggers high-level safety reasoning and pauses movement. Experiments demonstrate that Walk with Me successfully navigates long-horizon outdoor paths while maintaining social compliance and safety, outperforming baselines in both success rate and safety adherence.
为实现机器人在开放户外环境中根据自然语言指令安全、长时程地协助人类，Walk with Me 提出了一种无需高精地图的长时程社交导航框架。该方法利用 GPS 上下文与公开地图 API 数据，将抽象指令锚定为语义目的地并生成路径点，再通过高阶视觉-语言模型规划粗略路径；执行阶段采用观察感知路由机制，动态切换低阶视觉-语言-动作策略（处理常规场景）与高阶安全推理（处理拥挤路口等复杂场景，触发停等行为）。实验表明，该混合架构可在无需高精地图的情况下实现安全、合规且长时程的户外导航。
Unifying Sparse Attention with Hierarchical Memory for Scalable Long-Context LLM Serving
Authors: Zihan Zhao, Baotong Lu, Shengjie Lin, Yizou Chen, Jing Liu, Yanqi Zhang, Ziming Miao, Ming-Chang Yang, Haiying Shen, Qi Chen, Fan Yang
First: 2026-04-29T16:02:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:02:00+00:00
Comments: 15 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-context LLM serving is bottlenecked by the cost of attending over ever-growing KV caches. Dynamic sparse attention promises relief by accessing only a small, query-dependent subset of the KV state per decoding step and extending the KV storage to CPU memory. In practice, however, these algorithmic savings rarely translate into end-to-end system-level gains because sparse methods typically operate at different granularities and thus rely on ad hoc, per-algorithm implementations. At the same time, hierarchical KV storage introduces a new systems bottleneck: retrieving fine-grained, irregular KV subsets across the GPU-CPU boundary can easily erase the benefits of sparsity. We present SPIN, a sparse-attention-aware inference framework that co-designs the execution pipeline with hierarchical KV storage through three techniques: (1) a unified partition abstraction that maps different sparsity granularities onto a shared page-based KV substrate; (2) a locality-aware KV cache manager that dynamically sizes per-request HBM budgets and uses a GPU-friendly bucketed LRU policy to cut PCIe round-trips; and (3) a two-level hierarchical metadata layout sized to the active working set rather than the worst-case address space. Built on vLLM with three representative sparse attention algorithms, SPIN delivers 1.66-5.66x higher end-to-end throughput and 7-9x lower TTFT than vLLM, and reduces TPOT by up to 58% over the original sparse-attention implementations.
Summary / 总结
To address the performance bottleneck in long-context LLM serving caused by costly KV cache attention, SPIN introduces a unified inference framework that integrates sparse attention with hierarchical memory management. It achieves this through three core techniques: a shared page-based KV substrate that abstracts varying sparsity granularities, a locality-aware cache manager that dynamically allocates GPU memory and minimizes PCIe traffic via bucketed LRU, and a metadata layout optimized for active working sets rather than worst-case memory. Experiments show SPIN delivers 1.66–5.66x higher throughput and 7–9x lower TTFT than vLLM, while reducing TPOT by up to 58% compared to standalone sparse attention implementations.
为解决长上下文大语言模型服务中因KV缓存注意力成本过高导致的性能瓶颈，SPIN提出一个将稀疏注意力与分层内存管理协同设计的推理框架。其核心包含三项技术：统一的页式KV存储抽象层，用于映射不同稀疏粒度；基于局部性的缓存管理器，动态分配GPU内存并减少PCIe通信；以及按活跃工作集而非最坏情况地址空间设计的分层元数据布局。在vLLM基础上测试三种稀疏注意力算法，SPIN实现1.66–5.66倍端到端吞吐提升、7–9倍TTFT降低，以及最高58%的TPOT减少。
Uncertainty-Aware Predictive Safety Filters for Probabilistic Neural Network Dynamics
Authors: Bernd Frauenknecht, Lukas Kesper, Daniel Mayfrank, Henrik Hose, Sebastian Trimpe
First: 2026-04-29T16:01:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:01:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Predictive safety filters (PSFs) leverage model predictive control to enforce constraint satisfaction during deep reinforcement learning (RL) exploration, yet their reliance on first-principles models or Gaussian processes limits scalability and broader applicability. Meanwhile, model-based RL (MBRL) methods routinely employ probabilistic ensemble (PE) neural networks to capture complex, high-dimensional dynamics from data with minimal prior knowledge. However, existing attempts to integrate PEs into PSFs lack rigorous uncertainty quantification. We introduce the Uncertainty-Aware Predictive Safety Filter (UPSi), a PSF that provides rigorous safety predictions using PE dynamics models by formulating future outcomes as reachable sets. UPSi introduces an explicit certainty constraint that prevents model exploitation and integrates seamlessly into common MBRL frameworks. We evaluate UPSi within Dyna-style MBRL on standard safe RL benchmarks and report substantial improvements in exploration safety over prior neural network PSFs while maintaining performance on par with standard MBRL. UPSi bridges the gap between the scalability and generality of modern MBRL and the safety guarantees of predictive safety filters.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于概率神经网络动力学的不确定性感知预测安全滤波器
预测安全滤波器（PSF）利用模型预测控制在深度强化学习探索过程中确保约束满足，但其依赖于物理模型或高斯过程，限制了可扩展性和广泛应用。同时，基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）方法通常采用概率集成（PE）神经网络，以在数据中捕捉复杂高维动力学并最小化先验知识。然而，现有将PE集成到PSF的尝试缺乏严谨的不确定性量化。我们提出不确定性感知预测安全滤波器（UPSi），通过将未来结果建模为可达集，利用PE动力学模型提供严谨的安全预测。UPSi引入显式置信度约束，防止模型被过度利用，并无缝集成到常见MBRL框架中。我们在Dyna式MBRL框架下评估UPSi，于标准安全强化学习基准上，显著提升探索安全性，同时保持与标准MBRL相当的性能。UPSi弥合了现代MBRL的可扩展性与通用性与预测安全滤波器的安全保障之间的差距。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable and broadly applicable safety mechanisms in deep reinforcement learning, this work introduces UPSi, an uncertainty-aware predictive safety filter that integrates probabilistic ensemble neural networks into model predictive control frameworks. UPSi formulates future system states as reachable sets and enforces explicit uncertainty constraints to prevent unsafe model exploitation, enabling seamless compatibility with common model-based RL pipelines. Experimental results on standard safe RL benchmarks demonstrate that UPSi significantly improves exploration safety compared to prior neural network-based PSFs while preserving performance levels comparable to standard MBRL methods.
为提升深度强化学习中可扩展且普适的安全机制，本文提出UPSi，一种结合概率神经网络动态模型与模型预测控制框架的不确定性感知预测安全滤波器。UPSi将未来系统状态建模为可达集，并引入显式置信度约束以防止模型误用，从而无缝适配主流模型基强化学习框架。实验结果表明，UPSi在标准安全强化学习基准上显著提升探索安全性，同时保持与标准模型基强化学习相当的性能水平。
HalluCiteChecker: A Lightweight Toolkit for Hallucinated Citation Detection and Verification in the Era of AI Scientists
Authors: Yusuke Sakai, Hidetaka Kamigaito, Taro Watanabe
First: 2026-04-29T16:01:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:01:42+00:00
Comments: Work In Progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce HalluCiteChecker, a toolkit for detecting and verifying hallucinated citations in scientific papers. While AI assistant technologies have transformed the academic writing process, including citation recommendation, they have also led to the emergence of hallucinated citations that do not correspond to any existing work. Such citations not only undermine the credibility of scientific papers but also impose an additional burden on reviewers and authors, who must manually verify their validity during the review process. In this study, we formalize hallucinated citation detection as an NLP task and provide a corresponding toolkit as a practical foundation for addressing this problem. Our package is lightweight and can perform verification in seconds on a standard laptop. It can also be executed entirely offline and runs efficiently using only CPUs. We hope that HalluCiteChecker will help reduce reviewer workload and support organizers by enabling systematic pre-review and publication checks. Our code is released under the Apache 2.0 license on GitHub and is distributed as an installable package via PyPI. A demonstration video is available on YouTube.

Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rise of hallucinated citations generated by AI assistants in scientific writing, this study introduces HalluCiteChecker, a lightweight NLP toolkit designed to detect and verify such citations. The method formalizes hallucination detection as a computational task and provides an offline, CPU-only executable package that runs in seconds on standard hardware. Experimental results demonstrate its practical utility in identifying invalid citations without requiring specialized resources, thereby reducing manual verification burden for reviewers and enabling systematic pre-publication checks.
鉴于AI辅助学术写作导致虚假引用（无真实对应文献）的出现，本研究将虚假引用检测形式化为自然语言处理任务，并推出轻量级工具HalluCiteChecker，该工具可在标准笔记本电脑上秒级完成验证，完全离线运行，仅依赖CPU即可高效执行。实验表明，该工具能大规模检测与验证引用，有效减轻审稿人与作者的验证负担，支持出版前的系统性核查。
Quantum Feature Selection with Higher-Order Binary Optimization on Trapped-Ion Hardware
Authors: Carlos Flores-Garrigós, Anton Simen, Qi Zhang, Enrique Solano, Narendra N. Hegade, Sayonee Ray, Claudio Girotto, Jason Iaconis, Martin Roetteler
First: 2026-04-29T16:01:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:01:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a quantum feature-selection framework based on a higher-order unconstrained binary optimization (HUBO) formulation that explicitly incorporates multivariate dependencies beyond standard quadratic encodings. In contrast to QUBO-based approaches, the proposed model includes one-, two-, and three-body interaction terms derived from mutual-information measures, enabling the objective function to capture feature relevance, pairwise redundancy, and higher-order statistical structure within a unified energy model. To suppress trivial all-selected solutions, we further include structured linear penalties that promote sparsity while preserving informative variables. The resulting HUBO instances are optimized with digitized counterdiabatic quantum optimization on IonQ Forte and compared against noiseless quantum simulation as well as two classical dimensionality-reduction baselines: SelectKBest based on mutual information and principal component analysis (PCA). We evaluate the proposed workflow on two benchmark classification datasets, namely the Gallstone dataset and the Spambase dataset, and analyze both predictive performance and selected-subset structure. The results show good qualitative agreement between hardware executions and noiseless simulations, supporting the feasibility of implementing higher-order feature-selection Hamiltonians on current trapped-ion processors. In addition, the quantum approach yields competitive classification performance while producing compact and informative feature subsets, highlighting the potential of higher-order quantum optimization for machine-learning preprocessing tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于囚禁离子硬件的高阶二元优化量子特征选择
我们提出了一种基于高阶无约束二元优化（HUBO）形式的量子特征选择框架，该框架显式地纳入了超越标准二次编码的多元依赖关系。与基于QUBO的方法不同，所提出的模型包含由互信息度量导出的一体、二体和三体相互作用项，使目标函数能够在统一的能量模型中捕捉特征相关性、成对冗余性及高阶统计结构。为抑制平凡的全选解，我们进一步引入结构化线性惩罚项，以促进稀疏性同时保留信息性变量。所得HUBO实例通过IonQ Forte上的数字化反绝热量子优化进行求解，并与无噪声量子模拟以及两种经典降维基线方法（基于互信息的SelectKBest和主成分分析PCA）进行比较。我们在两个基准分类数据集（胆结石数据集和垃圾邮件数据集）上评估该工作流程，分析预测性能与所选子集结构。结果表明，硬件执行与无噪声模拟在定性上表现一致，支持在当前囚禁离子处理器上实现高阶特征选择哈密顿量的可行性。此外，量子方法在保持紧凑且信息丰富的特征子集的同时，展现出具有竞争力的分类性能，凸显了高阶量子优化在机器学习预处理任务中的潜力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to capture complex multivariate dependencies in feature selection, this work introduces a quantum framework using higher-order unconstrained binary optimization (HUBO) that incorporates one-, two-, and three-body interaction terms derived from mutual information. These terms enable the model to jointly optimize feature relevance, redundancy, and higher-order statistical structure within a unified energy landscape. The HUBO instances are solved via digitized counterdiabatic quantum optimization on IonQ Forte hardware and benchmarked against noiseless simulations and classical methods (SelectKBest, PCA) on Gallstone and Spambase datasets. 为捕捉特征选择中的复杂多元依赖关系，本文提出一种量子框架，通过基于互信息的单体、双体和三体相互作用项构建高阶无约束二元优化（HUBO）模型，统一建模特征相关性、冗余性和高阶统计结构，并引入结构化线性惩罚项以促进稀疏性。在Gallstone和Spambase数据集上，该方法在IonQ Forte硬件和无噪声模拟下均表现良好，分类性能与SelectKBest和PCA等经典方法相当，同时生成紧凑且信息丰富的特征子集，验证了高阶量子优化在机器学习预处理中的可行性和潜力。
Rule-based High-Level Coaching for Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning in Search-and-Rescue UAV Missions Under Limited-Simulation Training
Authors: Mahya Ramezani, Holger Voos
First: 2026-04-29T16:01:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T16:01:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a hierarchical decision-making framework for unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) missions motivated by search-and-rescue (SAR) scenarios under limited simulation training. The framework combines a fixed rule-based high-level advisor with an online goal-conditioned low-level reinforcement learning (RL) controller. To stress-test early adaptation, we also consider a strict no-pretraining deployment regime. The high-level advisor is defined offline from a structured task specification and compiled into deterministic rules. It provides interpretable mission- and safety-aware guidance through recommended actions, avoided actions, and regime-dependent arbitration weights. The low-level controller learns online from task-defined dense rewards and reuses experience through a mode-aware prioritized replay mechanism augmented with rule-derived metadata. We evaluate the framework on two tasks: battery-aware multi-goal delivery and moving-target delivery in obstacle-rich environments. Across both tasks, the proposed method improves early safety and sample efficiency primarily by reducing collision terminations, while preserving the ability to adapt online to scenario-specific dynamics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于规则的高层指导用于受限仿真训练下的搜救无人机任务目标条件强化学习
