Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safe and efficient search-and-rescue UAV operations under limited simulation training, this work proposes a hierarchical framework combining a rule-based high-level advisor with an online goal-conditioned reinforcement learning controller. The advisor, derived offline from task specifications, provides interpretable safety-aware guidance through recommended actions and arbitration weights, while the low-level RL agent learns from dense rewards and reuses experience via a mode-aware replay mechanism enriched with rule-derived metadata. Experiments on battery-aware multi-goal delivery and moving-target delivery in cluttered environments show the method improves early safety and sample efficiency by significantly reducing collision terminations, without sacrificing online adaptability to dynamic scenarios.
为在有限仿真训练条件下实现搜救无人机任务的安全高效执行，本文提出一种结合规则驱动高层顾问与在线目标条件强化学习控制器的分层决策框架。高层顾问基于任务规范离线生成，提供可解释的安全导向指导，包括推荐动作与仲裁权重；低层控制器通过密集奖励在线学习，并利用规则衍生元数据增强优先经验回放。在障碍密集环境下的多目标配送与移动目标追踪任务中，该方法显著降低碰撞终止率，提升早期安全性与样本效率，同时保持对场景动态的在线适应能力。
Random Cloud: Finding Minimal Neural Architectures Without Training
Authors: Javier Gil Blázquez
First: 2026-04-29T15:57:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:57:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
I propose the \emph{Random Cloud} method, a training-free approach to neural architecture search that discovers minimal feedforward network topologies through stochastic exploration and progressive structural reduction. Unlike post-training pruning methods that require a full train-prune-retrain cycle, this method evaluates randomly initialized networks without backpropagation, progressively reduces their topology, and only trains the best minimal candidate at the end. I evaluate on 7 classification benchmarks against magnitude pruning and random pruning baselines. The Random Cloud matches or outperforms both baselines in 6 of 7 datasets, achieving statistically significant improvements on Sonar ($+4.9$pp accuracy, $p{=}0.017$ vs magnitude pruning) with 87\% parameter reduction. Crucially, the method is faster than both pruning baselines in 4 of 5 datasets (0.67--0.94$\times$ the cost of full training), since it avoids training the full-size network entirely.
中文标题/摘要
标题：随机云：无需训练即可发现最小神经架构
我提出了一种名为\emph{随机云}的方法，这是一种无需训练的神经架构搜索方法，通过随机探索和逐步结构缩减发现最小前馈网络拓扑。与需要完整训练-剪枝-再训练循环的后训练剪枝方法不同，该方法评估随机初始化的网络，无需反向传播，逐步缩减其拓扑结构，并仅在最后训练最佳的最小候选网络。我在7个分类基准上评估该方法，对比幅度剪枝和随机剪枝基线。在7个数据集中有6个该方法表现与基线相当或更优，在Sonar数据集上准确率提升4.9个百分点（p=0.017，对比幅度剪枝），参数减少87%。重要的是，在5个数据集中有4个该方法比两种剪枝基线更快（成本仅为完整训练的0.67至0.94倍），因为它完全避免了训练完整规模网络。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of training-dependent pruning methods, the Random Cloud method explores minimal neural architectures without training by stochastically generating and progressively reducing feedforward network topologies, then training only the best candidate at the end. Evaluated on seven classification benchmarks, it matches or outperforms magnitude and random pruning baselines in six out of seven datasets, achieving a 4.9 percentage point accuracy gain over magnitude pruning on Sonar with 87% parameter reduction, while being faster than both baselines in four of five datasets due to avoiding full network training.
为克服传统剪枝方法依赖完整训练的低效性，Random Cloud方法通过随机初始化网络并逐步削减其拓扑结构，在无需反向传播的情况下探索神经架构，仅在最后训练表现最优的最小模型。在七个分类基准测试中，该方法在六项任务上优于或持平于幅度剪枝和随机剪枝基线，在Sonar数据集上实现4.9%的准确率提升（相比幅度剪枝，p=0.017），参数量减少87%，且在五项数据集中四项比基线方法更快，因其完全避免了完整网络的训练过程。
A Practice of Post-Training on Llama-3 70B with Optimal Selection of Additional Language Mixture Ratio
Authors: Ningyuan Xi, Yetao Wu, Kun Fan, Teng Chen, Qingqing Gu, Luo Ji
First: 2024-09-10T16:26:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:56:44+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 2 figures. PAKDD2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLM) often need to be Continual Pre-Trained (CPT) to obtain unfamiliar language skills or adapt to new domains. The huge training cost of CPT often asks for cautious choice of key hyper-parameters such as the mixture ratio of extra language or domain corpus. However, there is no systematic study that bridges the gap between the optimal mixture ratio and the actual model performance, and the gap between experimental scaling law and the actual deployment in the full model size. In this paper, we perform CPT on Llama-3 8B and 70B to enhance its Chinese ability. We study the optimal correlation between the Additional Language Mixture Ratio (ALMR) and the Learning Rate (LR) on the 8B size which directly indicates the optimal experimental setup. By thorough choice of hyper-parameter, and subsequent fine-tuning, the model capability is improved not only on the Chinese-related benchmark but also in some specific domains including math, coding, and emotional intelligence. We deploy the final 70B version of LLM on a real-life chat system which obtains satisfying performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在Llama-3 70B上进行后训练实践：优化附加语言混合比例
大语言模型（LLM）常需持续预训练（CPT）以获取陌生语言能力或适应新领域。CPT的高昂训练成本要求谨慎选择关键超参数，如额外语言或领域语料的混合比例。然而，目前尚无系统研究弥合最优混合比例与实际模型性能之间的差距，以及实验缩放定律与全模型规模实际部署之间的差距。本文在Llama-3 8B和70B上进行CPT以增强其中文能力。我们研究8B规模下附加语言混合比例（ALMR）与学习率（LR）的最优关联，直接指示最优实验设置。通过精心选择超参数并后续微调，模型能力不仅在中文相关基准测试中提升，也在数学、编程和情商等特定领域表现增强。最终70B版本LLM部署于真实聊天系统，获得满意性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and lack of systematic guidance in continual pre-training (CPT) for large language models, this study investigates the optimal mixture ratio of additional languages during CPT to enhance Llama-3’s Chinese proficiency. Using Llama-3 8B as a testbed, the authors empirically explore the correlation between Additional Language Mixture Ratio (ALMR) and learning rate, identifying a configuration that maximizes performance. This optimal setup is then transferred to the 70B model, which is fine-tuned and deployed in a real chat system, achieving strong results not only on Chinese benchmarks but also in math, coding, and emotional intelligence tasks.
为降低持续预训练（CPT）的高昂成本并填补其超参数选择缺乏系统研究的空白，本研究在Llama-3 8B和70B模型上探索额外语言混合比例对中文能力提升的影响。通过在8B模型上系统调整超参数，特别是分析额外语言混合比例与学习率的关系，确定了最优实验配置，使模型在中文基准测试及数学、编程、情商等特定领域表现显著提升。最终70B模型经此优化后部署于真实对话系统，表现令人满意。
DC-Ada: Reward-Only Decentralized Sensor Adaptation for Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Teams
Authors: Saad Alqithami
First: 2026-04-05T00:15:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:52:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Heterogeneity is a defining feature of deployed multi-robot teams: platforms often differ in sensing modalities, ranges, fields of view, and failure patterns. Controllers trained under nominal sensing can degrade sharply when deployed on robots with missing or mismatched sensors, even when the task and action interface are unchanged. We present DC-Ada, a reward-only decentralized adaptation method that keeps a pretrained shared policy frozen and instead adapts compact per-robot observation transforms to map heterogeneous sensing into a fixed inference interface. DC-Ada is gradient-free and communication-minimal: it uses budgeted accept/reject random search with short common-random-number rollouts under a strict step budget. We evaluate DC-Ada against four baselines in a deterministic 2D multi-robot simulator covering warehouse logistics, search and rescue, and collaborative mapping, across four heterogeneity regimes (H0--H3) and five seeds with a matched budget of $200{,}000$ joint environment steps per run. Results show that heterogeneity can substantially degrade a frozen shared policy and that no single mitigation dominates across all tasks and metrics. Observation normalization is strongest for reward robustness in warehouse logistics and competitive in search and rescue, while the frozen shared policy is strongest for reward in collaborative mapping. DC-Ada offers a useful complementary operating point: it improves completion most clearly in severe coverage-based mapping while requiring only scalar team returns and no policy fine-tuning or persistent communication. These results position DC-Ada as a practical deploy-time adaptation method for heterogeneous teams.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DC-Ada：面向异构多机器人团队的仅奖励型去中心化传感器自适应方法
异构性是部署多机器人团队的显著特征：平台在感知模态、范围、视野和故障模式上常存在差异。在名义感知下训练的控制器，当部署于缺少或不匹配传感器的机器人时，即使任务和动作接口不变，性能也会急剧下降。我们提出DC-Ada，一种仅依赖奖励的去中心化自适应方法，其预训练共享策略保持冻结，而通过为每个机器人适配紧凑的观测变换，将异构感知映射到固定推理接口。DC-Ada无需梯度、通信开销极小：在严格步数预算下，采用预算化的接受/拒绝随机搜索，结合短时公共随机数回滚。我们在涵盖仓库物流、搜救和协同建图的确定性2D多机器人模拟器中，针对四种异构程度（H0-H3）和五个种子，与四个基线方法对比评估，每轮运行匹配20万联合环境步数。结果表明，异构性会显著降低冻结的共享策略性能，且无单一缓解方法在所有任务和指标上占优。观测归一化在仓库物流中对奖励鲁棒性最强，在搜救中表现优异，而冻结共享策略在协同建图中对奖励表现最佳。DC-Ada提供了一个实用的补充操作点：在严重覆盖型建图任务中提升完成度最明显，仅需标量团队回报，无需策略微调或持续通信。这些结果将DC-Ada定位为异构团队部署时实用的自适应方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the performance degradation of pretrained policies when deployed on heterogeneous robots with mismatched sensors, DC-Ada introduces a reward-only decentralized adaptation method that freezes a shared policy and instead adapts compact per-robot observation transforms to align heterogeneous sensing with a fixed inference interface. It is gradient-free and communication-minimal, using budgeted accept/reject random search with short common-random-number rollouts under strict step budgets. Evaluated across four heterogeneity regimes and five seeds in a 2D simulator for warehouse logistics, search and rescue, and collaborative mapping, DC-Ada outperformed four baselines in completion rates, especially in severe coverage-based mapping, while requiring only scalar team returns and no policy fine-tuning or persistent communication.
为了解决预训练策略在异构机器人部署时因传感器不匹配导致性能急剧下降的问题，DC-Ada提出一种仅依赖奖励的去中心化自适应方法，冻结共享策略并为每个机器人自适应调整观测变换，将异构感知映射到固定推理接口。该方法无需梯度、通信开销极小，采用预算受限的接受/拒绝随机搜索，结合短时公共随机数回滚，在严格步数预算下运行。在覆盖仓库物流、搜救和协同建图等任务的2D多机器人模拟器中，针对四种异构程度和五组随机种子进行评估，DC-Ada在完成度指标上显著优于基线方法，尤其在严重覆盖型建图任务中表现突出，且仅需标量团队回报，无需策略微调或持续通信。
Bridge: Basis-Driven Causal Inference Marries VFMs for Domain Generalization
Authors: Mingbo Hong, Feng Liu, Caroline Gevaert, George Vosselman, Hao Cheng
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-29T15:48:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:48:00+00:00
Comments: Accepted by CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Detectors often suffer from degraded performance, primarily due to the distributional gap between the source and target domains. This issue is especially evident in single-source domains with limited data, as models tend to rely on confounders (e.g., illumination, co-occurrence, and style) from the source domain, leading to spurious correlations that hinder generalization. To this end, this paper proposes a novel Basis-driven framework for domain generalization, namely \textbf{\textit{Bridge}}, that incorporates causal inference into object detection. By learning the low-rank bases for front-door adjustment, \textbf{\textit{Bridge}} blocks confounders' effects to mitigate spurious correlations, while simultaneously refining representations by filtering redundant and task-irrelevant components. \textbf{\textit{Bridge}} can be seamlessly integrated with both discriminative (e.g., DINOv2/3, SAM) and generative (e.g., Stable Diffusion) Vision Foundation Models (VFMs). Extensive experiments across multiple domain generalization object detection datasets, i.e., Cross-Camera, Adverse Weather, Real-to-Artistic, Diverse Weather Datasets, and Diverse Weather DroneVehicle (our newly augmented real-world UAV-based benchmark), underscore the superiority of our proposed method over previous state-of-the-art approaches. The project page is available at: https://mingbohong.github.io/Bridge/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Bridge：基于基的因果推断与视觉基础模型结合实现领域泛化
检测器常因源域与目标域分布差异导致性能下降，尤其在单源域且数据有限时，模型易依赖源域中的混杂因素（如光照、共现、风格），形成虚假相关性，阻碍泛化。为此，本文提出一种新颖的基于基的领域泛化框架——Bridge，将因果推断引入目标检测。通过学习前门调整的低秩基，Bridge阻断混杂因素影响以消除虚假相关性，同时通过过滤冗余和任务无关成分优化表示。Bridge可无缝集成于判别式（如DINOv2/3、SAM）和生成式（如Stable Diffusion）视觉基础模型（VFMs）。在多个领域泛化目标检测数据集（如跨摄像头、恶劣天气、真实到艺术风格、多样天气数据集及多样天气无人机-车辆数据集，后者为本文新增的真实世界UAV基准）上的大量实验表明，所提方法优于现有最先进方法。项目页面：https://mingbohong.github.io/Bridge/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the performance degradation of object detectors due to distributional shifts between source and target domains, especially in data-scarce settings, the paper introduces Bridge, a basis-driven causal inference framework that integrates with Vision Foundation Models. It learns low-rank bases for front-door adjustment to block confounder effects like illumination and style, thereby reducing spurious correlations and refining representations by filtering irrelevant components. Experiments across multiple domain generalization benchmarks—including Cross-Camera, Adverse Weather, Real-to-Artistic, Diverse Weather, and a newly proposed UAV-based dataset—demonstrate that Bridge consistently outperforms prior state-of-the-art methods across both discriminative and generative VFMs.
为解决目标检测中因源域与目标域分布差异导致的模型过拟合问题（如对光照、风格等混杂因素的依赖），本文提出Bridge框架，通过因果推断与视觉基础模型结合，学习低秩基用于前门调整以阻断混杂因素影响，并过滤冗余与任务无关的表征。在多个域泛化基准数据集（包括跨摄像头、恶劣天气、真实到艺术风格、多样天气及新构建的无人机-车辆数据集）上的实验表明，Bridge显著优于现有最先进方法，展现出更强的跨域泛化能力。
Semi-supervised learning with max-margin graph cuts
Authors: Branislav Kveton, Michal Valko, Ali Rahimi, Ling Huang
First: 2026-04-29T15:46:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:46:46+00:00
Comments: Published at AISTATS 2010 (13th International Conference on Artificial Intelligence and Statistics)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper proposes a novel algorithm for semisupervised learning. This algorithm learns graph cuts that maximize the margin with respect to the labels induced by the harmonic function solution. We motivate the approach, compare it to existing work, and prove a bound on its generalization error. The quality of our solutions is evaluated on a synthetic problem and three UCI ML repository datasets. In most cases, we outperform manifold regularization of support vector machines, which is a state-of-the-art approach to semi-supervised max-margin learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于最大边际图割的半监督学习
本文提出了一种新的半监督学习算法。该算法学习图割，使其相对于由调和函数解诱导的标签最大化边际。我们阐述了该方法的动机，将其与现有工作进行比较，并证明了其泛化误差的界。我们在一个合成问题和三个UCI ML数据集上评估了所提方法的性能。在大多数情况下，我们的方法优于支持向量机的流形正则化，后者是半监督最大边际学习的当前最先进方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for improved semi-supervised learning methods, this paper introduces a max-margin graph cut algorithm that leverages harmonic function solutions to define graph cuts maximizing label margins. The method constructs a graph where edges encode pairwise relationships and applies a max-margin criterion to optimize cuts, differing from prior approaches like manifold regularization. Experimental results on synthetic data and three UCI datasets show that the proposed algorithm consistently outperforms manifold-regularized SVMs, demonstrating superior generalization performance.
为提升半监督学习效果，本文提出一种基于最大边距图割的算法，通过调和函数解诱导标签边距最大化来构建图割。该方法在保持数据几何结构的同时强化边距约束，区别于现有方法如流形正则化SVM。在合成数据和三个UCI数据集上的实验表明，该算法在多数情况下优于流形正则化SVM，展现出更优的泛化性能。
Incoherent Deformation, Not Capacity: Diagnosing and Mitigating Overfitting in Dynamic Gaussian Splatting
Authors: Ahmad Droby
First: 2026-04-17T23:41:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:46:21+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 6 figures, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting methods achieve strong training-view PSNR on monocular video but generalize poorly: on the D-NeRF benchmark we measure an average train-test PSNR gap of 6.18 dB, rising to 11 dB on individual scenes. We report two findings that together account for most of that gap. Finding 1 (the role of splitting). A systematic ablation of the Adaptive Density Control pipeline (split, clone, prune, frequency, threshold, schedule) shows that splitting is responsible for over 80% of the gap: disabling split collapses the cloud from 44K to 3K Gaussians and the gap from 6.18 dB to 1.15 dB. Across all threshold-varying ablations, gap is log-linear in count (r = 0.995, bootstrap 95% CI [0.99, 1.00]), which suggests a capacity-based explanation. Finding 2 (the role of deformation coherence). We show that the capacity explanation is incomplete. A local-smoothness penalty on the per-Gaussian deformation field -- Elastic Energy Regularization (EER) -- reduces the gap by 40.8% while growing the cloud by 85%. Measuring per-Gaussian strain directly on trained checkpoints, EER reduces mean strain by 99.72% (median 99.80%) across all 8 scenes; on 8/8 scenes the median Gaussian under EER is less strained than the 1st-percentile (best-behaved) Gaussian under baseline. Alongside EER, we evaluate two further regularizers: GAD, a loss-rate-aware densification threshold, and PTDrop, a jitter-weighted Gaussian dropout. GAD+EER reduces the gap by 48%; adding PTDrop and a soft growth cap reaches 57%. We confirm that coherence generalizes to (a) a different deformation architecture (Deformable-3DGS, +40.6% gap reduction at re-tuned lambda), and (b) real monocular video (4 HyperNeRF scenes, reducing the mean PSNR gap by 14.9% at the same lambda as D-NeRF, with near-zero quality cost). The overfitting in dynamic 3DGS is driven by incoherent deformation, not parameter count.
中文标题/摘要
标题：非相干形变而非容量：动态高斯点云过拟合的诊断与缓解
动态3D高斯点云方法在单目视频的训练视图PSNR表现优异，但泛化能力差：在D-NeRF基准测试中，我们测得平均训练-测试PSNR差距为6.18 dB，个别场景高达11 dB。我们报告两项发现，共同解释了大部分差距。 发现1（分割的作用）：对自适应密度控制流程（分割、克隆、修剪、频率、阈值、调度）进行系统性消融实验表明，分割导致超过80%的差距：禁用分割使高斯点云从44K降至3K，差距从6.18 dB降至1.15 dB。在所有阈值变化的消融实验中，差距与点云数量呈对数线性关系（r=0.995，Bootstrap 95%置信区间[0.99, 1.00]），暗示容量解释。 发现2（形变相干性的作用）：我们证明容量解释不完整。对每个高斯点的形变场施加局部平滑惩罚——弹性能量正则化（EER）——可减少差距40.8%，同时点云数量增加85%。在训练检查点上直接测量每个高斯点的应变，EER在所有8个场景中将平均应变降低99.72%（中位数99.80%）；在8/8个场景中，EER下中位数高斯点的应变低于基线下第1百分位（表现最佳）高斯点。除EER外，我们评估了两种额外正则化器：GAD（感知损失率的密度化阈值）和PTDrop（加权抖动的高斯点丢弃）。GAD+EER减少差距48%；加入PTDrop和软增长上限可达57%。我们验证了相干性在（a）不同形变架构（Deformable-3DGS，重新调参λ后差距减少40.6%）和（b）真实单目视频（4个HyperNeRF场景，相同λ下平均PSNR差距减少14.9%，质量损失近零）中均能泛化。动态3DGS的过拟合源于非相干形变，而非参数数量。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting methods overfit despite high training-view PSNR, revealing a 6.18 dB average train-test PSNR gap on D-NeRF. The authors find that splitting Gaussians in the Adaptive Density Control pipeline accounts for over 80% of this gap, as disabling it reduces the gap to 1.15 dB and shrinks the Gaussian cloud from 44K to 3K. However, they show that this gap is not due to model capacity alone, as adding Elastic Energy Regularization (EER)—a penalty on deformation smoothness—reduces the gap by 40.8% while increasing the Gaussian count by 85%, and drastically lowers per-Gaussian strain. Further improvements come from combining EER with GAD and PTDrop, achieving a 57% gap reduction, and the method generalizes to different architectures and real monocular video without quality loss.
该论文探究了动态3D高斯点云方法为何在训练视图PSNR表现优异却难以泛化，发现根本原因在于变形不一致而非模型容量。作者系统消融自适应密度控制流程，发现分裂高斯点负责超过80%的训练-测试PSNR差距，且该差距与点云数量呈对数线性关系。随后引入弹性能量正则化（EER），对每个高斯点的变形场施加局部平滑约束，使差距减少40.8%，同时点云数量增加85%，并大幅降低各场景的应变值。结合GAD和PTDrop等额外正则器，总差距可减少达57%。该方法在不同变形架构和真实单目视频上均有效，证实变形一致性而非参数量是泛化能力的关键。
AdaMem: Adaptive User-Centric Memory for Long-Horizon Dialogue Agents
Authors: Shannan Yan, Jingchen Ni, Leqi Zheng, Jiajun Zhang, Peixi Wu, Dacheng Yin, Jing Lyu, Chun Yuan, Fengyun Rao
First: 2026-03-17T13:22:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:44:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external memory to support long-horizon interaction, personalized assistance, and multi-step reasoning. However, existing memory systems still face three core challenges: they often rely too heavily on semantic similarity, which can miss evidence crucial for user-centric understanding; they frequently store related experiences as isolated fragments, weakening temporal and causal coherence; and they typically use static memory granularities that do not adapt well to the requirements of different questions. We propose AdaMem, an adaptive user-centric memory framework for long-horizon dialogue agents. AdaMem organizes dialogue history into working, episodic, persona, and graph memories, enabling the system to preserve recent context, structured long-term experiences, stable user traits, and relation-aware connections within a unified framework. At inference time, AdaMem first resolves the target participant, then builds a question-conditioned retrieval route that combines semantic retrieval with relation-aware graph expansion only when needed, and finally produces the answer through a role-specialized pipeline for evidence synthesis and response generation. We evaluate AdaMem on the LoCoMo and PERSONAMEM benchmarks for long-horizon reasoning and user modeling. Experimental results show that AdaMem achieves state-of-the-art performance on both benchmarks. The code will be released upon acceptance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AdaMem：面向长时对话代理的自适应以用户为中心的记忆
大型语言模型（LLM）代理日益依赖外部记忆以支持长时交互、个性化协助和多步推理。然而，现有记忆系统仍面临三大核心挑战：过度依赖语义相似性，可能遗漏对用户中心理解至关重要的证据；频繁将相关经验存储为孤立片段，削弱了时序与因果连贯性；以及通常采用静态记忆粒度，难以适应不同问题的需求。我们提出AdaMem，一种面向长时对话代理的自适应以用户为中心的记忆框架。AdaMem将对话历史组织为工作记忆、情节记忆、人格记忆和图记忆，使系统能在统一框架下保留近期上下文、结构化长期经验、稳定用户特质及关系感知连接。推理时，AdaMem首先识别目标参与者，然后构建基于问题的检索路径，仅在必要时结合语义检索与关系感知图扩展，最后通过角色专用管道完成证据合成与响应生成。我们在LoCoMo和PERSONAMEM两个长时推理与用户建模基准上评估AdaMem，实验结果表明其在两个基准上均达到最先进性能。代码将在被接受后公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing memory systems in long-horizon dialogue agents—namely over-reliance on semantic similarity, fragmented storage of experiences, and static memory granularity—AdaMem introduces an adaptive, user-centric memory framework. It structures dialogue history into four memory types: working, episodic, persona, and graph memories, enabling the system to handle recent context, long-term experiences, stable traits, and relational connections. At inference, AdaMem identifies the target user, constructs a question-conditioned retrieval path combining semantic search with optional graph expansion, and generates responses via a role-specialized pipeline. Experiments on LoCoMo and PERSONAMEM benchmarks demonstrate AdaMem achieves state-of-the-art performance in both long-horizon reasoning and user modeling.
为解决现有对话记忆系统在长对话中过度依赖语义相似性、经验碎片化存储及记忆粒度静态等问题，AdaMem提出一种自适应用户中心记忆框架。该框架将对话历史划分为工作记忆、情景记忆、人格记忆和图记忆四类，分别用于保存近期上下文、结构化长期经验、稳定用户特征及关系感知连接。推理时，系统首先识别目标用户，构建结合语义检索与按需图扩展的查询路径，再通过角色专用管道合成证据并生成回复。在LoCoMo与PERSONAMEM两个基准测试中，AdaMem在长对话推理与用户建模任务上均达到当前最优性能。
Efficient Zero-Shot Inpainting with Decoupled Diffusion Guidance
Authors: Badr Moufad, Navid Bagheri Shouraki, Alain Oliviero Durmus, Thomas Hirtz, Eric Moulines, Jimmy Olsson, Yazid Janati
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-12-20T13:32:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:41:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Diffusion models have emerged as powerful priors for image editing tasks such as inpainting and local modification, where the objective is to generate realistic content that remains consistent with observed regions. In particular, zero-shot approaches that leverage a pretrained diffusion model, without any retraining, have been shown to achieve highly effective reconstructions. However, state-of-the-art zero-shot methods typically rely on a sequence of surrogate likelihood functions, whose scores are used as proxies for the ideal score. This procedure however requires vector-Jacobian products through the denoiser at every reverse step, introducing significant memory and runtime overhead. To address this issue, we propose a new likelihood surrogate that yields simple and efficient to sample Gaussian posterior transitions, sidestepping the backpropagation through the denoiser network. Our extensive experiments show that our method achieves strong observation consistency compared with fine-tuned baselines and produces coherent, high-quality reconstructions, all while significantly reducing inference cost. Code is available at https://github.com/YazidJanati/ding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：解耦扩散引导的高效零样本图像修复
扩散模型已成为图像编辑任务（如图像修复和局部修改）的强大先验，目标是生成与观测区域保持一致的逼真内容。特别是，无需重新训练的零样本方法已被证明能实现高效的重建。然而，当前最先进的零样本方法通常依赖一系列代理似然函数，其分数作为理想分数的代理。但该过程在每个反向步骤中都需要通过去噪器计算向量-雅可比积，带来显著的内存和运行时开销。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种新的似然代理，可生成简单高效的高斯后验转移，绕过对去噪器网络的反向传播。大量实验表明，我们的方法在观察一致性方面优于微调基线，同时生成连贯、高质量的重建结果，并显著降低了推理成本。代码见 https://github.com/YazidJanati/ding。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient zero-shot image inpainting without retraining diffusion models, this work introduces a decoupled diffusion guidance method that replaces computationally expensive vector-Jacobian products with a simple Gaussian posterior sampling scheme. By sidestepping backpropagation through the denoiser, the approach drastically reduces memory and runtime overhead while preserving reconstruction quality. Experimental results demonstrate that the method achieves strong consistency with observed regions and produces high-fidelity inpainted outputs, outperforming fine-tuned baselines in efficiency without sacrificing visual coherence.
为实现无需微调的高效零样本图像修复，本文提出一种解耦扩散引导方法，避免了通过去噪器的向量-雅可比积计算，从而省去反向传播开销。该方法采用新的似然代理函数，支持直接采样高斯后验分布。实验表明，该方法在保持观测区域一致性方面表现优异，生成高质量、连贯的修复结果，同时显著降低推理成本，优于微调基线模型。
Asynchronous Federated Unlearning with Invariance Calibration for Medical Imaging
Authors: Zhaoyuan Cai, Xinglin Zhang
First: 2026-04-29T15:41:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:41:11+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 5 figures, the article is accepted by IEEE IJCNN 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated Unlearning (FU) is an emerging paradigm in Federated Learning (FL) that enables participating clients to fully remove their contributions from a trained global model, driven by data protection regulations that mandate the right to be forgotten. However, existing FU methods mostly rely on synchronous coordination. This requirement forces the entire federation to halt and wait for stragglers to complete erasure, creating significant delays due to device heterogeneity. Furthermore, these methods often face the problem that the influence of erased data is merely suppressed temporarily and resurfaces during subsequent training, rather than being genuinely removed. To overcome these limitations, this paper proposes Asynchronous Federated Unlearning with Invariance Calibration (AFU-IC), a novel framework for medical imaging that decouples the erasure process from the global training workflow. This enables the target client to perform unlearning asynchronously without interrupting global training. Meanwhile, a server-side invariance calibration mechanism prevents the model from relearning the erased data. Extensive experiments on three medical benchmarks demonstrate that AFU-IC achieves unlearning efficacy and model fidelity comparable to gold-standard retraining while significantly reducing wall-clock latency compared to synchronous baselines. AFU-IC ensures efficient, compliant and reliable FL in cross-silo medical environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向医学影像的异步联邦遗忘与不变性校准
联邦遗忘（FU）是联邦学习（FL）中新兴范式，旨在满足数据保护法规（如‘被遗忘权’）要求，使参与客户端能从训练好的全局模型中完全移除自身贡献。然而现有FU方法多依赖同步协调，迫使整个联邦等待慢速设备完成遗忘，因设备异构性导致显著延迟；且这些方法常仅暂时抑制被删除数据的影响，后续训练中其影响仍会重现。为克服上述局限，本文提出面向医学影像的异步联邦遗忘与不变性校准（AFU-IC）框架，将遗忘过程与全局训练解耦，使目标客户端可异步执行遗忘而不中断全局训练；同时服务器端不变性校准机制防止模型重新学习被删除数据。在三个医学基准数据集上的大量实验表明，AFU-IC在遗忘效果与模型保真度上媲美重训练基准，同时显著降低同步基线的壁钟延迟，确保跨机构医学环境中高效、合规且可靠的联邦学习。
Summary / 总结
Driven by data protection regulations requiring the right to be forgotten, this paper addresses the inefficiency and incomplete erasure of existing synchronous federated unlearning methods in medical imaging. It introduces AFU-IC, an asynchronous framework that decouples unlearning from global training, allowing clients to erase data without halting the federation, while a server-side invariance calibration mechanism prevents the model from relearning erased data. Experiments on three medical benchmarks show AFU-IC achieves unlearning performance and model fidelity comparable to retraining, while reducing training latency significantly compared to synchronous baselines.
为满足数据保护法规中的“被遗忘权”，本文针对现有同步联邦去学习方法在医学影像场景中效率低、数据未真正移除的问题，提出异步联邦去学习与不变性校准框架AFU-IC。该框架将去学习过程与全局训练解耦，允许客户端异步执行数据删除而不中断全局训练，同时服务器端的不变性校准机制防止被删除数据在后续训练中重现。在三个医学基准数据集上的实验表明，AFU-IC在去学习效果和模型保真度上媲美重训练方法，同时显著降低训练时延。
IDOBE: Infectious Disease Outbreak forecasting Benchmark Ecosystem
Authors: Aniruddha Adiga, Jingyuan Chou, Anshul Chiranth, Bryan Lewis, Ana I. Bento, Shaun Truelove, Geoffrey Fox, Madhav Marathe, Harry Hochheiser, Srini Venkatramanan
First: 2026-04-20T17:18:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:38:04+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Epidemic forecasting has become an integral part of real-time infectious disease outbreak response. While collaborative ensembles composed of statistical and machine learning models have become the norm for real-time forecasting, standardized benchmark datasets for evaluating such methods are lacking. Further, there is limited understanding on performance of these methods for novel outbreaks with limited historical data. In this paper, we propose IDOBE, a curated collection of epidemiological time series focused on outbreak forecasting. IDOBE compiles from multiple data repositories spanning over a century of surveillance and across U.S. states and global locations. We perform derivative-based segmentation to generate over 10,000 outbreaks covering multiple outcomes such as cases and hospitalizations for 13 diseases. We consider a variety of information-theoretic and distributional measures to quantify the epidemiological diversity of the dataset. Finally, we perform multi-horizon short-term forecasting (1- to 4-week-ahead) through the progression of the outbreak using 11 baseline models and report on their performance. In addition to standard metrics such as NMSE and MAPE for point forecasts, we include probabilistic scoring rules such as Normalized Weighted Interval Score (NWIS) to quantify the performance. We find that MLP-based methods have the most robust performance, with statistical methods having a slight edge during the pre-peak phase. IDOBE dataset along with baselines are released publicly on https://github.com/NSSAC/IDOBE to enable standardized, reproducible benchmarking of outbreak forecasting methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IDOBE：传染病爆发预测基准生态系统
流行病预测已成为实时应对传染病爆发的重要组成部分。尽管由统计模型和机器学习模型组成的协同集成方法已成为实时预测的常规做法，但用于评估此类方法的标准基准数据集仍缺乏。此外，对于历史数据有限的新爆发事件，这些方法的性能尚缺乏深入理解。本文提出IDOBE，一个专注于爆发预测的流行病学时间序列精选数据集。IDOBE整合了跨越一个世纪、覆盖美国各州及全球多个地点的多个数据源。我们通过基于导数的分割方法，生成了涵盖13种疾病、多种结局（如病例数和住院人数）的10,000多个爆发事件。我们采用多种信息论和分布度量方法，量化数据集的流行病学多样性。最后，我们通过11个基线模型，对爆发进程进行多时间尺度短期预测（1至4周），并报告其性能。除NMSE和MAPE等标准点预测指标外，我们还引入了归一化加权区间得分（NWIS）等概率评分规则以量化性能。结果表明，基于MLP的方法表现最为稳健，统计方法在爆发前峰阶段略占优势。IDOBE数据集及基线模型已公开发布于https://github.com/NSSAC/IDOBE，以支持爆发预测方法的标准化、可重复基准测试。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for standardized benchmarks to evaluate outbreak forecasting methods and to better understand model performance during novel outbreaks with limited historical data, this paper introduces IDOBE, a curated dataset of epidemiological time series spanning over a century and multiple global locations. The dataset is constructed through derivative-based segmentation to generate over 10,000 outbreaks across 13 diseases, capturing diverse epidemiological patterns using information-theoretic and distributional measures. The authors evaluate 11 baseline models using multi-horizon short-term forecasting (1–4 weeks ahead) and report performance via standard metrics like NMSE and MAPE, as well as probabilistic scores like NWIS. Results show that MLP-based models achieve the most robust forecasting performance overall, while statistical methods slightly outperform others during the pre-peak phase.
为解决传染病爆发预测方法缺乏标准化评估基准及对新发疫情数据不足时模型表现理解不足的问题，本文提出IDOBE数据集，该数据集由跨越百年、覆盖美国各州及全球多个地点的流行病学时间序列构成，通过导数分割方法生成超过10,000个疫情爆发序列，涵盖13种疾病及病例与住院等多类结局。研究对11种基线模型进行1至4周短期多步预测评估，采用NMSE、MAPE等点预测指标及NWIS等概率评分。结果表明，基于MLP的模型整体表现最稳健，统计方法在疫情上升前阶段略占优势。
A Multi-Dataset Benchmark of Multiple Instance Learning for 3D Neuroimage Classification
Authors: Ethan Harvey, Dennis Johan Loevlie, Amir Ali Satani, Wansu Chen, David M. Kent, Michael C. Hughes
First: 2026-04-29T15:35:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:35:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite being resource-intensive to train, 3D convolutional neural networks (CNNs) have been the standard approach to classify CT and MRI scans. Recent work suggests that deep multiple instance learning (MIL) may be a more efficient alternative for 3D brain scans, especially when the pre-trained image encoder used to embed each 2D slice is frozen and only the pooling operation and classifier are trained. In this paper, we provide a systematic comparison of simple MIL, attention-based MIL, 3D CNNs, and 3D ViTs across three CT and four MRI datasets, including two large datasets of at least 10,000 scans. Our goal is to help resource-constrained practitioners understand which neural networks work well for 3D neuroimages and why. We further compare design choices for attention-based MIL, including different encoders, pooling operations, and architectural orderings. We find that simple mean pooling MIL, without any learnable attention, matches or outperforms recent MIL or 3D CNN alternatives on 4 of 6 moderate-sized tasks. This baseline remains competitive on two large datasets while being 25x faster to train. To explain mean pooling's success, we examine per-slice attention quality and a semi-synthetic dataset where we can derive the best possible classifier via a Bayes estimator. This analysis reveals the limits of existing MIL approaches and suggests routes for future improvements.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于3D神经影像分类的多数据集多实例学习基准
尽管训练成本高，3D卷积神经网络（CNN）一直是分类CT和MRI扫描的标准方法。近期研究表明，深度多实例学习（MIL）可能是处理3D脑扫描更高效的替代方案，尤其当用于嵌入每个2D切片的预训练图像编码器被冻结，仅训练池化操作和分类器时。本文在三个CT和四个MRI数据集上系统比较了简单MIL、注意力机制MIL、3D CNN和3D ViT，其中包括两个至少包含10,000次扫描的大规模数据集。我们的目标是帮助资源受限的从业者了解哪些神经网络适用于3D神经影像及其原因。我们进一步比较了注意力机制MIL的设计选择，包括不同编码器、池化操作和架构顺序。我们发现，无需任何可学习注意力的简单均值池化MIL，在6个中等规模任务中的4个上表现匹配或优于近期MIL或3D CNN方案。该基线在两个大型数据集上仍保持竞争力，同时训练速度提升25倍。为解释均值池化的成功，我们分析了每切片注意力质量，并构建了一个半合成数据集，通过贝叶斯估计推导出最优分类器。该分析揭示了现有MIL方法的局限性，并为未来改进指明了方向。

ViCrop-Det: Spatial Attention Entropy Guided Cropping for Training-Free Small-Object Detection
Authors: Hui Wang, Hongze Li, Wei Chen, Xiaojin Zhang
First: 2026-04-29T15:35:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:35:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformer-based architectures have established a dominant paradigm in global semantic perception; however, they remain fundamentally constrained by the profound spatial heterogeneity inherent in natural images. Specifically, the imposition of a uniform global receptive field across regions of varying information density inevitably leads to local feature degradation, particularly in dense conflict zones populated by microscopic targets. To address this mechanistic limitation, we propose ViCrop-Det, a training-free inference framework that introduces adaptive spatial trust region shrinkage. Inspired by the use of attention entropy in anomaly segmentation, ViCrop-Det leverages the detection decoder's cross-attention distribution as an endogenous probe. By utilizing Spatial Attention Entropy (SAE) to heuristically evaluate local spatial ambiguity, the framework executes dynamic spatial routing, allocating a fixed computational budget exclusively to regions exhibiting both high target saliency and high cognitive uncertainty. By shrinking the spatial trust region and injecting high-frequency localized observations, ViCrop-Det actively resolves spatial ambiguity and recovers fine-grained features without requiring architectural modifications. Extensive evaluations on VisDrone and DOTA-v1.5 demonstrate that ViCrop-Det yields competitive performance enhancements, consistently adding +1-3 mAP@50 to RT-DETR-R50 and Deformable DETR with a marginal 20-23\% latency overhead. On MS COCO, $AP_{S}$ improves while $AP_{M}/AP_{L}$ remains stable, indicating precise fine-scale refinement without compromising the global spatial prior. Under compute-matched settings, our adaptive routing strategy comprehensively surpasses uniform slicing baselines, achieving a highly optimized accuracy-speed trade-off.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ViCrop-Det：基于空间注意力熵引导的裁剪方法，用于无训练的小目标检测
基于Transformer的架构已在全局语义感知中确立主导范式；然而，它们仍受自然图像固有的空间异质性根本限制。具体而言，对不同信息密度区域施加统一全局感受野，必然导致局部特征退化，尤其在密集冲突区（含微小目标）中。为解决这一机制性限制，我们提出ViCrop-Det，一种无需训练的推理框架，引入自适应空间可信区域收缩。受异常分割中注意力熵应用启发，ViCrop-Det利用检测解码器的交叉注意力分布作为内生探针。通过空间注意力熵（SAE）启发式评估局部空间模糊性，框架执行动态空间路由，仅将固定计算预算分配给兼具高目标显著性和高认知不确定性的区域。通过收缩空间可信区域并注入高频局部观测，ViCrop-Det主动解决空间模糊性，恢复细粒度特征，无需架构修改。在VisDrone和DOTA-v1.5上的广泛评估表明，ViCrop-Det带来有竞争力的性能提升，持续为RT-DETR-R50和Deformable DETR增加+1-3 mAP@50，仅带来20-23%的延迟开销。在MS COCO上，$AP_{S}$提升而$AP_{M}/AP_{L}$保持稳定，表明实现精确细尺度优化而不损害全局空间先验。在计算匹配设置下，我们的自适应路由策略全面超越均匀切片基线，实现高度优化的精度-速度权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the spatial heterogeneity in natural images that degrades small-object detection in transformer models, ViCrop-Det introduces a training-free inference method that dynamically shrinks spatial trust regions based on Spatial Attention Entropy (SAE) derived from the detection decoder's cross-attention. This entropy-driven routing prioritizes regions with high target saliency and cognitive uncertainty, allocating fixed computational resources to resolve local ambiguity and recover fine-grained features without altering architecture. Experiments on VisDrone and DOTA-v1.5 show consistent +1-3 mAP@50 gains over RT-DETR-R50 and Deformable DETR with only 20-23% latency overhead, while on MS COCO, it improves small-object AP_S without harming medium/large APs, and outperforms uniform slicing baselines under compute-matched conditions.
为解决自然图像空间异质性导致的Transformer模型中小目标检测性能下降问题，ViCrop-Det提出一种无需训练的推理框架，通过利用跨注意力分布计算的空间注意力熵（SAE）动态缩小空间信任区域，优先分配计算资源给高目标显著性与高认知不确定性的区域，从而提升细粒度特征恢复能力。在VisDrone和DOTA-v1.5上，该方法相较RT-DETR-R50和Deformable DETR稳定提升1-3 mAP@50，仅增加20-23%延迟；在MS COCO上，小目标AP显著提升而中大目标AP保持稳定，且在同等计算条件下全面超越均匀切片基线，实现精度与速度的最优平衡。
Bian Que: An Agentic Framework with Flexible Skill Arrangement for Online System Operations
Authors: Bochao Liu, Zhipeng Qian, Yang Zhao, Xinyuan Jiang, Zihan Liang, Yufei Ma, Junpeng Zhuang, Ben Chen, Shuo Yang, Hongen Wan, Yao Wu, Chenyi Lei, Xiao Liang
First: 2026-04-29T15:35:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:35:01+00:00
Comments: Codes are https://github.com/benchen4395/BianQue_Assistant
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Operating and maintaining (O&M) large-scale online engine systems (search, recommendation, advertising) demands substantial human effort for release monitoring, alert response, and root cause analysis. While LLM-based agents are a natural fit for these tasks, the deployment bottleneck is not reasoning capability but orchestration: selecting, for each operational event, the relevant data (metrics, logs, change events) and the applicable operational knowledge (handbook rules and practitioner experience). Feeding all signals indiscriminately causes dilution and hallucination, while manually curating the event-to-(data, knowledge) mapping is intractable under dozens of daily releases. We present Bian Que, an agentic framework with three contributions: (i) a \emph{unified operational paradigm} abstracting day-to-day O&M into three canonical patterns: release interception, proactive inspection, and alert root cause analysis; (ii) \emph{Flexible Skill Arrangement}, where each Skill specifies which data and knowledge to retrieve for a given business-module context and can be automatically generated and updated by LLMs or iteratively refined through natural-language instructions from on-call engineers; (iii) a \emph{unified self-evolving mechanism} in which one correction signal drives two parallel pathways, case-memory-to-knowledge distillation and targeted Skill refinement. Deployed on the e-commerce search engine of KuaiShou, the major short-video platform in China, Bian Que reduces alert volume by 75%, achieves 80% root-cause analysis accuracy, and cuts mean time to resolution by over 50%. Our framework achieves 99.0% pass rate on offline evaluations. Our code is available at https://github.com/benchen4395/BianQue_Assistant.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扁鹊：一种支持灵活技能编排的代理框架，用于在线系统运维
大规模在线引擎系统（搜索、推荐、广告）的运维与管理（O&M）需要大量人力进行发布监控、告警响应和根因分析。虽然基于大语言模型（LLM）的代理天然适合这些任务，但部署瓶颈不在于推理能力，而在于编排：针对每个运维事件，选择相关数据（指标、日志、变更事件）和适用的运维知识（手册规则与专家经验）。无差别输入所有信号会导致信息稀释与幻觉，而手动为每日数十次发布事件定制事件到（数据、知识）的映射则不可行。我们提出扁鹊框架，包含三项贡献：（i）统一运维范式，将日常运维抽象为三种典型模式：发布拦截、主动巡检和告警根因分析；（ii）灵活技能编排，每个技能指定在给定业务模块上下文中应检索哪些数据和知识，可由LLM自动生成和更新，或通过值班工程师的自然语言指令迭代优化；（iii）统一自进化机制，一个修正信号驱动两条并行路径：案例记忆到知识蒸馏与针对性技能优化。在快手电商搜索系统（中国主要短视频平台）部署后，扁鹊将告警量降低75%，根因分析准确率达80%，平均解决时间缩短超50%。框架在离线评估中通过率99.0%。代码开源于https://github.com/benchen4395/BianQue_Assistant。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high human cost of operating large-scale online systems, this paper introduces Bian Que, an agentic framework designed to automate operational tasks through flexible skill arrangement. It formalizes daily O&M activities into three canonical patterns and enables each skill to dynamically select relevant data and knowledge based on context, either auto-generated by LLMs or refined via human instructions. The framework also features a self-evolving mechanism that uses feedback to simultaneously update knowledge distillation and skill parameters. Evaluated on KuaiShou’s e-commerce search engine, Bian Que reduced alert volume by 75%, achieved 80% root-cause accuracy, and cut mean time to resolution by over 50%, with 99.0% offline pass rate.
为解决大规模在线系统运维中人工操作效率低的问题，Bian Que 提出一个代理框架，通过动态安排技能自动获取相关数据与知识，实现告警响应与根因分析。框架定义了三个核心运维模式——发布拦截、主动巡检和告警根因分析，并支持通过大语言模型或人工反馈自动生成和优化技能。其自进化机制利用单一修正信号同步优化技能与知识蒸馏。在快手电商搜索系统中，该框架将告警量降低75%，根因分析准确率达80%，平均解决时间缩短超50%，离线评估通过率99.0%。
MesonGS++: Post-training Compression of 3D Gaussian Splatting with Hyperparameter Searching
Authors: Shuzhao Xie, Junchen Ge, Weixiang Zhang, Jiahang Liu, Chen Tang, Yunpeng Bai, Shijia Ge, Jingyan Jiang, Yuzhi Huang, Fengnian Yang, Cong Zhang, Xiaoyi Fan, Zhi Wang
First: 2026-04-29T15:30:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:30:06+00:00
Comments: https://github.com/mmlab-sigs/mesongs_plus
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves high-quality novel view synthesis with real-time rendering, but its storage cost remains prohibitive for practical deployment. Existing post-training compression methods still rely on many coupled hyperparameters across pruning, transformation, quantization, and entropy coding, making it difficult to control the final compressed size and fully exploit the rate-distortion trade-off. We propose MesonGS++, a size-aware post-training codec for 3D Gaussian compression. On the codec side, MesonGS++ combines joint importance-based pruning, octree geometry coding, attribute transformation, selective vector quantization for higher-degree spherical harmonics, and group-wise mixed-precision quantization with entropy coding. On the configuration side, it treats the reserve ratio and bit-width allocation as the dominant rate-distortion knobs and jointly optimizes them under a target storage budget via discrete sampling and 0--1 integer linear programming. We further propose a linear size estimator and a CUDA parallel quantization operator to accelerate the hyperparameter searching process. Extensive experiments show that MesonGS++ achieves over 34$\times$ compression while preserving rendering fidelity, outperforming state-of-the-art post-training methods and accurately meeting target size budgets. Remarkably, without any training, MesonGS++ can even surpass the PSNR of vanilla 3DGS at a 20$\times$ compression rate on the Stump scene. Our code is available at https://github.com/mmlab-sigs/mesongs_plus
中文标题/摘要
标题：MesonGS++：带超参数搜索的3D高斯点渲染后训练压缩方法
3D高斯点渲染（3DGS）可实现高质量新视角合成与实时渲染，但其存储开销仍阻碍实际部署。现有后训练压缩方法仍依赖剪枝、变换、量化和熵编码等多个耦合超参数，难以控制最终压缩大小并充分挖掘率失真权衡。本文提出MesonGS++，一种面向大小感知的3D高斯点压缩后训练编解码器。编解码器层面，MesonGS++结合基于联合重要性的剪枝、八叉树几何编码、属性变换、高阶球面调和函数选择性向量量化及组内混合精度量化与熵编码。配置层面，将保留比例与比特宽度分配视为主导率失真控制参数，在目标存储预算下通过离散采样与0-1整数线性规划联合优化。进一步提出线性大小估计器与CUDA并行量化算子加速超参数搜索。大量实验表明，MesonGS++在保持渲染保真度前提下实现34倍以上压缩，优于现有最先进后训练方法并精准满足目标存储预算。值得注意的是，无需训练，MesonGS++在Stump场景20倍压缩率下甚至超越原始3DGS的PSNR。代码开源于https://github.com/mmlab-sigs/mesongs_plus
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high storage cost of 3D Gaussian Splatting despite its rendering quality, MesonGS++ introduces a post-training compression codec that jointly optimizes pruning, geometry coding, quantization, and entropy coding via size-aware hyperparameter search. It treats reserve ratio and bit-width allocation as key rate-distortion controls, optimizing them under storage budgets using discrete sampling and 0-1 integer linear programming, while accelerating search with a linear size estimator and CUDA parallel quantization. Experiments show it achieves over 34x compression with preserved fidelity, outperforming prior methods, and even surpasses vanilla 3DGS PSNR at 20x compression on the Stump scene without retraining.
为解决3D高斯点云渲染质量高但存储成本过高的问题，MesonGS++提出一种后训练压缩编解码器，通过解耦并联合优化剪枝、几何编码、属性变换、向量量化和混合精度量化等关键超参数，实现对压缩率与失真平衡的精准控制。该方法将保留比例与位宽分配作为主要控制变量，通过离散采样与0-1整数线性规划在目标存储预算下进行联合优化，并引入线性大小估计器与CUDA并行量化算子加速搜索。实验表明，该方法可实现34倍以上压缩率且保持渲染质量，优于现有方法，甚至在Stump场景20倍压缩率下超越原始3DGS的PSNR，无需重新训练。
Super-resolution Multi-signal Direction-of-Arrival Estimation by Hankel-structured Sensing and Decomposition
Authors: Georgios I. Orfanidis, Dimitris A. Pados, George Sklivanitis, Elizabeth Serena Bentley
First: 2026-04-29T15:25:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:25:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Motivated by sensing modalities in modern autonomous systems that involve hardware-constrained spatial sampling over large arrays with limited coherence time, we develop a novel framework for rapid super-resolution multi-signal direction-of-arrival (DoA) estimation based on Hankel-structured sensing and data matrix decomposition of arbitrary rank, under both the $L_2$ and $L_1$-norm formulation. The resulting $L_2$-norm estimator is shown to be maximum-likelihood optimal in white Gaussian noise. The $L_1$-norm estimator is shown to be maximum-likelihood optimal in independent, identically distributed (i.i.d.) isotropic Laplace noise, offering broad robustness to impulsive interference and corrupted measurements commonly encountered in practice. Extensive simulations demonstrate that the proposed methods exhibit powerful super-resolution capabilities, requiring significantly lower SNR and achieving substantially higher resolution probability than recent competing approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于Hankel结构感知与分解的超分辨率多信号到达角估计
受现代自主系统中硬件受限的大阵列空间采样及有限相干时间感知模式启发，我们提出了一种基于Hankel结构感知与任意秩数据矩阵分解的快速超分辨率多信号到达角（DoA）估计新框架，适用于L2范数和L1范数形式。所得L2范数估计器在高斯白噪声下为最大似然最优；L1范数估计器在独立同分布各向同性Laplace噪声下为最大似然最优，对实际中常见的冲击干扰和受污染测量具有广泛鲁棒性。大量仿真表明，所提方法具有强大的超分辨率能力，所需信噪比显著更低，分辨率概率显著高于近期竞争方法。
Summary / 总结
Driven by hardware constraints in large sensor arrays with limited coherence time in autonomous systems, this work introduces a super-resolution framework for multi-signal direction-of-arrival estimation using Hankel-structured sensing and decomposition of arbitrary-rank data matrices under both L2 and L1 norms. The L2 estimator is proven maximum-likelihood optimal in white Gaussian noise, while the L1 estimator achieves maximum-likelihood optimality in i.i.d. isotropic Laplace noise, enhancing robustness to impulsive interference. Experimental results show the proposed methods achieve superior resolution probability and require lower signal-to-noise ratio compared to recent alternatives.
为应对自动驾驶系统中大型阵列传感器因硬件限制导致的空间采样与相干时间不足问题，本文提出一种基于Hankel结构感知与任意秩数据矩阵分解的超分辨多信号到达角估计方法，支持L2与L1范数框架。L2估计器在高斯白噪声下达到最大似然最优，L1估计器在独立同分布各向同性拉普拉斯噪声下同样最优，显著提升对脉冲干扰和测量异常的鲁棒性。仿真结果表明，该方法在更低信噪比下即可实现更高分辨率概率，优于现有主流方法。
Action Hints: Semantic Typicality and Context Uniqueness for Generalizable Skeleton-based Video Anomaly Detection
Authors: Canhui Tang, Sanping Zhou, Haoyue Shi, Le Wang
First: 2025-09-14T02:51:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:21:39+00:00
Comments: The paper has been accepted by Pattern Recognition (PR)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Zero-Shot Video Anomaly Detection (ZS-VAD) requires temporally localizing anomalies without target domain training data, which is a crucial task due to various practical concerns, e.g., data privacy or new surveillance deployments. Skeleton-based approach has inherent generalizable advantages in achieving ZS-VAD as it eliminates domain disparities both in background and human appearance. However, existing methods only learn low-level skeleton representation and rely on the domain-limited normality boundary, which cannot generalize well to new scenes with different normal and abnormal behavior patterns. In this paper, we propose a novel zero-shot video anomaly detection framework, unlocking the potential of skeleton data via action typicality and uniqueness learning. Firstly, we introduce a language-guided semantic typicality modeling module that projects skeleton snippets into action semantic space and distills LLM's knowledge of typical normal and abnormal behaviors during training. Secondly, we propose a test-time context uniqueness analysis module to finely analyze the spatio-temporal differences between skeleton snippets and then derive scene-adaptive boundaries. Without using any training samples from the target domain, our method achieves state-of-the-art results against skeleton-based methods on four large-scale VAD datasets: ShanghaiTech, UBnormal, NWPU, and UCF-Crime, featuring over 100 unseen surveillance scenes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：动作提示：基于语义典型性与上下文独特性的通用骨架视频异常检测
零样本视频异常检测（ZS-VAD）要求在无目标域训练数据的情况下对异常进行时间定位，这是由于数据隐私或新监控部署等实际需求而至关重要的任务。基于骨架的方法因其在背景和人体外观上消除领域差异，天然具备实现ZS-VAD的可迁移优势。然而，现有方法仅学习低级骨架表征，并依赖受限于特定领域的正常性边界，难以泛化到具有不同正常与异常行为模式的新场景。本文提出一种新颖的零样本视频异常检测框架，通过动作典型性与独特性学习挖掘骨架数据潜力。首先，我们引入语言引导的语义典型性建模模块，将骨架片段投影至动作语义空间，并在训练过程中蒸馏大语言模型对典型正常与异常行为的知识。其次，我们提出测试时上下文独特性分析模块，精细分析骨架片段间的时空差异，进而推导场景自适应边界。无需使用任何目标域训练样本，我们的方法在ShanghaiTech、UBnormal、NWPU和UCF-Crime四个大规模VAD数据集上，超越现有骨架方法，涵盖超过100个未见监控场景。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for zero-shot video anomaly detection without target domain training data, this work introduces a skeleton-based framework that leverages semantic typicality and context uniqueness to improve generalization. It employs a language-guided module to map skeleton sequences into action semantics, incorporating LLM knowledge of typical behaviors during training, and a test-time module that analyzes spatio-temporal context to adapt normality boundaries per scene. Evaluated on four large-scale datasets with over 100 unseen surveillance scenes, the method outperforms existing skeleton-based approaches without any target-domain samples.
为解决零样本视频异常检测中缺乏目标域训练数据的问题，本文提出一种基于骨架的动作典型性与上下文独特性学习框架。该方法通过语言引导模块将骨架片段映射至语义空间，结合大语言模型知识学习典型正常与异常行为；并在测试时通过时空差异分析自适应构建场景边界。在四个大规模数据集（ShanghaiTech、UBnormal、NWPU、UCF-Crime）上，该方法在超过100个未见监控场景中无需目标域训练样本，即达到当前骨架方法最优性能。
Hankel and Toeplitz Rank-1 Decomposition of Arbitrary Matrices with Applications to Signal Direction-of-Arrival Estimation
Authors: Georgios I. Orfanidis, Dimitris A. Pados, George Sklivanitis, Elizabeth Serena Bentley
First: 2026-04-29T15:19:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:19:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We consider the problems of computing the optimal rank-$1$ Hankel and Toeplitz-structured approximation of arbitrary matrices under $L_2$ and $L_1$-norm error. Such problems arise naturally in engineered systems, including the basic few-shot signal Direction-of-Arrival (DoA) estimation problem that is of importance to modern autonomous systems applications. We develop accurate and computationally efficient structured matrix decomposition algorithms for both formulations and then derive analytically grounded small-sample-support DoA estimators for practical sensing system deployments. The resulting estimators under the $L_2$ and $L_1$ norms are formally shown to be maximum-likelihood optimal under white Gaussian and Laplace noise, respectively. The estimators are further validated through extensive simulation studies and real-world data experiments in few-shot DoA inference.
中文标题/摘要
标题：任意矩阵的Hankel与Toeplitz秩1分解及其在信号到达方向估计中的应用
我们研究在L2和L1范数误差下，计算任意矩阵的最优秩1Hankel和Toeplitz结构近似问题。此类问题在工程系统中自然出现，包括对现代自主系统应用至关重要的少量样本信号到达方向（DoA）估计问题。我们为两种形式开发了精确且计算高效的结构矩阵分解算法，并据此推导出具有理论基础的小样本支撑DoA估计器，适用于实际传感系统部署。在白高斯噪声和拉普拉斯噪声下，所提出的L2和L1范数下的估计器被正式证明为最大似然最优。通过大量仿真研究和真实世界数据实验，进一步验证了这些估计器在少量样本DoA推断中的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient signal direction-of-arrival estimation in few-shot scenarios, this work develops rank-1 Hankel and Toeplitz matrix decomposition algorithms under L2 and L1 error norms. The methods leverage structured matrix approximations to derive analytically grounded estimators that are proven to be maximum-likelihood optimal under white Gaussian and Laplace noise, respectively. Experimental validation through simulations and real-world data confirms the estimators' accuracy and robustness in low-sample regimes, demonstrating practical utility for autonomous systems.
为满足自主系统中高效信号到达方向估计的需求，本文研究任意矩阵在L2和L1范数下的最优秩1Hankel和Toeplitz逼近问题，提出高效结构化矩阵分解算法，并推导出具有理论保障的小样本到达方向估计器，该估计器在高斯和拉普拉斯噪声下分别达到最大似然最优。仿真与真实数据实验验证了其在少量样本场景下的准确性和鲁棒性。
A projection-based framework for gradient-free and parallel learning
Authors: Andreas Bergmeister, Manish Krishan Lal, Stefanie Jegelka, Suvrit Sra
First: 2025-06-06T08:44:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:18:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a feasibility-seeking approach to neural network training. This mathematical optimization framework is distinct from conventional gradient-based loss minimization and uses projection operators and iterative projection algorithms. We reformulate training as a large-scale feasibility problem: finding network parameters and states that satisfy local constraints derived from its elementary operations. Training then involves projecting onto these constraints, a local operation that can be parallelized across the network. We introduce PJAX, a JAX-based software framework that enables this paradigm. PJAX composes projection operators for elementary operations, automatically deriving the solution operators for the feasibility problems (akin to autodiff for derivatives). It inherently supports GPU/TPU acceleration, provides a familiar NumPy-like API, and is extensible. We train diverse architectures (MLPs, CNNs, RNNs) on standard benchmarks using PJAX, demonstrating its functionality and generality. Our results show that this approach is a compelling alternative to gradient-based training, with clear advantages in parallelism and the ability to handle non-differentiable operations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种基于投影的无梯度并行学习框架
我们提出了一种用于神经网络训练的可行性搜索方法。该数学优化框架不同于传统的基于梯度的损失最小化方法，采用投影算子和迭代投影算法。我们将训练重新表述为一个大规模可行性问题：寻找满足由基本运算导出的局部约束的网络参数和状态。训练过程即为在这些约束上投影，这一局部操作可在网络中并行化。我们引入了PJAX，一个基于JAX的软件框架，支持该范式。PJAX为基本运算组合投影算子，自动推导可行性问题的解算子（类似于自动微分对导数的处理）。它原生支持GPU/TPU加速，提供熟悉的NumPy风格API，并具备可扩展性。我们使用PJAX在标准基准上训练多种架构（MLP、CNN、RNN），展示了其功能与通用性。结果表明，该方法是梯度训练的有力替代方案，在并行性和处理不可微操作方面具有明显优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of gradient-based training in handling non-differentiable operations and enabling parallel computation, this work proposes a projection-based framework that reformulates neural network training as a feasibility problem, where parameters must satisfy local constraints derived from elementary operations. The method uses iterative projection algorithms and automatically composes projection operators via a JAX-based framework called PJAX, which mimics automatic differentiation for constraints. Experimental results across MLPs, CNNs, and RNNs on standard benchmarks show that this gradient-free approach achieves comparable performance while offering inherent parallelism and GPU/TPU acceleration, making it a viable alternative to traditional gradient descent.
为克服梯度训练在处理非可微操作和并行化方面的局限性，该研究提出一种基于投影的框架，将神经网络训练重构为满足由基本操作导出的局部约束的可行性问题。方法采用迭代投影算法，并通过名为PJAX的JAX框架自动组合投影算子，其机制类似自动微分。在MLP、CNN和RNN等架构的标准基准测试中，实验表明该无梯度方法性能可比，同时具备天然并行性和对GPU/TPU加速的良好支持。
FedPF: Accurate Target Privacy Preserving Federated Learning Balancing Fairness and Utility
Authors: Kangkang Sun, Jun Wu, Minyi Guo, Jianhua Li, Jianwei Huang
Venue: ICDCS conference 2026
First: 2025-10-30T07:14:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:16:42+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 4 figures, 33 conference, The paper has been accepted by ICDCS conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Federated Learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without data sharing, yet participants face a fundamental challenge, e.g., simultaneously ensuring fairness across demographic groups while protecting sensitive client data. We introduce a differentially private fair FL algorithm (FedPF) that transforms this multi-objective optimization into a zero-sum game where fairness and privacy constraints compete against model utility. Our theoretical analysis reveals an inverse relationship: privacy mechanisms that protect sensitive attributes can reduce the statistical power available for detecting and correcting demographic biases under finite samples in federated settings. We further show that our theoretical bounds are consistent with a non-monotonic fairness-utility relationship, which is empirically validated by experiments where moderate fairness constraints improve generalization before excessive enforcement degrades performance. Compared with mainstream algorithms, even under strict privacy constraints, FedPF still maintains the lowest discrimination level among all tested algorithms while retaining high utility. Experimental validation demonstrates up to 42.9 % discrimination reduction across three datasets while maintaining competitive accuracy, but more importantly, reveals that achieving strong privacy and fairness simultaneously requires carefully balanced tradeoffs rather than optimizing either objective in isolation. Furthermore, hardware-level simulations demonstrate that FedPF maintains a low computational footprint, making it suitable for resource-constrained edge devices. The source code for our proposed algorithm is publicly accessible at https://github.com/szpsunkk/FedPF.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FedPF：准确的隐私保护联邦学习算法，在公平性与实用性之间取得平衡
联邦学习（FL）允许在不共享数据的情况下协同训练模型，但参与者面临根本性挑战，例如同时确保各人口群体间的公平性并保护敏感客户端数据。我们提出一种差分隐私公平联邦学习算法（FedPF），将多目标优化转化为零和博弈，其中公平性与隐私约束与模型效用相互竞争。理论分析揭示出一种反向关系：保护敏感属性的隐私机制可能在联邦设置下，有限样本条件下降低检测和纠正人口偏见的统计效力。我们进一步表明，理论界限与非单调的公平性-效用关系一致，实验验证了适度的公平约束可提升泛化能力，而过度强制则会降低性能。与主流算法相比，即使在严格隐私约束下，FedPF仍保持所有测试算法中最低的歧视水平，同时保持高效用。实验验证显示，在三个数据集上最多可降低42.9%的歧视，同时保持竞争力的准确率；更重要的是，揭示了同时实现强隐私与公平性需要精心平衡的权衡，而非孤立优化任一目标。此外，硬件级仿真表明FedPF计算开销低，适用于资源受限的边缘设备。该算法源代码公开于https://github.com/szpsunkk/FedPF。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to balance fairness and privacy in federated learning while preserving model utility, FedPF introduces a differentially private algorithm that frames the multi-objective optimization as a zero-sum game between fairness, privacy, and utility. Theoretical analysis shows that privacy mechanisms can reduce statistical power for detecting demographic biases under finite samples, revealing a non-monotonic relationship between fairness and utility. Experiments across three datasets demonstrate that FedPF achieves up to 42.9% reduction in discrimination while maintaining competitive accuracy, outperforming mainstream algorithms in fairness under strict privacy constraints, and exhibits low computational overhead suitable for edge devices.
为在联邦学习中平衡公平性、隐私性和模型效用，FedPF提出一种差分隐私公平联邦学习算法，将多目标优化问题建模为公平性与隐私性对模型效用进行零和博弈。理论分析表明，隐私机制会降低有限样本下检测和纠正群体偏倚的统计能力，揭示公平性与效用的非单调关系。实验显示，FedPF在三个数据集上实现最高42.9%的歧视度下降，同时保持较高准确率，并在严格隐私约束下优于主流算法，且硬件模拟表明其计算开销低，适合边缘设备部署。
Reasoning Gets Harder for LLMs Inside A Dialogue
Authors: Ivan Kartáč, Mateusz Lango, Ondřej Dušek
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-03-20T17:01:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:16:10+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ACL 2026 (Main)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong performance on many reasoning benchmarks, yet these evaluations typically focus on isolated tasks that differ from real-world usage in task-oriented dialogue (TOD). In this setting, LLMs must perform reasoning inherently while generating text and adhering to instructions on role, format, and style. This mismatch raises concerns about whether benchmark performance accurately reflects models' reasoning robustness in TOD setting. We investigate how framing reasoning tasks within TOD affects LLM performance by introducing BOULDER, a new dynamic benchmark covering eight travel-related tasks that require arithmetic, spatial, and temporal reasoning with both commonsense and formal aspects. Each problem is presented in both isolated and dialogue-based variants, enabling controlled comparison while mitigating data contamination. Experiments on eight LLMs reveal a substantial and consistent performance gap between isolated and dialogue settings. Through ablations and qualitative analysis, we show that this gap is largely driven by the multi-turn nature of dialogue, with additional effects from role conditioning and tool-use requirements. Our results highlight the need to evaluate LLM reasoning in realistic interactive scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在对话中，大语言模型的推理变得更困难
大语言模型（LLMs）在许多推理基准测试中表现优异，但这些评估通常聚焦于孤立任务，与任务导向对话（TOD）中的实际使用场景存在差异。在TOD场景中，LLMs必须在生成文本并遵循角色、格式和风格指令的同时进行推理。这种不匹配引发了对基准测试性能是否能准确反映模型在TOD场景中推理鲁棒性的担忧。我们通过引入BOULDER——一个涵盖八个需要算术、空间和时间推理的旅行相关动态基准（兼具常识与形式化方面）——研究在TOD框架下如何影响LLM表现。每个问题均提供孤立和对话式两种变体，以实现可控比较并避免数据污染。对八种LLM的实验表明，孤立与对话场景间存在显著且一致的性能差距。通过消融实验和定性分析，我们发现该差距主要由对话的多轮特性驱动，同时角色条件和工具使用需求也带来额外影响。我们的结果强调了在真实交互场景中评估LLM推理能力的必要性。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how large language models perform on reasoning tasks when embedded within task-oriented dialogue, contrasting their performance with isolated benchmarks. The authors introduce BOULDER, a dynamic dialogue-based benchmark with eight travel-related tasks requiring arithmetic, spatial, and temporal reasoning, each presented in both isolated and dialogue formats to enable controlled comparison. Experiments across eight LLMs reveal a consistent and significant performance drop in dialogue settings, attributed primarily to the multi-turn nature of dialogue, compounded by role conditioning and tool-use demands. The findings suggest that current reasoning benchmarks may not reliably capture LLMs’ reasoning robustness in real-world interactive contexts.
本研究探讨了大语言模型（LLM）在任务导向对话（TOD）场景中进行推理时的表现，与孤立任务基准进行对比。作者提出了BOULDER，一个包含八个旅游相关任务的动态对话基准，这些任务需进行算术、空间和时间推理，每项任务同时提供孤立和对话两种形式以实现可控对比。在八种LLM上的实验表明，对话场景下性能显著下降，主要归因于多轮对话特性，辅以角色设定和工具使用需求。结果强调当前推理基准可能无法真实反映LLM在交互式环境中的推理能力。
Virtual-reality based patient-specific simulation of spine surgical procedures: A fast, highly automated and high-fidelity system for surgical education and planning
Authors: Raj Kumar Ranabhat, Tayler D Ross, Tony Jiao, Jeremie Larouche, Joel Finkelstein, Michael Hardisty
First: 2026-04-29T15:12:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:12:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Surgical training involves didactic teaching, mentor-led learning, surgical skills laboratories, and direct exposure to surgery; however, increasing clinical pressures have limited operating room (OR) exposure. This work leverages virtual reality (VR) to provide a safe and immersive training environment. Existing VR training is often based on standardized scenarios not tailored to individual clinical cases. This study addresses this limitation using artificial intelligence (AI) based computer vision methods to generate patient-specific simulations from computed tomography (CT) and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI). This study focuses on patient-specific spinal decompression simulation for spinal stenosis in a virtual operating room. The objectives were (1) automatic creation of 3D anatomical models and (2) VR simulation of spinal decompression procedures including laminectomy, disc resection, and foraminotomy. Model construction required multimodal fusion (registration) of CT and MRI and segmentation of relevant structures. Segmentation was evaluated using the Dice Similarity Coefficient (DSC), and registration accuracy using Target Registration Error (TRE). Qualitative feedback was obtained from surgeons and trainees. High-fidelity patient-specific 3D models were generated efficiently (approximately 2.5 minutes per case, N = 15). Segmentation accuracy was high, with a DSC of 0.95 (+/- 0.03) for vertebral bone and 0.895 (+/- 0.02) for soft tissue structures. Registration accuracy showed a mean TRE of 1.73 (+/- 0.42) mm. Semi-structured interviews indicated improved spatial understanding, increased procedural confidence, and strong perceived educational value. This platform significantly reduced the time and costs of patient-specific modelling, thereby facilitating pre-operative planning, post-procedural assessments, and comprehensive surgical simulation.

CommFuse: Hiding Tail Latency via Communication Decomposition and Fusion for Distributed LLM Training
Authors: Rezaul Karim, Austin Wen, Wang Zongzuo, Weiwei Zhang, Yang Liu, Walid Ahmed
First: 2026-04-27T03:48:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:12:16+00:00
Comments: Slightly modified the title, and corresponding minor wording change in the content
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid growth in the size of large language models has necessitated the partitioning of computational workloads across accelerators such as GPUs, TPUs, and NPUs. However, these parallelization strategies incur substantial data communication overhead significantly hindering computational efficiency. While communication-computation overlap presents a promising direction, existing data slicing based solutions suffer from tail latency. To overcome this limitation, this research introduces a novel communication-computation overlap technique to eliminate this tail latency in state of the art overlap methods for distributed LLM training. The aim of this technique is to effectively mitigate communication bottleneck of tensor parallelism and data parallelism for distributed training and inference. In particular, we propose a novel method termed CommFuse that replaces conventional collective operations of reduce-scatter and all-gather with decomposed peer-to-peer (P2P) communication and schedules partitioned computations to enable fine-grained overlap. Our method provides an exact algorithm for reducing communication overhead that eliminates tail latency. Moreover, it presents a versatile solution compatible with data-parallel training and various tensor-level parallelism strategies, including TPSP and UP. Experimental evaluations demonstrate that our technique consistently achieves lower latency, superior Model FLOPS Utilization (MFU), and high throughput.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CommFuse：通过通信分解与融合隐藏分布式LLM训练中的尾部延迟
大型语言模型规模的快速增长要求将计算任务分布在GPU、TPU和NPU等加速器上。然而，这些并行化策略会带来显著的数据通信开销，严重阻碍计算效率。尽管通信与计算重叠是一种有前景的方向，但现有基于数据切片的解决方案仍存在尾部延迟问题。为克服这一局限，本研究提出了一种新颖的通信-计算重叠技术，旨在消除分布式LLM训练中现有最先进重叠方法的尾部延迟。该技术旨在有效缓解分布式训练和推理中张量并行与数据并行的通信瓶颈。具体而言，我们提出了一种名为CommFuse的新方法，用分解的点对点（P2P）通信替代传统的reduce-scatter和all-gather集体操作，并调度分片计算以实现细粒度重叠。该方法提供了一种精确算法，可降低通信开销并消除尾部延迟。此外，它还提供了一种通用解决方案，兼容数据并行训练及多种张量级并行策略，包括TPSP和UP。实验评估表明，该技术持续实现更低延迟、更优的模型FLOPS利用率（MFU）和高吞吐量。

Experimental results show that CommFuse consistently reduces latency, improves Model FLOPS Utilization (MFU), and boosts throughput across various parallelism strategies including data parallelism and tensor parallelism variants.
为提升分布式加速器上大语言模型训练效率，该研究针对通信瓶颈导致的性能下降问题，提出CommFuse方法，通过将reduce-scatter和all-gather等传统集体通信替换为分解的点对点通信，并调度分片计算以实现细粒度重叠，从而消除尾延迟。实验表明，该方法在数据并行和张量并行训练中均能稳定降低延迟、提升模型FLOPS利用率（MFU）并提高吞吐量。
Accelerating RL Post-Training Rollouts via System-Integrated Speculative Decoding
Authors: Hayate Iso, Tiyasa Mitra, Sudipta Mondal, Rasoul Shafipour, Venmugil Elango, Terry Kong, Yuki Huang, Seonjin Na, Izzy Putterman, Benjamin Chislett, Maor Ashkenazi, Joseph Guman, Gerald Shen, Tugrul Konuk, Ashwath Aithal, Ritika Borkar, Ran Zilberstein, Bita Rouhani
First: 2026-04-29T15:11:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:11:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
RL post-training of frontier language models is increasingly bottlenecked by autoregressive rollout generation, making rollout acceleration a central systems challenge. Many existing efficiency methods improve throughput by changing the rollout or optimization regime, for example, through off-policy execution, replay, or lower-precision generation. We study speculative decoding as a lossless acceleration primitive for RL rollouts that preserves the target model's output distribution. We implement speculative decoding in NeMo-RL with a vLLM backend, supporting both synchronous and asynchronous pipelines and enabling speculation during RL rollouts. This benefit is realizable across speculation mechanisms, such as pretrained MTP heads, small external draft models or even techniques such as Eagle3, which are traditionally applied after RL phase. This yields a deployment path for state-of-the-art speculative decoding inside RL training. In a reasoning post-training workload at 8B scale under synchronous RL, speculative decoding improves rollout throughput by 1.8x. Using a high-fidelity performance simulator, we project that combining speculative decoding with asynchronous RL yields up to 2.5x end-to-end training speedup at 235B scale.
