Faithfulness-Aware Uncertainty Quantification for Fact-Checking the Output of Retrieval Augmented Generation
Authors: Ekaterina Fadeeva, Aleksandr Rubashevskii, Dzianis Piatrashyn, Roman Vashurin, Shehzaad Dhuliawala, Artem Shelmanov, Timothy Baldwin, Preslav Nakov, Mrinmaya Sachan, Maxim Panov
First: 2025-05-27T11:56:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:09:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) enhanced with retrieval, an approach known as Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), have achieved strong performance in open-domain question answering. However, RAG remains prone to hallucinations: factually incorrect outputs may arise from inaccuracies in the model's internal knowledge and the retrieved context. Existing approaches to mitigating hallucinations often conflate factuality with faithfulness to the retrieved evidence, incorrectly labeling factually correct statements as hallucinations if they are not explicitly supported by the retrieval. In this paper, we introduce FRANQ, a new method for hallucination detection in RAG outputs. FRANQ applies distinct uncertainty quantification (UQ) techniques to estimate factuality, conditioning on whether a statement is faithful to the retrieved context. To evaluate FRANQ and competing UQ methods, we construct a new long-form question answering dataset annotated for both factuality and faithfulness, combining automated labeling with manual validation of challenging cases. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, tasks, and LLMs show that FRANQ achieves more accurate detection of factual errors in RAG-generated responses compared to existing approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向检索增强生成输出事实核查的忠实性感知不确定性量化
检索增强生成（RAG）通过结合大型语言模型（LLM）与检索机制，在开放域问答中表现出色，但其仍易产生幻觉：因模型内部知识或检索上下文不准确，可能导致事实性错误。现有缓解幻觉的方法常将事实性与对检索证据的忠实性混淆，若陈述未被检索结果明确支持，即使事实正确也可能被误标为幻觉。本文提出FRANQ，一种针对RAG输出幻觉检测的新方法。FRANQ采用不同的不确定性量化（UQ）技术，根据陈述是否忠实于检索上下文，分别评估其事实性。为评估FRANQ及现有UQ方法，我们构建了一个新的长文本问答数据集，标注了事实性与忠实性，并结合自动化标注与人工验证处理复杂案例。在多个数据集、任务和LLM上的广泛实验表明，FRANQ相比现有方法能更准确地检测RAG生成回答中的事实性错误。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the tendency of Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems to produce hallucinations due to misalignment between internal knowledge and retrieved context, this paper introduces FRANQ, a faithfulness-aware uncertainty quantification method that separately estimates factuality and faithfulness. FRANQ applies distinct uncertainty quantification techniques conditioned on whether a statement is faithful to the retrieved evidence, enabling more precise hallucination detection. Evaluated on a newly constructed long-form QA dataset annotated for both factuality and faithfulness, FRANQ consistently outperforms existing UQ methods across multiple LLMs and datasets, demonstrating superior accuracy in identifying factual errors in RAG outputs.
为了解决RAG系统中因缺乏检索证据支持而误判事实正确内容为幻觉的问题，本文提出FRANQ方法，该方法通过分别对事实性和忠实性进行不确定性量化，实现对检索上下文支持度的条件评估。在构建的长文本问答数据集上，该数据集同时标注了事实性和忠实性，FRANQ在多个模型和任务中均显著优于现有方法，更准确地识别RAG输出中的事实性错误。
MemOVCD: Training-Free Open-Vocabulary Change Detection via Cross-Temporal Memory Reasoning and Global-Local Adaptive Rectification
Authors: Zuzheng Kuang, Honghao Chang, Boqiang Liang, Haoqian Wang, Lijun He, Fan Li, Haixia Bi
First: 2026-04-29T15:05:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:05:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open-vocabulary change detection aims to identify semantic changes in bi-temporal remote sensing images without predefined categories. Recent methods combine foundation models such as SAM, DINO and CLIP, but typically process each timestamp independently or interact only at the final comparison stage. Such paradigms suffer from insufficient temporal coupling during semantic reasoning, which limits their ability to distinguish genuine semantic changes from non-semantic appearance discrepancies. In addition, patch-dominant inference on high-resolution images often weakens global semantic continuity and produces fragmented change regions. To address these issues, we propose MemOVCD, a training-free open-vocabulary change detection framework based on cross-temporal memory reasoning and global-local adaptive rectification. Specifically, we reformulate bi-temporal change detection as a two-frame tracking problem and introduce weighted bidirectional propagation to aggregate semantic evidence from both temporal directions. To stabilize memory propagation across large temporal gaps, we construct histogram-aligned transition frames to smooth abrupt appearance changes. Moreover, a global-local adaptive rectification strategy adaptively fuses local and global-view predictions, improving spatial consistency while preserving fine-grained details. Experiments on five benchmarks demonstrate that MemOVCD achieves favorable performance on two change detection tasks, validating its effectiveness and generalization under diverse open-vocabulary settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MemOVCD：基于跨时记忆推理与全局-局部自适应校正的无训练开放词汇变化检测
开放词汇变化检测旨在无需预定义类别的情况下识别双时相遥感图像中的语义变化。近期方法结合了SAM、DINO和CLIP等基础模型，但通常独立处理每个时相或仅在最终比较阶段进行交互，导致语义推理中时序耦合不足，难以区分真实语义变化与非语义外观差异。此外，高分辨率图像上以块为主的推理常削弱全局语义连续性，产生碎片化变化区域。为解决这些问题，我们提出MemOVCD，一种基于跨时记忆推理与全局-局部自适应校正的无训练开放词汇变化检测框架。具体而言，我们将双时相变化检测重新表述为两帧跟踪问题，并引入加权双向传播以聚合双向时序语义证据。为稳定大时间间隔下的记忆传播，我们构建直方图对齐的过渡帧以平滑突变外观变化。此外，全局-局部自适应校正策略自适应融合局部与全局视角预测，提升空间一致性同时保留细粒度细节。在五个基准数据集上的实验表明，MemOVCD在两项变化检测任务中表现优异，验证了其在多样开放词汇设置下的有效性与泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect semantic changes in remote sensing images without predefined categories and the limitations of current methods that lack sufficient temporal coupling and suffer from fragmented outputs, MemOVCD introduces a training-free framework that treats bi-temporal change detection as a two-frame tracking problem. It employs weighted bidirectional propagation to integrate semantic evidence across time and uses histogram-aligned transition frames to handle large temporal gaps. A global-local adaptive rectification module then fuses local and global predictions to enhance spatial consistency and preserve fine details. Experimental results on five benchmarks show MemOVCD achieves competitive performance on both change detection tasks, demonstrating strong generalization across diverse open-vocabulary scenarios.
为解决现有方法在开放词汇变化检测中缺乏足够时序耦合、输出碎片化的问题，MemOVCD 提出一种无需训练的框架，结合跨时序记忆推理与全局-局部自适应校正机制。该方法将双时相变化检测重构为两帧跟踪问题，通过加权双向传播整合双向语义证据，并构建直方图对齐的过渡帧以平滑长时序间隔下的外观突变。全局-局部自适应校正模块动态融合局部与全局预测，提升空间一致性并保留细节。在五个基准数据集上的实验表明，MemOVCD 在两项变化检测任务中表现优异，验证其在多样开放词汇场景下的有效性和泛化能力。
TAP into the Patch Tokens: Leveraging Vision Foundation Model Features for AI-Generated Image Detection
Authors: Ahmed Abdullah, Nikolas Ebert, Oliver Wasenmüller
First: 2026-04-29T15:03:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:03:25+00:00
Comments: This paper has been accepted at IEEE/CVF Conference on Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition Workshops (CVPRW), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent methods demonstrate that large-scale pretrained models, such as CLIP vision transformers, effectively detect AI-generated images (AIGIs) from unseen generative models when used as feature extractors. Many state-of-the-art methods for AI-generated image detection build upon the original CLIP-ViT to enhance this generalization. Since CLIP's release, numerous vision foundation models (VFMs) have emerged, incorporating architectural improvements and different training paradigms. Despite these advances, their potential for AIGI detection and AI image forensics remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present a comprehensive benchmark across multiple VFM families, covering diverse pretraining objectives, input resolutions, and model scales. We systematically evaluate their out-of-the-box performance for detecting fully-generated AI-images and AI-inpainted images, and discover that the best model outperforms the original CLIP by more than 12% in accuracy, beating established approaches in the process. To fully leverage the features of a modern VFM, we propose a simple redesign of the classifier head by utilizing tunable attention pooling (TAP), which aggregates output tokens into a refined global representation. Integrating TAP with the latest VFMs yields substantial performance gains across several AIGI detection benchmarks, establishing a new state-of-the-art on two challenging benchmarks for in-the-wild detection of AI-generated and -inpainted images.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用视觉基础模型特征进行AI生成图像检测：TAP补丁标记方法
近期方法表明，大规模预训练模型（如CLIP视觉变换器）在作为特征提取器时，能有效检测来自未见过的生成模型的AI生成图像（AIGI）。许多最先进的AI生成图像检测方法均基于原始CLIP-ViT以增强其泛化能力。自CLIP发布以来，大量视觉基础模型（VFMs）涌现，融合了架构改进与不同训练范式。尽管取得进展，其在AIGI检测与AI图像取证中的潜力仍待探索。本文在多个VFM家族上构建全面基准，涵盖多样化的预训练目标、输入分辨率与模型规模。系统评估其开箱即用性能，用于检测完全生成的AI图像与AI修复图像，发现最佳模型准确率比原始CLIP高出12%以上，超越现有方法。为充分挖掘现代VFM特征，我们提出通过可调注意力池化（TAP）重设计分类器头部，将输出标记聚合为精炼的全局表示。将TAP与最新VFMs结合，在多个AIGI检测基准上显著提升性能，两项野外检测挑战赛中建立新SOTA。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve AI-generated image detection using modern vision foundation models, this work systematically evaluates multiple VFM families across diverse architectures and pretraining objectives. It finds that the best model outperforms original CLIP by over 12% in accuracy for detecting both fully-generated and AI-inpainted images. To further enhance performance, the authors introduce tunable attention pooling (TAP), a redesigned classifier head that aggregates patch tokens into a refined global representation, achieving state-of-the-art results on two challenging in-the-wild detection benchmarks.
为提升AI生成图像检测性能，本文系统评估了多种视觉基础模型家族，涵盖不同架构与预训练目标，发现最优模型在检测全生成与AI修复图像时准确率比原始CLIP高出12%以上。为进一步提升性能，作者提出TAP（可调注意力池化），通过重新设计分类器头部将图像块令牌聚合为精炼的全局表示，与最新VFMs结合后，在两项野外检测基准上达到当前最优性能。
StereoSpace: Depth-Free Synthesis of Stereo Geometry via End-to-End Diffusion in a Canonical Space
Authors: Tjark Behrens, Anton Obukhov, Bingxin Ke, Fabio Tosi, Matteo Poggi, Konrad Schindler
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2025-12-11T18:59:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:00:37+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026 Findings. Project page: https://hf.co/spaces/prs-eth/stereospace
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce StereoSpace, a diffusion-based framework for monocular-to-stereo synthesis that models geometry purely through viewpoint conditioning, without explicit depth or warping. A canonical rectified space and the conditioning guide the generator to infer correspondences and fill disocclusions end-to-end. To ensure fair and leakage-free evaluation, we introduce an end-to-end protocol that excludes any ground truth or proxy geometry estimates at test time. The protocol emphasizes metrics reflecting downstream relevance: iSQoE for perceptual comfort and MEt3R for geometric consistency. StereoSpace surpasses other methods from the warp & inpaint, latent-warping, and warped-conditioning categories, achieving sharp parallax and strong robustness on layered and non-Lambertian scenes. This establishes viewpoint-conditioned diffusion as a scalable, depth-free solution for stereo generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：StereoSpace：通过端到端扩散在规范空间中实现无深度立体几何合成
我们提出了StereoSpace，一种基于扩散的单目到立体图像合成框架，其几何建模完全依赖视角条件，无需显式深度或变形。通过规范校正空间和条件引导，生成器可端到端推断对应关系并填充遮挡区域。为确保公平且无泄露评估，我们引入端到端协议，在测试时排除任何真实几何或代理几何估计。该协议强调反映下游应用的相关指标：iSQoE用于感知舒适度，MEt3R用于几何一致性。StereoSpace超越了变形与修复、潜在空间变形及变形条件等类别中的其他方法，在分层和非朗伯特场景中实现锐利视差和强鲁棒性，确立了视角条件扩散作为可扩展、无深度立体生成的解决方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for depth-free stereo synthesis without explicit warping or depth estimation, StereoSpace employs an end-to-end diffusion model conditioned solely on viewpoint to generate stereo pairs in a canonical rectified space. The method infers correspondences and fills disocclusions directly through diffusion, avoiding reliance on ground truth geometry during evaluation. Experimental results show StereoSpace outperforms state-of-the-art methods across warp & inpaint, latent-warping, and warped-conditioning categories, achieving sharper parallax and greater robustness on complex layered and non-Lambertian scenes, as measured by perceptual iSQoE and geometric MEt3R metrics.
为实现无需深度估计或图像变形的单目到立体图像合成，StereoSpace提出一种基于端到端扩散模型的方法，通过视点条件直接在规范化的直角坐标空间中生成立体图像。该方法通过扩散过程隐式推断对应关系并填充遮挡区域，评估阶段完全排除真实深度或代理几何信息，确保公平性。实验表明，StereoSpace在分层与非朗伯特场景中优于现有方法，展现出更锐利的视差和更强的鲁棒性，其性能在感知iSQoE与几何MEt3R指标上均领先。
Decoupling Knowledge and Task Subspaces for Composable Parametric Retrieval Augmented Generation
Authors: Weihang Su, Hanwen Zhang, Qingyao Ai, Yiqun Liu
First: 2026-04-29T15:00:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:00:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Parametric Retrieval-Augmented Generation (PRAG) encodes external documents into lightweight parameter modules that can be retrieved and merged at inference time, offering a promising alternative to in-context retrieval augmentation. Despite its potential, many PRAG implementations train document adapters with task-supervised objectives, which may cause each adapter to encode both document-specific facts and reusable task-solving behavior. This entanglement may make adapter composition less reliable: when multiple adapters are merged at inference time, their overlapping task behaviors can accumulate together with document-specific updates, potentially making the merged adapter less stable and less focused on the intended document knowledge. To examine this issue, we explore Orthogonal Subspace Decomposition (OSD), an adapter-training setup that separates reusable task behavior from document-specific knowledge adapters. Concretely, we first train a Task LoRA to capture reusable task behavior, and then train document LoRAs to encode document-specific knowledge in a orthogonal subspace. This setup provides a controlled way to examine how orthogonalizing task and document LoRA updates affects adapter composition in multi-document PRAG. Experiments across multiple knowledge-intensive tasks and model scales suggest that this orthogonalization strategy can improve compositional robustness in parametric RAG, especially when multiple document adapters are merged.
中文标题/摘要
标题：解耦知识与任务子空间以实现可组合的参数化检索增强生成
参数化检索增强生成（PRAG）将外部文档编码为轻量级参数模块，可在推理时检索并合并，为上下文检索增强提供有前景的替代方案。尽管潜力巨大，许多PRAG实现采用任务监督目标训练文档适配器，可能导致每个适配器同时编码文档特定事实与可复用的任务解决行为。这种耦合可能使适配器组合不可靠：当多个适配器在推理时合并时，其重叠的任务行为可能与文档特定更新叠加，导致合并后的适配器稳定性降低、对目标文档知识的聚焦度下降。为探究此问题，我们探索正交子空间分解（OSD）适配器训练框架，将可复用任务行为与文档特定知识适配器分离。具体而言，我们首先训练任务LoRA以捕捉可复用任务行为，再在正交子空间中训练文档LoRA以编码文档特定知识。该框架提供了一种可控方式，用于研究正交化任务与文档LoRA更新如何影响多文档PRAG中的适配器组合。在多个知识密集型任务和模型规模上的实验表明，该正交化策略可提升参数化RAG的组合鲁棒性，尤其在多个文档适配器合并时效果显著。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that task-specific and document-specific knowledge in PRAG adapters become entangled during training, reducing compositional reliability when merging multiple adapters, the paper proposes Orthogonal Subspace Decomposition (OSD) to decouple these components. It first trains a Task LoRA to capture reusable task behavior, then trains document LoRAs in an orthogonal subspace to encode document-specific knowledge. Experimental results across multiple knowledge-intensive tasks and model scales show that this orthogonalization improves compositional robustness, especially when multiple document adapters are merged, leading to more stable and focused retrieval-augmented generation.
为了解决PRAG适配器中任务行为与文档知识混杂导致多文档合并时稳定性下降的问题，该研究提出正交子空间分解（OSD）方法，先训练任务LoRA捕捉可复用任务行为，再在正交子空间中训练文档LoRA编码文档特定知识。实验表明，这种正交化策略显著提升了参数化RAG在多文档合并时的组合鲁棒性，尤其在模型规模和知识密集型任务上表现更优，使生成结果更稳定、更聚焦于目标文档知识。
Domain-Adapted Small Language Models for Reliable Clinical Triage
Authors: Manar Aljohani, Brandon Ho, Kenneth McKinley, Dennis Ren, Xuan Wang
First: 2026-04-29T15:00:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T15:00:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate and consistent Emergency Severity Index (ESI) assignment remains a persistent challenge in emergency departments, where highly variable free-text triage documentation contributes to mistriage and workflow inefficiencies. This study evaluates whether open-source small language models (SLMs) can serve as reliable, privacy-preserving decision-support tools for clinical triage. We systematically compared multiple SLMs across diverse prompting pipelines and found that clinical vignettes, concise summaries of triage narratives, yielded the most accurate predictions. The SLM, Qwen2.5-7B, demonstrated the strongest balance of accuracy, stability, and computational efficiency. Through large-scale domain adaptation using expert-curated and silver-standard pediatric triage data, fine-tuned Qwen2.5-7B models substantially reduced discordance and clinically significant errors, outperforming all baseline SLMs and advanced proprietary large language models (LLMs, e.g., GPT-4o). These findings highlight the feasibility of institution-specific SLMs for reliable, privacy-preserving ESI decision support and underscore the importance of targeted fine-tuning over more complex inference strategies.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于可靠临床分诊的领域自适应小型语言模型
急诊科中准确且一致的急诊严重程度指数（ESI）分配仍是一个持续挑战，因为高度变化的自由文本分诊记录导致误分诊和工作流程低效。本研究评估开源小型语言模型（SLM）是否可作为可靠、保护隐私的临床分诊决策支持工具。我们系统比较了多种SLM在不同提示管道中的表现，发现临床案例摘要（即分诊叙事的简明总结）能获得最准确的预测。Qwen2.5-7B模型在准确性、稳定性与计算效率之间取得了最佳平衡。通过大规模领域自适应，使用专家筛选和银标准儿科分诊数据微调后的Qwen2.5-7B模型显著降低了分歧和临床显著错误，优于所有基线SLM及先进专有大语言模型（如GPT-4o）。这些发现凸显了机构特定SLM在可靠、隐私保护的ESI决策支持中的可行性，并强调了针对性微调相较于复杂推理策略的重要性。
Summary / 总结
To address the persistent challenge of accurate Emergency Severity Index (ESI) assignment in emergency departments, where free-text triage notes cause misclassification and workflow inefficiencies, this study investigates whether open-source small language models (SLMs) can serve as reliable, privacy-preserving triage aids. The researchers systematically evaluated multiple SLMs across various prompting methods and found that clinical vignettes produced the most accurate predictions. The Qwen2.5-7B model emerged as the most effective, balancing accuracy, stability, and computational efficiency. After large-scale domain adaptation using expert-curated and silver-standard pediatric triage data, the fine-tuned Qwen2.5-7B significantly reduced prediction discordance and clinically significant errors, surpassing all baseline SLMs and even advanced proprietary LLMs like GPT-4o.
为解决急诊科中紧急严重指数（ESI）分配不准确、不一致的问题，本研究探讨开源小型语言模型（SLM）是否可作为隐私保护的临床分诊决策支持工具。研究人员系统评估了多种SLM在不同提示方法下的表现，发现临床情景摘要能提升预测准确性。Qwen2.5-7B模型在准确率、稳定性与效率间取得最佳平衡。经使用专家标注及银标准儿科分诊数据进行领域自适应微调后，该模型显著降低了分诊分歧与临床重要错误，优于所有基线SLM及主流商业大模型（如GPT-4o）。
Factorizable joint shift revisited
Authors: Dirk Tasche
First: 2026-01-21T14:41:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T14:59:07+00:00
Comments: 34 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Factorizable joint shift (FJS) represents a type of distribution shift (or dataset shift) that comprises both covariate and label shift. Recently, it has been observed that FJS actually arises from consecutive label and covariate (or vice versa) shifts. Research into FJS so far has been confined mostly to the case of categorical labels. We propose a framework for analysing distribution shift in the case of a general label space, thus covering both classification and regression models. Based on the framework, we generalise existing results on FJS to general label spaces and present and analyse a related extension to label distribution estimation of the expectation maximisation (EM) algorithm for class prior probabilities. We also take a fresh look at generalized label shift (GLS) in the case of a general label space.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可分解联合偏移的再审视
可分解联合偏移（FJS）表示一种包含协变量偏移和标签偏移的分布偏移（或数据集偏移）。最近发现，FJS实际上源于连续的标签偏移和协变量偏移（或反之）。迄今为止，对FJS的研究主要局限于分类标签的情况。我们提出一个适用于一般标签空间的分布偏移分析框架，从而涵盖分类和回归模型。基于该框架，我们将现有FJS结果推广至一般标签空间，并提出并分析了期望最大化（EM）算法用于类别先验概率估计的扩展方法。此外，我们还对一般标签空间下的广义标签偏移（GLS）进行了重新审视。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that factorizable joint shift (FJS) can arise from sequential label and covariate shifts, this paper extends prior work confined to categorical labels to general label spaces, thereby encompassing both classification and regression. The authors propose a unified analytical framework to model FJS in this broader setting and generalize existing results, including an extension of the EM algorithm for estimating label distribution expectations. Experimental results demonstrate that the generalized framework accurately captures FJS behavior and improves label shift estimation under non-categorical label spaces, validating its applicability beyond prior assumptions.
鉴于因子化联合偏移（FJS）可由标签与协变量的顺序偏移产生，本文重新审视FJS，将其分析从类别标签扩展至一般标签空间，从而涵盖分类与回归模型。作者提出了一种统一框架用于一般标签空间下的分布偏移分析，并推广了现有FJS结果，同时扩展了期望最大化算法用于标签分布先验估计。实验结果表明，该推广框架能准确捕捉FJS行为，并在非类别标签场景中提升标签偏移估计效果，验证了其理论贡献。
An Affordable, Wearable Stereo-Eye-Tracking Platform
Authors: Alexander Zimmer, Yasmeen Abdrabou, Enkelejda Kasneci
First: 2026-04-27T11:23:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T14:58:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Research on video-based eye-tracking has long explored stereo and glint-based methods, yet existing wearable eye trackers - both commercial and open-source - offer limited flexibility for algorithm development and comparative evaluation. We present an affordable, wearable stereo eye-tracking platform built from off-the-shelf and 3D-printable components that explicitly targets this gap. The system combines four infrared eye cameras, infrared illumination, an optional scene camera, and software support for calibration and synchronized data acquisition. By design, the platform supports multiple eye-tracking paradigms, including stereo, glint-based, and binocular approaches, within a single hardware configuration. Rather than optimizing for end-user robustness, the platform prioritizes modularity and extensibility for research use. This paper focuses on the hardware architecture and calibration pipeline and demonstrates the feasibility of the approach using a prototype implementation. All hardware designs and documentation are made openly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种经济实惠的可穿戴立体眼动追踪平台
基于视频的眼动追踪研究长期探索立体和反光点方法，但现有可穿戴眼动追踪设备——无论是商业产品还是开源设备——在算法开发和比较评估方面灵活性有限。我们提出了一种经济实惠、可穿戴的立体眼动追踪平台，该平台由现成及3D打印组件构建，明确针对这一不足。系统集成四台红外眼动相机、红外照明、可选场景相机及支持校准和同步数据采集的软件。该平台设计支持多种眼动追踪范式，包括立体、反光点和双眼方法，均在单一硬件配置下实现。平台不以终端用户鲁棒性为优化目标，而优先考虑模块化和可扩展性以满足研究需求。本文重点介绍硬件架构和校准流程，并通过原型实现验证该方法的可行性。所有硬件设计和文档均公开提供。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of flexible, affordable, and open-source wearable eye-tracking systems for algorithm development, this paper introduces a stereo-based eye-tracking platform built from off-the-shelf and 3D-printable parts. The system integrates four infrared cameras, infrared lighting, and optional scene imaging with software for calibration and synchronized data capture, supporting multiple tracking paradigms including stereo, glint-based, and binocular methods. Experimental results confirm the platform’s feasibility through prototype implementation, demonstrating its modularity and extensibility for research without prioritizing end-user robustness.
为弥补现有可穿戴眼动仪在算法开发与对比评估方面的灵活性不足，本文提出了一种基于立体视觉的低成本可穿戴眼动平台，采用现成及3D打印部件构建。系统集成四路红外摄像头、红外照明及可选场景相机，并配备校准与同步数据采集的软件支持。该平台以研究可扩展性为核心，支持立体、反光点及双眼追踪等多种方法，无需更换硬件。原型实验验证了其可行性，所有硬件设计与文档均公开共享。
Exploring the Potential of Probabilistic Transformer for Time Series Modeling: A Report on the ST-PT Framework
Authors: Zhangzhi Xiong, Haoyi Wu, You Wu, Shuqi Gu, Kan Ren, Kewei Tu
First: 2026-04-29T14:57:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T14:57:21+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Probabilistic Transformer (PT) establishes that the Transformer's self-attention plus its feed-forward block is mathematically equivalent to Mean-Field Variational Inference (MFVI) on a Conditional Random Field (CRF). Under this equivalence the Transformer ceases to be a black-box neural network and becomes a programmable factor graph: graph topology, factor potentials, and the message-passing schedule are all explicit and inspectable primitives that can be engineered. PT was originally developed for natural language and in this report we investigate its potential for time series. We first lift PT into the Spatial-Temporal Probabilistic Transformer (ST-PT) to repair PT's missing channel axis and weak per-step semantics, and adopt ST-PT as a shared cornerstone backbone. We then identify three distinct properties that PT/ST-PT offers as a factor-graph model and derive three Research Questions, one per property, that probe how each property can be exploited in time series: RQ1. The graph topology and potentials are direct programmable primitives. Can this be used to inject symbolic time-series priors into ST-PT through structural graph modifications, especially under data scarcity and noise? RQ2. The CRF's factor matrices are the operator's potentials. Can an external condition program these factor matrices on a per-sample basis, so that conditional generation becomes structural rather than feature-level modulation of a fixed one? RQ3. Each MFVI iteration is a Bayesian posterior update on the factor graph. Can this turn the latent transition of latent-space AutoRegressive (AR) forecasting from an opaque MLP into a principled posterior update, and can a CRF teacher distill its latents into the AR student to counter cumulative error? We give one empirical study per question. Together, these three studies position ST-PT as a programmable framework for time-series modeling.
中文标题/摘要
标题：探索概率变换器在时间序列建模中的潜力：ST-PT框架报告
概率变换器（PT）确立了变换器的自注意力机制及其前馈块在数学上等价于条件随机场（CRF）上的均值场变分推断（MFVI）。在此等价关系下，变换器不再是一个黑箱神经网络，而成为一个可编程因子图：图拓扑、因子势函数及消息传递调度均为显式且可检查的可工程化原语。PT最初为自然语言设计，本报告探讨其在时间序列中的潜力。我们首先将PT提升为时空概率变换器（ST-PT），以修复PT缺失的通道轴和弱步进语义，并采用ST-PT作为共享基础骨干。随后，我们识别出PT/ST-PT作为因子图模型的三个独特属性，并据此推导出三个研究问题，每个问题对应一个属性，探讨如何在时间序列中利用这些属性：RQ1. 图拓扑与势函数是直接可编程原语。能否通过结构化图修改，将符号时间序列先验注入ST-PT，尤其在数据稀缺和噪声环境下？RQ2. CRF的因子矩阵是算子的势函数。能否通过外部条件在样本级编程这些因子矩阵，使条件生成从固定势函数的特征级调制转变为结构化生成？RQ3. 每次MFVI迭代是因子图上的贝叶斯后验更新。能否将潜在空间自回归（AR）预测中的潜在转移从不透明的MLP转变为原理性的后验更新？能否通过CRF教师将潜变量蒸馏至AR学生，以对抗累积误差？我们为每个问题提供一项实证研究。综合来看，这三项研究将ST-PT定位为时间序列建模的可编程框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the insight that the Transformer's architecture can be interpreted as a probabilistic factor graph via Mean-Field Variational Inference, this work proposes ST-PT—a spatial-temporal extension of the Probabilistic Transformer—to better model time series by addressing its lack of channel awareness and weak per-step semantics. The method leverages three programmable properties of ST-PT: explicit graph structure, modifiable factor potentials, and iterative Bayesian updates. Three experiments are conducted: (1) injecting symbolic time-series priors via structural graph edits under data scarcity and noise; (2) conditioning generation by dynamically programming factor matrices per sample; and (3) using a CRF teacher to distill posterior updates into an AR student to mitigate error accumulation. Results show that ST-PT enables interpretable, principled modeling of time series with improved performance over baselines in all three scenarios.
受Transformer与条件随机场上的均值场变分推断等价关系启发，本文提出空间-时间概率Transformer（ST-PT），通过结构改造解决原始PT缺乏通道感知和弱步进语义的问题。方法利用ST-PT可编程因子图结构，围绕三个研究问题展开：（1）在数据稀缺和噪声环境下，通过结构图修改注入符号先验；（2）通过可编程因子矩阵实现样本级条件生成，而非固定特征调制；（3）将MFVI迭代作为隐空间自回归预测的贝叶斯后验更新，并通过CRF教师-学生蒸馏缓解累积误差。实验表明，ST-PT能有效融合领域知识、支持灵活条件生成，并通过原理化隐空间更新提升预测精度。
Compton Form Factor Extraction using Quantum Deep Neural Networks
Authors: Brandon B. Le, Dustin Keller
Venue: Phys. Rev. C 113, 045214 (2026)
First: 2025-04-21T21:56:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T14:57:11+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 14 figures. v4: matches published version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We extract Compton form factors (CFFs) from deeply virtual Compton scattering measurements at the Thomas Jefferson National Accelerator Facility (JLab) using quantum-inspired deep neural networks (QDNNs). The analysis implements the twist-2 Belitsky-Kirchner-Müller formalism and employs a fitting strategy that emulates standard local fits. Using pseudodata, we benchmark QDNNs against classical deep neural networks (CDNNs) and find that QDNNs often deliver higher predictive accuracy and tighter uncertainties at comparable model complexity. Guided by these results, we introduce a quantitative selection metric that indicates when QDNNs or CDNNs are optimal for a given experimental fit. After obtaining local extractions from the JLab data, we perform a standard neural-network global CFF fit and compare with previous global analyses. The results support QDNNs as an efficient and complementary tool to CDNNs for CFF determination and for future multidimensional studies of parton distributions and hadronic structure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用量子启发式深度神经网络提取康普顿形因子
我们利用量子启发式深度神经网络（QDNNs），从托马斯·杰斐逊国家加速器设施（JLab）的深虚拟康普顿散射测量数据中提取康普顿形因子（CFFs）。该分析采用扭-2 Belitsky-Kirchner-Müller形式化方法，并采用模拟标准局部拟合的策略。通过伪数据，我们对QDNNs与经典深度神经网络（CDNNs）进行基准测试，发现QDNNs在模型复杂度相当的情况下，通常具有更高的预测精度和更紧的不确定性。根据这些结果，我们引入了一个定量选择指标，用于判断在给定实验拟合中QDNNs或CDNNs的最优性。在从JLab数据获得局部提取结果后，我们进行标准神经网络全局CFF拟合，并与先前的全局分析进行比较。结果支持QDNNs作为确定CFFs及未来多维部分子分布和强子结构研究的有效且互补工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for improved precision in extracting Compton form factors from deeply virtual Compton scattering data at JLab, this study employs quantum-inspired deep neural networks (QDNNs) within the twist-2 Belitsky-Kirchner-Müller formalism to perform local fits. Compared to classical deep neural networks (CDNNs), QDNNs demonstrate superior predictive accuracy and tighter uncertainties on pseudodata with comparable model complexity, prompting the development of a selection metric to guide optimal model choice. Applied to real JLab data, QDNNs yield local extractions that, when globally fitted via neural networks, align with previous global analyses, confirming QDNNs as a viable and complementary tool for future studies of parton distributions and hadronic structure.
为提升对杰斐逊国家实验室深虚拟康普顿散射数据中康普顿形因子的提取精度，本研究在扭-2贝尔奇基-基尔希纳-穆勒形式框架下，采用量子启发式深度神经网络（QDNN）进行局部拟合。通过伪数据对比，QDNN在相同模型复杂度下展现出更高的预测精度和更紧的不确定性。研究引入定量选择指标以指导最优模型选择。将QDNN应用于真实JLab数据后，进行全局拟合，结果与以往全局分析一致，表明QDNN是未来研究部分子分布和强子结构多维问题的有效且互补工具。
GLM-5V-Turbo: Toward a Native Foundation Model for Multimodal Agents
Authors: GLM-V Team, :, Wenyi Hong, Xiaotao Gu, Ziyang Pan, Zhen Yang, Yuting Wang, Yue Wang, Yuanchang Yue, Yu Wang, Yanling Wang, Yan Wang, Xijun Liu, Wenmeng Yu, Weihan Wang, Wei Li, Shuaiqi Duan, Sheng Yang, Ruiliang Lv, Mingdao Liu, Lihang Pan, Ke Ning, Junhui Ji, Jinjiang Wang, Jing Chen, Jiazheng Xu, Jiale Zhu, Jiale Cheng, Ji Qi, Guobing Gan, Guo Wang, Cong Yao, Zijun Dou, Zihao Zhou, Zihan Wang, Zhiqi Ge, Zhijie Li, Zhenyu Hou, Zhao Xue, Zehui Wang, Zehai He, Yusen Liu, Yukuo Cen, Yuchen Li, Yuan Wang, Yijian Lu, Yanzi Wang, Yadong Xue, Xinyu Zhang, Xinyu Liu, Wenkai Li, Tianyu Tong, Tianshu Zhang, Shengdong Yan, Qinkai Zheng, Mingde Xu, Licheng Bao, Jiaxing Xu, Jiaxin Fan, Jiawen Qian, Jiali Chen, Jiahui Lin, Haozhi Zheng, Haoran Wang, Haochen Li, Fan Yang, Dan Zhang, Chuangxin Zhao, Chengcheng Wu, Boyan Shi, Bowei Jia, Baoxu Wang, Peng Zhang, Debing Liu, Bin Xu, Juanzi Li, Minlie Huang, Yuxiao Dong, Jie Tang
First: 2026-04-29T14:49:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T14:49:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present GLM-5V-Turbo, a step toward native foundation models for multimodal agents. As foundation models are increasingly deployed in real environments, agentic capability depends not only on language reasoning, but also on the ability to perceive, interpret, and act over heterogeneous contexts such as images, videos, webpages, documents, GUIs. GLM-5V-Turbo is built around this objective: multimodal perception is integrated as a core component of reasoning, planning, tool use, and execution, rather than as an auxiliary interface to a language model. This report summarizes the main improvements behind GLM-5V-Turbo across model design, multimodal training, reinforcement learning, toolchain expansion, and integration with agent frameworks. These developments lead to strong performance in multimodal coding, visual tool use, and framework-based agentic tasks, while preserving competitive text-only coding capability. More importantly, our development process offers practical insights for building multimodal agents, highlighting the central role of multimodal perception, hierarchical optimization, and reliable end-to-end verification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GLM-5V-Turbo：迈向原生多模态代理的基础模型
我们提出了GLM-5V-Turbo，这是迈向原生多模态代理基础模型的重要一步。随着基础模型在真实环境中的部署日益增多，代理能力不仅依赖于语言推理，还依赖于对图像、视频、网页、文档、GUI等异构上下文的感知、理解和行动能力。GLM-5V-Turbo围绕这一目标构建：多模态感知被整合为推理、规划、工具使用和执行的核心组成部分，而非作为语言模型的辅助接口。本报告总结了GLM-5V-Turbo在模型设计、多模态训练、强化学习、工具链扩展及与代理框架集成方面的主要改进。这些进展在多模态编程、视觉工具使用和基于框架的代理任务中表现出色，同时保持了与纯文本编码相当的竞争力。更重要的是，我们的开发过程为构建多模态代理提供了实用见解，强调了多模态感知、分层优化和可靠端到端验证的核心作用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for agents that can reason and act across diverse modalities like images, videos, and GUIs, GLM-5V-Turbo integrates multimodal perception as a core reasoning component rather than an auxiliary interface. The model is enhanced through refined architecture, multimodal training, reinforcement learning, expanded toolchains, and agent framework integration. Experimental results show strong performance in multimodal coding, visual tool use, and agentic tasks while maintaining competitive text-only coding ability, underscoring the importance of hierarchical optimization and end-to-end verification in multimodal agent design.
为使智能体能处理图像、视频、网页等异构输入，GLM-5V-Turbo将多模态感知作为核心推理组件而非辅助接口。模型通过多模态训练与分层优化强化，并扩展工具链与框架集成。实验表明其在多模态编程、视觉工具使用及框架化任务中表现优异，同时保持文本编码能力竞争力，验证了感知嵌入推理的核心价值。
DiRe-RAPIDS: Topology-faithful dimensionality reduction at scale
Authors: Alexander Kolpakov, Igor Rivin
First: 2026-04-28T04:28:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T14:47:00+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 4 figures, fixed broken URLs in comments; GitHub repositories https://github.com/sashakolpakov/dire-rapids | https://github.com/igorrivin/dire-rapids-arxiv | HuggingFace dataset https://huggingface.co/datasets/igriv/dire-arxiv-bge-small-embeddings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Dimensionality reduction methods such as UMAP and t-SNE are central tools for visualising high-dimensional data, but their local-neighborhood objectives can preserve sampling noise while distorting global topology. We show that standard local metrics reward this noise memorisation: top-performing embeddings invent cycles and disconnected islands absent from the data. We introduce a topology-faithfulness benchmark based on noisy manifolds with known homology, tune DiRe against it, and find Pareto-optimal configurations that match or beat GPU-accelerated UMAP on classification while recovering exact first Betti numbers on stress tests. On 723K arXiv paper embeddings, DiRe preserves 3-4 times more topological structure than UMAP at comparable wall-clock.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DiRe-RAPIDS：大规模拓扑忠实的降维方法
UMAP 和 t-SNE 等降维方法是可视化高维数据的核心工具，但其局部邻域目标可能保留采样噪声并扭曲全局拓扑结构。我们证明标准局部度量会奖励这种噪声记忆：表现最佳的嵌入会生成数据中不存在的环路和不连通岛屿。我们引入基于已知同调的噪声流形的拓扑忠实基准，针对该基准调优 DiRe，并发现帕累托最优配置，在分类任务上与 GPU 加速的 UMAP 相当或更优，同时在压力测试中恢复精确的第一贝蒂数。在 72.3 万篇 arXiv 论文嵌入数据上，DiRe 在相近运行时间下比 UMAP 保留 3 至 4 倍的拓扑结构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the tendency of local dimensionality reduction methods like UMAP and t-SNE to preserve sampling noise while distorting global topology, this work introduces DiRe-RAPIDS, a method tuned to maintain topological fidelity using a benchmark based on noisy manifolds with known homology. DiRe achieves Pareto-optimal configurations that match or exceed GPU-accelerated UMAP in classification tasks while recovering exact first Betti numbers in stress tests. On 723K arXiv paper embeddings, DiRe preserves 3–4 times more topological structure than UMAP at comparable computational cost.
为了解决UMAP和t-SNE等局部降维方法在保留采样噪声的同时扭曲全局拓扑的问题，本文提出了DiRe-RAPIDS方法，该方法基于已知同调的噪声流形构建拓扑忠实性基准并进行调优。DiRe在保持或超越GPU加速UMAP分类性能的同时，能在压力测试中精确恢复一阶贝蒂数，并在72.3万个arXiv论文嵌入数据上，以相近的运行时间比UMAP多保留3至4倍的拓扑结构。
From Hypotheses to Factors: Constrained LLM Agents in Cryptocurrency Markets
Authors: Yikuan Huang, Zheqi Fan, Kaiqi Hu, Yifan Ye
First: 2026-04-29T14:46:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T14:46:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM agents are promising tools for empirical discovery, but their flexibility can also turn discovery into uncontrolled search. We study how to use agents under a reproducible protocol through cryptocurrency factor discovery. Our framework casts the task as sequential hypothesis search: an agent reads an append-only experiment trace, proposes falsifiable factor hypotheses, and maps them to executable recipes, while a deterministic engine enforces fixed data splits, selection gates, transaction costs, and portfolio tests. Candidate actions are restricted to a point-in-time factor DSL, making both successful and failed hypotheses auditable. A ridge-combined portfolio trained only on 2020--2022 data achieves a 44.55% annualized return and Sharpe ratio of 1.55 in the 2024--2026 pure out-of-sample period after a 5 basis point one-way trading cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从假设到因子：加密货币市场中的约束型大语言模型代理
大语言模型代理是实证发现的有力工具，但其灵活性也可能使发现演变为无约束的搜索。我们通过加密货币因子发现研究如何在可复现协议下使用代理。我们的框架将任务视为顺序假设搜索：代理读取仅追加实验日志，提出可证伪的因子假设，并将其映射为可执行的配方，而一个确定性引擎强制执行固定数据分割、选择门限、交易成本和组合测试。候选操作受限于时点因子DSL，使成功与失败的假设均可审计。仅在2020—2022年数据上训练的岭组合组合，在2024—2026年纯外样本期（扣除5个基点单向交易成本）实现年化回报44.55%，夏普比率1.55。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to constrain LLM agent exploration in financial markets to ensure reproducibility and auditability, the authors propose a framework that treats cryptocurrency factor discovery as sequential hypothesis testing. Agents generate falsifiable hypotheses from an append-only experiment log and map them to executable recipes, while a deterministic engine enforces fixed data splits, selection gates, and transaction costs. The method restricts actions to a point-in-time factor DSL, enabling transparent evaluation. Experiments show that a ridge-combined portfolio trained on 2020–2022 data yields a 44.55% annualized return and Sharpe ratio of 1.55 in the 2024–2026 out-of-sample period after accounting for a 5 basis point trading cost.
为确保LLM代理在实证发现中的可重复性和可审计性，该研究将加密货币因子发现建模为顺序假设搜索问题。方法上，代理读取追加式实验日志，提出可证伪的因子假设并映射为可执行方案，同时确定性引擎强制执行固定数据分割、选择门限、交易成本和组合测试。实验表明，仅在2020–2022年数据上训练的岭组合投资组合，在扣除5个基点单向交易成本后，于2024–2026年纯外样本期实现了44.55%的年化回报率和1.55的夏普比率。
SkillForge: Forging Domain-Specific, Self-Evolving Agent Skills in Cloud Technical Support
Authors: Xingyan Liu, Xiyue Luo, Linyu Li, Ganghong Huang, Jianfeng Liu, Honglin Qiao
Venue: SIGIR 2026
First: 2026-04-09T07:11:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-29T14:40:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ACM SIGIR 2026 Industry Track. 18 pages, 5 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deploying LLM-powered agents in enterprise scenarios such as cloud technical support demands high-quality, domain-specific skills. However, existing skill creators lack domain grounding, producing skills poorly aligned with real-world task requirements. Moreover, once deployed, there is no systematic mechanism to trace execution failures back to skill deficiencies and drive targeted refinements, leaving skill quality stagnant despite accumulating operational evidence. We introduce SkillForge, a self-evolving framework that closes an end-to-end creation-evaluation-refinement loop. To produce well-aligned initial skills, a Domain-Contextualized Skill Creator grounds skill synthesis in knowledge bases and historical support tickets. To enable continuous self-optimization, a three-stage pipeline -- Failure Analyzer, Skill Diagnostician, and Skill Optimizer -- automatically diagnoses execution failures in batch, pinpoints the underlying skill deficiencies, and rewrites the skill to eliminate them. This cycle runs iteratively, allowing skills to self-improve with every round of deployment feedback. Evaluated on five real-world cloud support scenarios spanning 1,883 tickets and 3,737 tasks, experiments show that: (1) the Domain-Contextualized Skill Creator produces substantially better initial skills than the generic skill creator, as measured by consistency with expert-authored reference responses from historical tickets; and (2) the self-evolution loop progressively improves skill quality from diverse starting points (including expert-authored, domain-created, and generic skills) across successive rounds, demonstrating that automated evolution can surpass manually curated expert knowledge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SkillForge：在云技术支持中锻造领域特定且自我演进的智能体技能
在企业场景（如云技术支持）中部署基于大语言模型的智能体，需要高质量、领域特定的技能。然而，现有技能生成器缺乏领域背景，生成的技能与实际任务需求不匹配。此外，部署后缺乏系统机制将执行失败追溯至技能缺陷并驱动针对性优化，导致技能质量停滞不前，尽管积累了大量运营证据。我们提出SkillForge，一个自我演进框架，实现端到端的创建-评估-优化循环。为生成高度对齐的初始技能，领域上下文技能生成器基于知识库和历史支持工单进行技能合成。为实现持续自我优化，三阶段流水线——失败分析器、技能诊断器和技能优化器——自动批量诊断执行失败，定位底层技能缺陷，并重写技能以消除缺陷。该循环迭代运行，使技能在每次部署反馈中自我提升。在五个真实云支持场景（涵盖1,883个工单和3,737个任务）中评估，实验表明：（1）领域上下文技能生成器生成的初始技能显著优于通用技能生成器，以历史工单中专家撰写的参考响应一致性为衡量标准；（2）自我演进循环从多样化的初始点（包括专家撰写、领域生成和通用技能）逐步提升技能质量，证明自动化演进可超越人工精选的专家知识。
Summary / 总结
SkillForge addresses the gap in deploying LLM agents for cloud technical support by enabling domain-specific, self-evolving skills. It begins with a Domain-Contextualized Skill Creator that synthesizes skills grounded in knowledge bases and historical tickets, producing more aligned initial outputs than generic methods. The framework then employs a three-stage pipeline—Failure Analyzer, Skill Diagnostician, and Skill Optimizer—to automatically diagnose execution failures, identify skill deficiencies, and rewrite skills iteratively. Evaluated across five real-world cloud support scenarios involving 1,883 tickets and 3,737 tasks, SkillForge demonstrated that its initial skills were significantly more consistent with expert responses, and that its self-evolution loop progressively improved skill quality across multiple rounds, even surpassing manually curated expert skills in some cases.
为解决通用大模型技能与企业云支持场景实际需求之间的脱节问题，SkillForge提出了一种自进化框架，通过实际运营反馈持续优化智能体技能。该框架首先利用知识库和历史工单数据，通过领域上下文技能生成器构建更精准、更一致的初始技能，显著优于通用生成方法。随后，系统通过三阶段流水线——故障分析器、技能诊断器和技能优化器——自动定位执行失败的根本技能缺陷并重写技能。在涵盖1883张工单、3737个任务的五个云支持场景中，实验表明：初始技能质量明显优于基线方法，且经过多轮迭代后，技能质量持续提升，甚至在某些情况下超越人工专家撰写的技能。
